




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai

itivuttaka-aṭṭhakathā

ganthārambhakathā

mahākāruṇikaṃ  nāthaṃ, ñaiyyasāgarapāraguṃ|

vandai nipuṇagambhīra-vicitranayadaisanaṃ||

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nā, yaina niyyanti laukatau|

vandai tamuttamaṃ dhammaṃ, sammāsambuddhapūjitaṃ||

sīlādiguṇasampan‍nau, ṭhitau maggaphalaisu yau|

vandai ariyasaṅghaṃ taṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ anuttaraṃ||

vandanājanitaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, iti yaṃ ratanattayai|

hatantarāyau sabbattha, hutvāhaṃ tassa taijasā||

aikakādippabhaidaina, daisitāni mahaisinā|

laubhādīnaṃ pahānāni, dīpanāni visaisatau||

suttāni aikatau katvā, itivuttapadakkharaṃ|

dhammasaṅgāhakā thairā, saṅgāyiṃsu mahaisayau||

itivuttakamic‍caiva, nāmaina vasinau purai|

yaṃ khuddakanikāyasmiṃ, gambhīratthapadak‍kamaṃ||

tassa gambhīrañāṇaihi, augāhaitabbabhāvatau|

kiñ‍cāpi duk‍karā kātuṃ, atthasaṃvaṇṇanā mayā||

sahasaṃvaṇṇanaṃ  yasmā, dharatai satthu sāsanaṃ|

pubbācariyasīhānaṃ, tiṭṭhataiva vinicchayau||

tasmā taṃ avalambitvā, augāhaitvāna pañ‍capi|

nikāyai upanissāya, paurāṇaṭṭhakathānayaṃ||

nissitaṃ vācanāmaggaṃ, suvisuddhaṃ anākulaṃ|

mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ, nipuṇatthavinicchayaṃ||

punappunāgataṃ atthaṃ, vaj‍jayitvāna sādhukaṃ|

yathābalaṃ karissāmi, itivuttakavaṇṇanaṃ||

iti ākaṅkhamānassa, saddhammassa ciraṭṭhitiṃ|

vibhajantassa tassatthaṃ, nisāmayatha sādhavauti||

tattha itivuttakaṃ nāma aikakanipātau, dukanipātau, tikanipātau, catuk‍kanipātauti catunipātasaṅgahaṃ| tampi vinayapiṭakaṃ, suttantapiṭakaṃ, abhidhammapiṭakanti tīsu piṭakaisu suttantapiṭakapariyāpan‍naṃ; dīghanikāyau majjhimanikāyau, saṃyuttanikāyau, aṅguttaranikāyau, khuddakanikāyauti  pañ‍casu nikāyaisu khuddakanikāyapariyāpan‍naṃ; suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthā, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍lanti navasu sāsanaṅgaisu itivuttakaṅgabhūtaṃ|

‘‘dvāsīti buddhatau gaṇhiṃ, dvaisahassāni bhikkhutau|

caturāsīti sahassāni, yai mai dhammā pavattinau’’ti|| (thairagā॰ 1027) –

aivaṃ dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaina paṭiñ‍ñātaisu caturāsītiyā dhammakkhandhasahassaisu katipayadhammakkhandhasaṅgahaṃ| suttatau aikakanipātai tāva sattavīsati suttāni, dukanipātai dvāvīsati, tikanipātai pañ‍ñāsa, catuk‍kanipātai tairasāti dvādasādhikasuttasatasaṅgahaṃ| tassa nipātaisu aikakanipātau ādi, vaggaisu pāṭibhaugavaggau, suttaisu laubhasuttaṃ| tassāpi ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā’’tiādikaṃ āyasmatā ānandaina paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai vuttaṃ nidānamādi| sā  panāyaṃ paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti vinayapiṭakai tantimāruḷhā aiva| yau panaittha nidānakausal‍latthaṃ vattabbau kathāmaggau, saupi sumaṅgalavilāsiniyā dīghanikāya-aṭṭhakathāya vitthāratau vuttauyaivāti tattha vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbau|

nidānavaṇṇanā

yaṃ  panaitaṃ vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatātiādikaṃ nidānaṃ| aikadhammaṃ, bhikkhavai, pajahathātiādikaṃ suttaṃ| tattha vuttaṃ bhagavatātiādīni nāmapadāni| itīti nipātapadaṃ| pajahathāti aittha pa-iti upasaggapadaṃ, jahathā-ti ākhyātapadaṃ| iminā nayaina sabbattha padavibhāgau vaiditabbau|

atthatau pana vuttasaddau tāva saupasaggau anupasaggau ca vapanai vāpasamakaraṇai kaisauhāraṇai jīvitavuttiyaṃ pavuttabhāvai pāvacanabhāvaina pavattitai ajjhaisanai kathanaiti aivamādīsu dissati| tathā haisa –

‘‘gāvau tassa pajāyanti, khaittai vuttaṃ virūhati|

vuttānaṃ phalamasnāti, yau mittānaṃ na dubbhatī’’ti|| –

ādīsu (jā॰ 2.22.19) vapanai āgatau| ‘‘nau ca khau paṭivutta’’ntiādīsu (pārā॰ 289) aṭṭhadantakādīhi vāpasamakaraṇai| ‘‘kāpaṭikau māṇavau daharau vuttasirau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.426) kaisauhāraṇai| ‘‘pan‍nalaumau paradattavuttau migabhūtaina caitasā viharatī’’tiādīsu (cūḷava॰ 332) jīvitavuttiyaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi nāma paṇḍupalāsau bandhanā pavuttau abhabbau haritatthāyā’’tiādīsu (pārā॰ 92; pāci॰ 666; mahāva॰ 129) bandhanatau pavuttabhāvai| ‘‘yaisamidaṃ aitarahi, brāhmaṇā, paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samihita’’ntiādīsu pāvacanabhāvaina pavattitai| laukai pana – ‘‘vuttau gaṇau vuttau pārāyanau’’tiādīsu ajjhainai| ‘‘vuttaṃ khau panaitaṃ bhagavatā dhammadāyādā mai, bhikkhavai, bhavatha, mā āmisadāyādā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.30) kathanai| idhāpi kathanai daṭṭhabbau| tasmā vuttaṃ kathitaṃ bhāsitanti atthau|

dutiyau  pana vuttasaddau vacanai ciṇṇabhāvai ca vaiditabbau| hi-iti jātu vibyattanti aitasmiṃ  atthai nipātau| sau idāni vuc‍camānasuttassa  bhagavatau vibyattaṃ bhāsitabhāvaṃ jautaiti| vācakasaddasan‍nidhānai hi payuttā nipātā| taihi vattabbamatthaṃ jautainti| aitanti ayaṃ aitasaddau –

‘‘yau ca buddhañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, saṅghañ‍ca saraṇaṃ gatau|

cattāri ariyasac‍cāni, sammappañ‍ñāya passati||

‘‘dukkhaṃ dukkhasamuppādaṃ, dukkhassa ca atik‍kamaṃ|

ariyañ‍caṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

‘‘aitaṃ khau saraṇaṃ khaimaṃ, aitaṃ saraṇamuttamaṃ|

aitaṃ saraṇamāgamma, sabbadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 190-192) –

ādīsu yathāvuttai āsan‍napac‍cakkhai āgatau| ‘‘appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, auramattakaṃ sīlamattakaṃ, yaina puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyyā’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.7) pana vakkhamānai āsan‍napac‍cakkhai| idhāpi vakkhamānaiyaiva daṭṭhabbau| saṅgāyanavasaina vakkhamānañhi suttaṃ dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaina buddhiyaṃ ṭhapaitvā tadā ‘‘aita’’nti vuttaṃ|

bhagavatāti aittha bhagavāti garuvacanaṃ| garuṃ hi laukai bhagavāti vadanti| tathāgatau ca sabbaguṇavisiṭṭhatāya sattānaṃ garu, tasmā bhagavāti vaiditabbau| paurāṇaihipi vuttaṃ –

‘‘bhagavāti vacanaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, bhagavāti vacanamuttamaṃ|

garu gāravayuttau sau, bhagavā taina vuc‍catī’’ti||

saiṭṭhavācakañhi vacanaṃ saiṭṭhaguṇasahacaraṇatau saiṭṭhanti vuttaṃ| atha vā vuc‍catīti vacanaṃ, atthau| tasmā bhagavāti vacanaṃ saiṭṭhanti bhagavāti iminā vacanaina vacanīyau  yau atthau, sau saiṭṭhauti atthau| bhagavāti vacanamuttamanti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| gāravayuttauti garubhāvayuttau garuguṇayaugatau, garukaraṇaṃ vā sātisayaṃ arahatīti gāravayuttau, gāravārahauti atthau| aivaṃ guṇavisiṭṭhasattuttamagarugāravādhivacanamaitaṃ yadidaṃ bhagavāti| apica –

‘‘bhagī  bhajī bhāgī vibhattavā iti,

akāsi bhagganti garūti bhāgyavā|

bahūhi ñāyaihi subhāvitattanau,

bhavantagau sau bhagavāti vuc‍catī’’ti|| –

niddaisai āgatanayaina –

‘‘bhāgyavā bhaggavā yuttau, bhagaihi ca vibhattavā|

bhattavā vantagamanau, bhavaisu bhagavā tatau’’ti||

imissā gāthāya ca vasaina bhagavāti padassa atthau vattabbau| sau panāyaṃ atthau sabbākāraina visuddhimaggai buddhānussatiniddaisai vuttauti| tattha vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbau|

aparau nayau – bhāgavāti bhagavā, bhatavāti bhagavā, bhāgai vanīti bhagavā, bhagai vanīti bhagavā, bhattavāti bhagavā, bhagai vamīti bhagavā, bhāgai vamīti bhagavā|

‘‘bhāgavā bhatavā bhāgai, bhagai ca vani bhattavā|

bhagai vami tathā bhāgai, vamīti bhagavā jinau’’||

tattha kathaṃ bhāgavāti bhagavā? yai tai sīlādayau dhammakkhandhā guṇakauṭṭhāsā, tai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā niratisayā tathāgatassa atthi upalabbhanti| tathā hissa sīlaṃ, samādhi, pañ‍ñā, vimutti, vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ, hirī, auttappaṃ, saddhā, vīriyaṃ, sati, sampajañ‍ñaṃ, sīlavisuddhi, cittavisuddhi, diṭṭhivisuddhi, samathau, vipassanā, tīṇi kusalamūlāni, tīṇi sucaritāni, tayau sammāvitak‍kā, tissau anavaj‍jasañ‍ñā, tissau dhātuyau, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, cattārau ariyamaggā, cattāri ariyaphalāni, catassau paṭisambhidā, catuyauniparicchaidakañāṇāni, cattārau ariyavaṃsā, cattāri vaisāraj‍jañāṇāni, pañ‍ca padhāniyaṅgāni, pañ‍caṅgikau sammāsamādhi, pañ‍cañāṇikau sammāsamādhi, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, pañ‍ca nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau, pañ‍ca vimuttāyatanañāṇāni, pañ‍ca vimuttiparipācanīyā sañ‍ñā, cha anussatiṭṭhānāni, cha gāravā, cha nissāraṇīyā dhātuyau, cha satatavihārā, cha anuttariyāni, cha nibbaidhabhāgiyā  sañ‍ñā, cha  abhiñ‍ñā, cha asādhāraṇañāṇāni, satta aparihāniyā dhammā, satta ariyadhanāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, satta sappurisadhammā, satta nij‍jaravatthūni, satta sañ‍ñā, satta dakkhiṇaiyyapuggaladaisanā, satta khīṇāsavabaladaisanā, aṭṭha pañ‍ñāpaṭilābhahaitudaisanā, aṭṭhasammattāni, aṭṭha laukadhammātik‍kamau, aṭṭha ārambhavatthūni, aṭṭha akkhaṇadaisanā, aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kā, aṭṭha abhibhāyatanadaisanā, aṭṭha vimaukkhā, nava yaunisaumanasikāramūlakā dhammā, nava pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgāni, nava sattāvāsadaisanā, nava āghātappaṭivinayā, nava sañ‍ñā, nava nānattā, nava anupubbavihārā, dasa nāthakaraṇā dhammā, dasa kasiṇāyatanāni, dasa kusalakammapathā, dasa sammattāni, dasa ariyavāsā, dasa asaikkhā dhammā, dasa tathāgatabalāni, aikādasa maittānisaṃsā, dvādasa dhammacak‍kākārā, tairasa dhutaguṇā, cuddasa buddhañāṇāni, pañ‍cadasa vimuttiparipācanīyā dhammā, sauḷasavidhā ānāpānassati, sauḷasa aparantapanīyā dhammā, aṭṭhārasa buddhadhammā, aikūnavīsati pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇāni, catucattālīsa ñāṇavatthūni, pañ‍ñāsa udayabbayañāṇāni, paraupaṇṇāsa kusaladhammā, sattasattati ñāṇavatthūni, catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassasamāpattisañ‍cārimahāvajirañāṇaṃ, anantanayasamantapaṭṭhānapavicayapac‍cavaikkhaṇadaisanāñāṇāni, tathā anantāsu laukadhātūsu anantānaṃ sattānaṃ āsayādivibhāvanañāṇāni cāti, aivamādayau anantā aparimāṇabhaidā anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā niratisayā guṇabhāgā guṇakauṭṭhāsā vij‍janti upalabbhanti| tasmā yathāvuttavibhāgā guṇabhāgā assa atthīti  bhāgavāti vattabbai| ākārassa rassattaṃ katvā ‘‘bhagavā’’ti vuttau| aivaṃ tāva bhāgavāti bhagavā|

‘‘yasmā sīlādayau sabbai, guṇabhāgā asaisatau|

vij‍janti sugatai tasmā, bhagavāti pavuc‍cati’’||

kathaṃ bhatavāti bhagavā? yai tai sabbalaukahitāya ussuk‍kamāpan‍naihi manussattādikai aṭṭha dhammai samaudhānaitvā sammāsambaudhiyā katamahābhinīhāraihi mahābaudhisattaihi paripūraitabbā dānapāramī, sīlanaikkhammapañ‍ñāvīriyakhantisac‍caadhiṭṭhānamaittāupaikkhāpāramīti dasa pāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyauti samatiṃsa  pāramiyau, dānādīni cattāri saṅgahavatthūni, cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, attaparic‍cāgau, nayanadhanaraj‍japuttadāraparic‍cāgauti pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgā, pubbayaugau, pubbacariyā, dhammakkhānaṃ, laukatthacariyā, ñātatthacariyā, buddhatthacariyāti aivamādayau saṅkhaipatau vā puñ‍ñasambhārañāṇasambhārā buddhakaradhammā, tai mahābhinīhāratau paṭṭhāya kappānaṃ satasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni yathā hānabhāgiyā, saṃkilaisabhāgiyā, ṭhitibhāgiyā, vā na haunti; atha khau uttaruttari visaisabhāgiyāva haunti; aivaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ nirantaraṃ anavasaisatau bhatā sambhatā assa atthīti bhatavāti bhagavā; niruttinayaina takārassa gakāraṃ katvā| atha vā bhatavāti taiyaiva yathāvuttai buddhakaradhammai vuttanayaina bhari sambhari paripūraisīti atthau| aivampi bhatavāti bhagavā|

‘‘yasmā sambaudhiyā sabbai, dānapāramiādikai|

sambhārai bhatavā nāthau, tasmāpi bhagavā matau’’||

kathaṃ bhāgai vanīti bhagavā? yai tai catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassasaṅkhā daivasikaṃ vaḷañ‍janakasamāpattibhāgā, tai anavasaisatau laukahitatthaṃ attanau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthañ‍ca nic‍cakappaṃ vani bhaji saivi bahulamakāsīti bhāgai vanīti bhagavā| atha vā abhiñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu kusalādīsu khandhādīsu ca yai tai pariñ‍ñaiyyādivasaina saṅkhaipatau vā catubbidhā abhisamayabhāgā, vitthāratau pana ‘‘cakkhu pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ …pai॰… jarāmaraṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyya’’ntiādinā (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.21) anaikai pariñ‍ñaiyyabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhussa samudayau pahātabbau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa samudayau pahātabbau’’tiādinā pahātabbabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhussa niraudhau sacchikātabbau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa niraudhau sacchikātabbau’’tiādinā sacchikātabbabhāgā, ‘‘cakkhuniraudhagāminīpaṭipadā bhāvaitabbā…pai॰… cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’tiādinā ca anaikabhaidā bhāvaitabbabhāgā ca dhammā, tai sabbai vani bhaji yathārahaṃ gaucarabhāvanāsaivanānaṃ vasaina saivi| aivampi bhāgai vanīti bhagavā| atha vā yai imai sīlādayau dhammakkhandhā sāvakaihi sādhāraṇā guṇakauṭṭhāsā guṇabhāgā, kinti nu khau tai vainaiyyasantānaisu patiṭṭhapaiyyanti mahākaruṇāya vani abhipatthayi| sā  cassa abhipatthanā yathādhippaitaphalāvahā ahausi| aivampi bhāgai vanīti bhagavā|

‘‘yasmā ñaiyyasamāpatti-guṇabhāgai tathāgatau|

bhaji patthayi sattānaṃ, hitāya bhagavā tatau’’||

kathaṃ bhagai vanīti bhagavā? samāsatau tāva katapuñ‍ñaihi payaugasampan‍naihi yathāvibhavaṃ bhajīyantīti bhagā, laukiyalaukuttarā sampattiyau| tattha laukiyai tāva tathāgatau sambaudhitau pubbai baudhisattabhūtau paramuk‍kaṃsagatai vani bhaji saivi, yattha patiṭṭhāya niravasaisatau buddhakaradhammai saman‍nānaintau buddhadhammai paripācaisi| buddhabhūtau pana tai niravaj‍jasukhūpasaṃhitai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇai laukuttaraipi  vani bhaji saivi| vitthāratau pana padaisaraj‍jaissariyacak‍kavattisampattidaivaraj‍jasampattiādivasaina jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattiñāṇadassanamaggabhāvanāphala- sacchikiriyādiuttarimanussadhammavasaina ca anaikavihitai anañ‍ñasādhāraṇai bhagai vani bhaji saivi| aivaṃ bhagai vanīti bhagavā|

‘‘yā tā sampattiyau laukai, yā ca laukuttarā puthū|

sabbā tā bhaji sambuddhau, tasmāpi bhagavā matau’’||

kathaṃ  bhattavāti bhagavā? bhattā daḷhabhattikā assa bahū atthīti bhagavā| tathāgatau hi mahākaruṇāsabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādiaparimitanirupamappabhāvaguṇavisaisasamaṅgibhāvatau sabbasattuttamau, sabbānatthaparihārapubbaṅgamāya niravasaisahitasukhavidhānatapparāya niratisayāya payaugasampattiyā sadaivamanussāya pajāya ac‍cantūpakāritāya dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaasītianubyañ‍janabyāmappabhādi- anañ‍ñasādhāraṇaguṇavisaisapaṭimaṇḍitarūpakāyatāya, yathābhuc‍caguṇādhigataina ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinayappavattaina laukattayabyāpinā suvipulaina suvisuddhaina ca thutighausaina saman‍nāgatattā uk‍kaṃsapāramippattāsu appicchatāsantuṭṭhitādīsu suppatiṭṭhitabhāvatau dasabalacatuvaisāraj‍jādiniratisayaguṇavisaisasamaṅgibhāvatau ca rūpappamāṇau rūpappasan‍nau, ghausappamāṇau ghausappasan‍nau, lūkhappamāṇau lūkhappasan‍nau, dhammappamāṇau dhammappasan‍nauti  aivaṃ catuppamāṇikai laukasan‍nivāsai sabbathāpi pasādāvahabhāvaina samantapāsādikattā aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ sadaivamanussānaṃ ādarabahumānagāravāyatanatāya paramapaimasambhattiṭṭhānaṃ| yai ca tassa auvādai patiṭṭhitā avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā haunti, kainaci asaṃhāriyā taisaṃ sambhatti samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā| tathā hi tai attanau jīvitaparic‍cāgaipi tattha pasādaṃ na paric‍cajanti, tassa vā āṇaṃ daḷhabhattibhāvatau| tainaivāha –

‘‘yau vai katañ‍ñū katavaidi dhīrau|

kalyāṇamittau daḷhabhatti ca hautī’’ti|| (jā॰ 2.17.78)|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ mayā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, taṃ mama sāvakā jīvitahaitupi nātik‍kamantī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 8.20; udā॰ 45; cūḷava॰ 385) ca|

aivaṃ  bhattavāti bhagavā niruttinayaina aikassa takārassa laupaṃ katvā itarassa gakāraṃ katvā|

‘‘guṇātisayayuttassa, yasmā laukahitaisinau|

sambhattā bahavau satthu, bhagavā taina vuc‍catī’’ti||

kathaṃ  bhagai vamīti bhagavā? yasmā tathāgatau baudhisattabhūtaupi purimāsu jātīsu pāramiyau pūraintau bhagasaṅkhātaṃ siriṃ issariyaṃ yasañ‍ca vami uggiri khaiḷapiṇḍaṃ viya anapaikkhau chaḍḍayi| tathā hissa saumanassakumārakālai, hatthipālakumārakālai, ayaugharapaṇḍitakālai, mūgapakkhapaṇḍitakālai, cūḷasutasaumakālaiti aivamādīsu naikkhammapāramipūraṇavasaina daivaraj‍jasadisāya raj‍jasiriyā paric‍cattattabhāvānaṃ parimāṇaṃ natthi| carimattabhāvaipi hatthagataṃ cak‍kavattisiriṃ daivalaukādhipac‍casadisaṃ catuddīpissariyaṃ cak‍kavattisampattisan‍nissayaṃ sattaratanasamuj‍jalaṃ yasañ‍ca tiṇāyapi amañ‍ñamānau nirapaikkhau pahāya abhinikkhamitvā sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau| tasmā imai siriādikai bhagai vamīti bhagavā| atha vā bhāni nāma nakkhattāni, taihi samaṃ gacchanti pavattantīti bhagā, sinairuyugandharauttarakuruhimavantādibhājanalaukavisaisasan‍nissayā saubhā kappaṭṭhitiyabhāvatau| taipi bhagavā vami taṃnivāsisattāvāsasamatik‍kamanatautappaṭibaddhachandarāgappahānaina pajahīti| aivampi bhagai vamīti bhagavā|

‘‘cak‍kavattisiriṃ yasmā, yasaṃ issariyaṃ sukhaṃ|

pahāsi laukacittañ‍ca, sugatau bhagavā tatau’’||

kathaṃ bhāgai vamīti bhagavā? bhāgā nāma kauṭṭhāsā| tai khandhāyatanadhātādivasaina, tatthāpi rūpavaidanādivasaina, atītādivasaina ca anaikavidhā| tai ca bhagavā sabbaṃ papañ‍caṃ, sabbaṃ yaugaṃ, sabbaṃ ganthaṃ, sabbaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, samucchinditvā amatadhātuṃ samadhigacchantau vami uggiri anapaikkhau chaḍḍayi, na pac‍cāgami| tathā haisa sabbatthakamaiva pathaviṃ, āpaṃ, taijaṃ, vāyaṃ, cakkhuṃ, sautaṃ, ghānaṃ, jīvhaṃ, kāyaṃ, manaṃ, rūpai, saddai, gandhai, rasai, phauṭṭhabbai, dhammai, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, cakkhusamphassaṃ  …pai॰… manausamphassaṃ, cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ, cakkhusamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ; cakkhusamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassajaṃ caitanaṃ; rūpataṇhaṃ  …pai॰… dhammataṇhaṃ; rūpavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… dhammavitak‍kaṃ; rūpavicāraṃ…pai॰… dhammavicārantiādinā anupadadhammavibhāgavasainapi sabbaiva dhammakauṭṭhāsai anavasaisatau vami uggiri anapaikkhaparic‍cāgaina chaḍḍayi| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘yaṃ taṃ, ānanda, cattaṃ vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau puna pac‍cāgamissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| (dī॰ ni॰ 2.183) –

aivampi bhāgai vamīti bhagavā| atha vā bhāgai vamīti sabbaipi kusalākusalai sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jai hīnappaṇītai kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai dhammai ariyamaggañāṇamukhaina vami uggiri anapaikkhau paric‍caji pajahi, paraisañ‍ca tathattāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘dhammāpi vau, bhikkhavai, pahātabbā pagaiva adhammā, kul‍lūpamaṃ, vau bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi, nittharaṇatthāya nau gahaṇatthāyā’’tiādi| (ma॰ ni॰ 1.240) –

aivampi  bhāgai vamīti bhagavā|

‘‘khandhāyatanadhātādi-dhammabhaidā mahaisinā|

kaṇhasuk‍kā yatau vantā, tataupi bhagavā matau’’||

taina vuttaṃ –

‘‘bhāgavā bhatavā bhāgai, bhagai ca vani bhattavā|

bhagai vami tathā bhāgai, vamīti bhagavā jinau’’ti||

taina bhagavatā| arahatāti kilaisaihi ārakattā, anavasaisānaṃ vā kilaisārīnaṃ hatattā, saṃsāracak‍kassa vā arānaṃ hatattā, pac‍cayādīnaṃ arahattā, pāpakaraṇai rahābhāvāti imaihi kāraṇaihi arahatā| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau| vitthārau pana visuddhimaggai vuttanayaina vaiditabbau|

aittha  ca bhagavatāti imināssa bhāgyavantatādīpanaina kappānaṃ anaikaisu asaṅkhyaiyyaisu upacitapuñ‍ñasambhārabhāvatau satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇadharassa dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaasītianubyañ‍jana- byāmappabhākaitumālādipaṭimaṇḍitā  anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā rūpakāyasampattidīpitā hauti| arahatāti imināssa anavasaisakilaisappahānadīpanaina āsavakkhayapadaṭṭhānasabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādhigamaparidīpanatau dasabalacatuvaisāraj‍jachaasādhāraṇañāṇaaṭṭhārasāvaiṇikabuddhadhammādi- acintaiyyāparimaiyyadhammakāyasampatti dīpitā hauti| tadubhayainapi laukiyasarikkhakānaṃ bahumatabhāvau, gahaṭṭhapabbajitaihi abhigamanīyatā, tathā abhigatānañ‍ca taisaṃ kāyikacaitasikadukkhāpanayanai paṭibalabhāvau, āmisadānadhammadānaihi upakāritā, laukiyalaukuttaraihi guṇaihi saṃyaujanasamatthatā ca pakāsitā hauti|

tathā bhagavatāti iminā caraṇadhammaisu muddhabhūtadibbavihārādivihāravisaisasamāyaugaparidīpanaina caraṇasampadā dīpitā hauti| arahatāti iminā sabbavij‍jāsu sikhāppattaāsavakkhayañāṇādhigamaparidīpanaina vij‍jāsampadā dīpitā hauti| purimaina vā antarāyikaniyyānikadhammānaṃ aviparītavibhattabhāvadīpanaina pacchimavaisāraj‍jadvayasamāyaugau, pacchimaina savāsananiravasaisakilaisappahānadīpanaina  purimavaisāraj‍jadvayasamāyaugau vibhāvitau hauti|

tathā purimaina tathāgatassa paṭiñ‍ñāsac‍cavacīsac‍cañāṇasac‍caparidīpanaina, kāmaguṇalaukiyādhipac‍cayasalābhasak‍kārādiparic‍cāgaparidīpanaina, anavasaisakilaisābhisaṅkhāraparic‍cāgaparidīpanaina, ca sac‍cādhiṭṭhānacāgādhiṭṭhānapāripūri pakāsitā hauti; dutiyaina sabbasaṅkhārūpasamasamadhigamaparidīpanaina, sammāsambaudhiparidīpanaina ca, upasamādhiṭṭhānapañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānapāripūri pakāsitā hauti| tathā hi bhagavatau baudhisattabhūtassa laukuttaraguṇai katābhinīhārassa mahākaruṇāyaugaina yathāpaṭiñ‍ñaṃ sabbapāramitānuṭṭhānaina sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ, pāramitāpaṭipakkhaparic‍cāgaina cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ, pāramitāguṇaihi cittavūpasamaina upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ, pāramitāhi aiva parahitūpāyakausal‍latau pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ pāripūrigataṃ|

tathā ‘yācakajanaṃ avisaṃvādaitvā  dassāmī’ti paṭijānanaina paṭiñ‍ñaṃ avisaṃvādaitvā dānaina ca sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ, daiyyaparic‍cāgatau cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ, daiyyapaṭiggāhakadānadaiyyaparikkhayaisu laubhadausamauhabhayavūpasamaina upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ, yathārahaṃ yathākālaṃ yathāvidhi ca dānaina pañ‍ñuttaratāya ca pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ pāripūrigataṃ| iminā nayaina saisapāramīsupi caturādhiṭṭhānapāripūri vaiditabbā| sabbā hi pāramiyau sac‍cappabhāvitā cāgābhibyañ‍jitā upasamānubrūhitā pañ‍ñāparisuddhāti aivaṃ caturādhiṭṭhānasamudāgatassa tathāgatassa sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina sīlavisuddhi, cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina  ājīvavisuddhi, upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina cittavisuddhi, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina diṭṭhivisuddhi| tathā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamainassa saṃvāsaina sīlaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina saṃvauhāraina saucaiyyaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina āpadāsu thāmau vaiditabbau, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina sākacchāya pañ‍ñā vaiditabbā|

tathā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina aduṭṭhau adhivāsaiti, cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina aluddhau paṭisaivati, upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina abhītau parivaj‍jaiti, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina amūḷhau vinaudaiti| tathā  sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina cassa naikkhammasukhappatti, cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina pavivaikasukhappatti, upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina upasamasukhappatti, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina sambaudhisukhappatti dīpitā hauti| sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina vā vivaikajapītisukhappatti, cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina samādhijapītisukhappatti, upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina apītijakāyasukhappatti, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina satipārisuddhijaupaikkhāsukhappatti| tathā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina parivārasampattilakkhaṇapac‍cayasukhasamāyaugau paridīpitau hauti avisaṃvādanatau, cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina santuṭṭhilakkhaṇasabhāvasukhasamāyaugau alaubhabhāvatau, upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina  katapuñ‍ñatālakkhaṇahaitusukhasamāyaugau kilaisaihi anabhibhūtabhāvatau, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina vimuttisampattilakkhaṇadukkhūpasamasukhasamāyaugau paridīpitau hauti, ñāṇasampattiyā nibbānādhigamanatau|

tathā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina ariyassa sīlakkhandhassa anubaudhappaṭivaidhasiddhi, cāgādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina ariyassa samādhikkhandhassa, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina ariyassa pañ‍ñākkhandhassa, upasamādhiṭṭhānasamudāgamaina ariyassa vimuttikkhandhassa anubaudhappaṭivaidhasiddhi dīpitā hauti| sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaina ca tapasiddhi, cāgādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaina sabbanissaggasiddhi, upasamādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaina indriyasaṃvarasiddhi, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaina buddhisiddhi, taina ca nibbānasiddhi| tathā sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaina catuariyasac‍cābhisamayappaṭilābhau, cāgādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaina catuariyavaṃsappaṭilābhau, 0.upasamādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaina catuariyavihārappaṭilābhau, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaparipūraṇaina catuariyavauhārappaṭilābhau dīpitau hauti|

aparau nayau – bhagavatāti aitaina sattānaṃ laukiyalaukuttarasampattiabhikaṅkhādīpanaina tathāgatassa mahākaruṇā pakāsitā hauti| arahatāti aitaina pahānasampattidīpanaina pahānapañ‍ñā pakāsitā  hauti| tattha pañ‍ñāyassa dhammaraj‍japatti, karuṇāya dhammasaṃvibhāgau; pañ‍ñāya saṃsāradukkhanibbidā, karuṇāya saṃsāradukkhasahanaṃ; pañ‍ñāya paradukkhaparijānanaṃ, karuṇāya paradukkhappaṭikārārambhau| pañ‍ñāya parinibbānābhimukhabhāvau, karuṇāya tadadhigamau; pañ‍ñāya sayaṃ taraṇaṃ, karuṇāya paraisaṃ tāraṇaṃ; pañ‍ñāya buddhabhāvasiddhi, karuṇāya buddhakic‍casiddhi| karuṇāya vā baudhisattabhūmiyaṃ saṃsārābhimukhabhāvau, pañ‍ñāya tattha anabhirati| tathā karuṇāya paraisaṃ avihiṃsanaṃ, pañ‍ñāya sayaṃ paraihi abhāyanaṃ; karuṇāya paraṃ rakkhantau attānaṃ rakkhati, pañ‍ñāya attānaṃ rakkhantau paraṃ rakkhati| tathā karuṇāya aparantapau, pañ‍ñāya anattantapau| taina attahitāya paṭipan‍nādīsu catutthapuggalabhāvau siddhau hauti|

tathā karuṇāya laukanāthatā, pañ‍ñāya attanāthatā; karuṇāya cassa nin‍natābhāvau, pañ‍ñāya un‍natābhāvau| tathā karuṇāya sabbasattaisu janitānuggahau, pañ‍ñānugatattā na ca na sabbattha virattacittau; pañ‍ñāya sabbadhammaisu virattacittau, karuṇānugatattā na ca na sabbasattānuggahāya pavattau| yathā hi karuṇā tathāgatassa sinaihasaukavirahitā, aivaṃ pañ‍ñā ahaṃkāramamaṃkāravinimuttāti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ visaudhitā paramavisuddhāti daṭṭhabbā| tattha pañ‍ñākhaittaṃ balāni, karuṇākhaittaṃ vaisāraj‍jāni| taisu balasamāyaugaina paraihi na abhibhuyyati, vaisāraj‍jasamāyaugaina parai abhibhavati| balaihi satthusampadāsiddhi, vaisāraj‍jaihi sāsanasampadāsiddhi| tathā balaihi buddharatanasiddhi, vaisāraj‍jaihi dhammaratanasiddhīti ayamaittha ‘‘bhagavatā arahatā’’ti padadvayassa atthayaujanāya mukhamattadassanaṃ|

kasmā panaittha ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā’’ti vatvā puna ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ? anussavapaṭikkhaipaina niyamadassanatthaṃ| yathā hi kainaci paratau sutvā vuttaṃ yadipi ca jānantaina vuttaṃ, na tainaiva vuttaṃ parainapi vuttattā| na ca taṃ taina vuttamaiva, apica khau sutampi, na aivamidha| bhagavatā hi paratau asutvā sayambhuñāṇaina attanā adhigatamaiva vuttanti imassa visaisassa dassanatthaṃ dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā’’ tañ‍ca khau bhagavatāva vuttaṃ, na añ‍ñaina, vuttamaiva ca, na sutanti| adhikavacanañhi añ‍ñamatthaṃ baudhaitīti na punaruttidausau| aisa nayau itau paraisupi|

tathā  pubbaracanābhāvadassanatthaṃ dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| bhagavā hi sammāsambuddhatāya ṭhānuppattikappaṭibhānaina sampattaparisāya ajjhāsayānurūpaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti, na tassa kāraṇā dānādīnaṃ viya pubbaracanākic‍caṃ atthi| tainaitaṃ dassaiti – ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā, tañ‍ca khau na  pubbaracanāvasaina tak‍kapariyāhataṃ vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ, apica khau vainaiyyajjhāsayānurūpaṃ ṭhānasau vuttamaivā’’ti|

appaṭivattiyavacanabhāvadassanatthaṃ vā dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| yañhi bhagavatā vuttaṃ, vuttamaiva  taṃ, na kainaci paṭikkhipituṃ sak‍kā akkharasampattiyā atthasampattiyā ca| vuttaṃ haitaṃ –

‘‘aitaṃ bhagavatā bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ kainaci samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā’’tiādi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1081; mahāva॰ 17)|

aparampi vuttaṃ –

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, āgacchaiyya samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ‘na yidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, yaṃ samaṇaina gautamaina pañ‍ñattaṃ, ahamidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’tiādi| –

tasmā appaṭivattiyavacanabhāvadassanatthampi dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ|

atha vā sautūnaṃ atthanipphādakabhāvadassanatthaṃ dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| yañhi paraisaṃ āsayādiṃ ajānantaina asabbañ‍ñunā adaisai akālai vā vuttaṃ, taṃ sac‍campi samānaṃ sautūnaṃ atthanipphādanai asamatthatāya avuttaṃ nāma siyā, pagaiva asac‍caṃ| bhagavatā pana sammāsambuddhabhāvatau sammadaiva paraisaṃ āsayādiṃ daisakālaṃ atthasiddhiñ‍ca jānantaina vuttaṃ aikantaina sautūnaṃ yathādhippaitatthanipphādanatau vuttamaiva, natthi tassa avuttatāpariyāyau| tasmā sautūnaṃ atthanipphādakabhāvadassanatthampi dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| apica yathā na taṃ sutaṃ nāma, yaṃ na viñ‍ñātatthaṃ yañ‍ca na tathattāya paṭipan‍naṃ, aivaṃ na taṃ vuttaṃ nāma, yaṃ na sammā paṭiggahitaṃ| bhagavatau pana vacanaṃ catassaupi parisā sammadaiva paṭiggahaitvā  tathattāya paṭipaj‍janti| tasmā sammadaiva paṭiggahitabhāvadassanatthampi dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ|

atha vā ariyaihi aviruddhavacanabhāvadassanatthaṃ dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| yathā hi bhagavā  kusalākusalasāvaj‍jānavaj‍jabhaidai dhammai pavattinivattiyau sammutiparamatthai ca avisaṃvādaintau vadati, aivaṃ dhammasaināpatippabhutayau ariyāpi bhagavati dharamānai parinibbutai ca tassaiva daisanaṃ anugantvā vadanti, na tattha nānāvādatā| tasmā vuttamarahatā tatau parabhāgai  arahatā ariyasaṅghaināpīti aivaṃ ariyaihi aviruddhavacanabhāvadassanatthampi aivaṃ vuttaṃ|

atha vā purimaihi sammāsambuddhaihi vuttanayabhāvadassanatthaṃ dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| satipi hi jātigauttāyuppamāṇādivisaisai dasabalādiguṇaihi viya dhammadaisanāya buddhānaṃ visaisau natthi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ attanā ca tai pubbaināparaṃ aviruddhamaiva vadanti| tasmā vuttañhaitaṃ yathā buddhaihi attanā ca pubbai, idānipi amhākaṃ bhagavatā tathaiva vuttaṃ arahatāti aivaṃ purimabuddhaihi attanā ca suttantaraisu vuttanayabhāvadassanatthampi dvikkhattuṃ ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| taina buddhānaṃ daisanāya sabbattha aviraudhau dīpitau hauti|

atha vā ‘‘vutta’’nti yadaitaṃ dutiyaṃ padaṃ, taṃ arahantavuttabhāvavacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā arahatāpi vuttaṃ – ‘‘aikadhammaṃ, bhikkhavai’’tiādikaṃ idāni vuc‍camānaṃ vacananti| atha vā ‘‘vutta’’nti yadaitaṃ dutiyaṃ padaṃ, taṃ na vacanatthaṃ, atha khau vapanatthaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| tainaitaṃ dassaiti – ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā, tañ‍ca khau na vuttamattaṃ, na kathitamattaṃ; atha khau vainaiyyānaṃ kusalamūlaṃ vapita’’nti atthau| atha vā yadaitaṃ vuttanti dutiyaṃ padaṃ, taṃ vattanatthaṃ| ayaṃ hissa atthau – vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā arahatā, tañ‍ca khau na vuttamattaṃ, apica tadatthajātaṃ vuttaṃ caritanti| taina ‘‘yathā vādī bhagavā tathā kārī’’ti dassaiti| atha vā vuttaṃ bhagavatā, vuttavacanaṃ arahatā vattuṃ yuttaināti atthau|

atha  vā ‘‘vutta’’nti saṅkhaipakathāuddisanaṃ sandhāyāha, puna ‘‘vutta’’nti vitthārakathānidassanaṃ| bhagavā hi saṅkhaipatau vitthāratau ca dhammaṃ daisaiti| atha vā bhagavatau duruttavacanābhāvadassanatthaṃ ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā’’ti vatvā puna ‘‘vutta’’nti vuttaṃ| sabbadā ñāṇānugatavacīkammatāya hi bhagavatau savāsanapahīnasabbadausassa akkhalitabyappathassa kadācipi duruttaṃ nāma natthi| yathā kaici laukai satisammausaina vā davā vā ravā vā kiñ‍ci vatvā atha paṭiladdhasañ‍ñā pubbai vuttaṃ  avuttaṃ vā karaunti paṭisaṅkharaunti vā, na aivaṃ bhagavā| bhagavā pana nic‍cakālaṃ samāhitau| asammausadhammau asammauhadhammau ca sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇasamupabyūḷhāya paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāya upanītamatthaṃ aparimitakālaṃ sambhatapuñ‍ñasambhārasamudāgataihi anañ‍ñasādhāraṇaihi visadavisuddhaihi karaṇavisaisaihi sautāyatanarasāyanabhūtaṃ  suṇantānaṃ amatavassaṃ vassantau viya sautabbasāraṃ savanānuttariyaṃ catusac‍caṃ pakāsaintau karavīkarutamañ‍junā saraina sabhāvaniruttiyā vainaiyyajjhāsayānurūpaṃ vacanaṃ vadati, natthi tattha vālaggamattampi avakkhalitaṃ, kutau pana duruttāvakāsau| tasmā ‘‘yaṃ bhagavatā vuttaṃ, taṃ vuttamaiva, na avuttaṃ duruttaṃ vā kadāci hautī’’ti dassanatthaṃ – ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā’’ti vatvā puna – ‘‘vuttamarahatā’’ti vuttanti na aittha punaruttidausauti| aivamaittha punaruttasaddassa sātthakatā vaiditabbā|

iti mai sutanti aittha itīti ayaṃ itisaddau haituparisamāpanādipadatthavipariyāyapakāranidassanāvadhāraṇādianaikatthappabhaidau| tathā haisa – ‘‘ruppatīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā rūpanti vuc‍catī’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.79) haituatthai dissati| ‘‘tasmātiha mai, bhikkhavai, dhammadāyādā bhavatha, mā āmisadāyādā| atthi mai tumhaisu anukampā – kinti mai sāvakā dhammadāyādā bhavaiyyuṃ, nau āmisadāyādā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.30) parisamāpanai| ‘‘iti vā iti aivarūpā visūkadassanā paṭiviratau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.10) ādiatthai| ‘‘māgaṇḍiyauti vā tassa brāhmaṇassa saṅkhā samañ‍ñā pañ‍ñatti vauhārau nāmaṃ nāmakammaṃ nāmadhaiyyaṃ nirutti byañ‍janaṃ abhilāpau’’tiādīsu (mahāni॰ 75) padatthavipariyāyai| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, sappaṭibhayau bālau, appaṭibhayau paṇḍitau; saupaddavau bālau, anupaddavau paṇḍitau; saupasaggau bālau, anupasaggau paṇḍitau’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 3.1) pakārai| ‘‘sabbamatthīti khau, kac‍cāna, ayamaikau antau, sabbaṃ natthīti khau, kac‍cāna, ayaṃ dutiyau antau’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.15) nidassanai| ‘‘atthi idappac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti iti puṭṭhaina satā, ānanda, atthītissa vacanīyaṃ| kiṃpac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti iti cai vadaiyya, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti ic‍cassa vacanīya’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.96) avadhāraṇai, san‍niṭṭhānaiti atthau| svāyamidha pakāranidassanāvadhāraṇaisu daṭṭhabbau|

tattha pakāratthaina itisaddaina aitamatthaṃ dīpaiti – nānānayanipuṇamanaikajjhāsayasamuṭṭhānaṃ atthabyañ‍janasampan‍naṃ vividhapāṭihāriyaṃ dhammatthadaisanāpaṭivaidhagambhīraṃ sabbasattānaṃ sakasakabhāsānurūpatau sautapathamāgacchantaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ sabbappakāraina kau samatthau viñ‍ñātuṃ, sabbathāmaina pana sautukāmataṃ janaitvāpi iti mai sutaṃ, mayāpi aikaina pakāraina sutanti|

aittha  ca aikattanānattaabyāpāraaivaṃdhammatāsaṅkhātā nandiyāvattatipukkhalasīhavik‍kīḷitadisālaucanaaṅkusasaṅkhātā ca visayādibhaidaina nānāvidhā nayā nānānayā| nayā vā pāḷigatiyau, tā ca pañ‍ñattianupañ‍ñattiādivasaina saṃkilaisabhāgiyādilaukiyāditadubhayavaumissatādivasaina, kusalādivasaina, khandhādivasaina, saṅgahādivasaina, samayavimuttādivasaina, ṭhapanādivasaina, kusalamūlādivasaina, tikapaṭṭhānādivasaina ca nānappakārāti nānānayā| taihi nipuṇaṃ saṇhaṃ sukhumanti nānānayanipuṇaṃ|

āsayauva ajjhāsayau, sau ca sassatādibhaidaina apparajakkhatādibhaidaina ca anaikavidhau| attajjhāsayādikau aiva vā anaikau ajjhāsayau anaikajjhāsayau| sau samuṭṭhānaṃ uppattihaitu aitassāti anaikajjhāsayasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

kusalādiatthasampattiyā tabbibhāvanabyañ‍janasampattiyā saṅkāsanapakāsanavivaraṇavibhajanauttānīkaraṇapañ‍ñattivasaina chahi atthapadaihi akkharapadabyañ‍janākāraniruttiniddaisavasaina chahi byañ‍janapadaihi ca saman‍nāgatattā atthabyañ‍janasampan‍naṃ|

iddhiādaisanānusāsanībhaidaina  taisu ca aikaikassa visayādibhaidaina vividhaṃ bahuvidhaṃ vā pāṭihāriyaṃ  aitassāti vividhapāṭihāriyaṃ| tattha paṭipakkhaharaṇatau rāgādikilaisāpanayanatau paṭihāriyanti atthai sati bhagavatau paṭipakkhā rāgādayau na santi yai haritabbā, puthuj‍janānampi vigatūpak‍kilaisai aṭṭhaguṇasaman‍nāgatai cittai hatapaṭipakkhai iddhividhaṃ pavattati| tasmā tattha pavattavauhāraina ca na sak‍kā idha pāṭihāriyanti vattuṃ| yasmā pana mahākāruṇikassa bhagavatau vainaiyyagatā ca kilaisā paṭipakkhā, tasmā taisaṃ haraṇatau pāṭihāriyaṃ| atha vā bhagavatau sāsanassa ca paṭipakkhā titthiyā, taisaṃ haraṇatau pāṭihāriyaṃ| tai hi diṭṭhiharaṇavasaina diṭṭhippakāsanai asamatthabhāvaina ca iddhiādaisanānusāsanīhi haritā apanītā haunti| paṭīti vā pacchāti atthau| tasmā samāhitai cittai vigatūpak‍kilaisai katakic‍caina pacchā haritabbaṃ pavattaitabbanti paṭihāriyaṃ| attanau vā upak‍kilaisaisu catutthajjhānamaggaihi haritaisu pacchā haraṇaṃ paṭihāriyaṃ| iddhiādaisanānusāsaniyau ca vigatūpak‍kilaisaina katakic‍caina sattahitatthaṃ puna pavattaitabbā, haritaisu ca attanau upak‍kilaisaisu parasantānai upak‍kilaisaharaṇāni hauntīti paṭihāriyāni bhavanti| paṭihāriyamaiva pāṭihāriyaṃ, paṭihāriyai vā iddhiādaisanānusāsanisamudāyai bhavaṃ aikaikaṃ  pāṭihāriyanti vuc‍cati| paṭihāriyaṃ vā catutthajjhānaṃ maggau ca paṭipakkhaharaṇatau, tattha jātaṃ, tasmiṃ vā nimittabhūtai, tatau vā āgatanti pāṭihāriyaṃ|

yasmā pana tantiatthadaisanātabbauhārābhisamayasaṅkhātā haituhaituphalatadubhayapañ‍ñattipaṭivaidhasaṅkhātā vā dhammatthadaisanāpaṭivaidhā gambhīrā, anupacitasambhāraihi sasādīhi viya mahāsamuddau dukkhaugāḷhā alabbhanaiyyappatiṭṭhā ca| tasmā taihi catūhi gambhīrabhāvaihi yuttanti dhammatthadaisanāpaṭivaidhagambhīraṃ|

aikau aiva bhagavatau dhammadaisanāghausau aikasmiṃ khaṇai pavattamānau nānābhāsānaṃ sattānaṃ attanau attanau bhāsāvasaina apubbaṃ acarimaṃ gahaṇūpagau hutvā atthādhigamāya hauti| acintaiyyau hi  buddhānaṃ buddhānubhāvauti sabbasattānaṃ  sakasakabhāsānurūpatau sautapathamāgacchatīti vaiditabbaṃ|

nidassanatthaina – ‘‘nāhaṃ sayambhū, na mayā idaṃ sacchikata’’nti attānaṃ parimaucaintau – ‘‘iti mai sutaṃ, mayāpi aivaṃ suta’’nti idāni vattabbaṃ sakalaṃ suttaṃ nidassaiti|

avadhāraṇatthaina – ‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, mama sāvakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahussutānaṃ yadidaṃ ānandau, gatimantānaṃ, satimantānaṃ, dhitimantānaṃ, upaṭṭhākānaṃ yadidaṃ ānandau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 1.219-223) aivaṃ bhagavatā, ‘‘āyasmā ānandau atthakusalau dhammakusalau byañ‍janakusalau niruttikusalau pubbāparakusalau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 5.169) aivaṃ dhammasaināpatinā ca pasatthabhāvānurūpaṃ attanau dhāraṇabalaṃ dassaintau sattānaṃ sautukamyataṃ janaiti – ‘‘iti mai sutaṃ, tañ‍ca khau atthatau vā byañ‍janatau vā anūnamanadhikaṃ, aivamaiva, na añ‍ñathā, daṭṭhabba’’nti| añ‍ñathāti bhagavatau sammukhā sutākāratau añ‍ñathā, na pana bhagavatā daisitākāratau| acintaiyyānubhāvā hi bhagavatau daisanā, sā na sabbākāraina sak‍kā viñ‍ñātunti vuttauvāyamatthau| sutākārāvirujjhanamaiva hi dhāraṇabalaṃ| na haittha atthantaratāparihārau dvin‍nampi atthānaṃ aikavisayattā| itarathā hi thairau bhagavatau daisanāya sabbathā paṭiggahaṇai samatthau asamatthauti vā āpaj‍jaiyyāti|

mai-saddau tīsu atthaisu dissati| tathā hissa – ‘‘gāthābhigītaṃ mai abhaujanaiyya’’ntiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.194; su॰ ni॰ 81) mayāti atthau| ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā  saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitū’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.88; 5.381; a॰ ni॰ 4.257) mayhanti atthau| ‘‘dhammadāyādā mai, bhikkhavai, bhavathā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.29) mamāti atthau| idha pana ‘‘mayā suta’’nti ca ‘‘mama suta’’nti ca atthadvayai yuj‍jati|

aittha ca yau parau na hauti, sau attāti aivaṃ vattabbai niyakajjhattasaṅkhātai sakasantānai vattanatau tividhaupi mai-saddau yadipi aikasmiṃyaiva atthai dissati, karaṇasampadānādivisaisasaṅkhātau panassa vij‍jataivāyaṃ atthabhaidauti āha – ‘‘mai-saddau tīsu atthaisu dissatī’’ti|

sutanti  ayaṃ suta-saddau saupasaggau anupasaggau ca gamanavissutakilin‍nūpacitānuyaugasautaviñ‍ñaiyyasautadvārānusāraviñ‍ñātādianaikatthappabhaidau| kiñ‍cāpi hi kiriyāvisaisakau upasaggau, jautakabhāvatau pana satipi tasmiṃ suta-saddau aiva taṃ taṃ atthaṃ vadatīti anupasaggassa sutasaddassa atthuddhārai saupasaggaupi udāharīyati|

tattha ‘‘saināya pasutau’’tiādīsu gacchantauti atthau| ‘‘sutadhammassa passatau’’tiādīsu (udā॰ 11) vissutadhammassāti atthau| ‘‘avassutā avassutassā’’tiādīsu (pāci॰ 657) kilin‍nā kilin‍nassāti atthau| ‘‘tumhaihi puñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ anappaka’’ntiādīsu (khu॰ pā॰ 7.12) upacitanti atthau| ‘‘yai jhānappasutā dhīrā’’tiādīsu (dha॰ pa॰ 181) jhānānuyuttāti atthau| ‘‘diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ muta’’ntiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.241) sautaviñ‍ñaiyyanti atthau| ‘‘sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.339) sautadvārānusāraviñ‍ñātadharauti atthau| idha panassa ‘‘sautadvārānusāraina upadhārita’’nti vā ‘‘upadhāraṇa’’nti vā atthau| mai-saddassa hi mayāti atthai sati ‘‘iti mai sutaṃ, mayā sautadvārānusāraina upadhārita’’nti atthau| mamāti atthai sati ‘‘iti mama sutaṃ sautadvārānusāraina upadhāraṇa’’nti atthau|

aivamaitaisu tīsu padaisu yasmā sutasaddasan‍nidhānai payuttaina itisaddaina savanakiriyājautakaina bhavitabbaṃ| tasmā itīti sautaviñ‍ñāṇādiviñ‍ñāṇakic‍canidassanaṃ| maiti vuttaviñ‍ñāṇasamaṅgipuggalanidassanaṃ| sabbānipi vākyāni aivakāratthasahitāniyaiva avadhāraṇaphalattā| taina sutanti assavanabhāvappaṭikkhaipatau anūnāviparītaggahaṇanidassanaṃ| yathā hi sutaṃ sutamaivāti vattabbataṃ  arahati, taṃ sammā sutaṃ anūnaggahaṇaṃ aviparītaggahaṇañ‍ca hautīti| atha vā saddantaratthāpauhanavasaina saddau atthaṃ vadatīti, yasmā sutanti aitassa asutaṃ na hautīti ayamatthau, tasmā sutanti assavanabhāvappaṭikkhaipatau anūnāviparītaggahaṇanidassanaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – iti mai sutaṃ, na diṭṭhaṃ, na sayambhuñāṇaina sacchikataṃ, na añ‍ñathā vā upaladdhaṃ, apica sutaṃva, tañ‍ca khau sammadaivāti| avadhāraṇatthai  vā itisaddai ayamatthayaujanāti tadapaikkhassa suta-saddassa niyamatthau  sambhavatīti assavanabhāvappaṭikkhaipau, anūnāviparītaggahaṇanidassanatā ca vaiditabbā| aivaṃ savanahaitusavanavisaisavasaina padattayassa atthayaujanā katāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

tathā itīti sautadvārānusāraina pavattāya viñ‍ñāṇavīthiyā nānatthabyañ‍janaggahaṇatau nānappakāraina ārammaṇai pavattibhāvappakāsanaṃ ākāratthau itisaddauti katvā| maiti attappakāsanaṃ| sutanti dhammappakāsanaṃ yathāvuttāya viñ‍ñāṇavīthiyā pariyattidhammārammaṇattā| ayañhaittha saṅkhaipau – nānappakāraina ārammaṇai pavattāya viñ‍ñāṇavīthiyā kāraṇabhūtāya mayā na añ‍ñaṃ kataṃ, idaṃ pana kataṃ, ayaṃ dhammau sutauti|

tathā itīti nidassitabbappakāsanaṃ nidassanatthau iti-saddauti katvā nidassaitabbassa nidassitabbattābhāvābhāvatau| tasmā itisaddaina sakalampi sutaṃ pac‍cāmaṭṭhanti vaiditabbaṃ| maiti puggalappakāsanaṃ| sutanti puggalakic‍cappakāsanaṃ| suta-saddaina hi labbhamānā savanakiriyā savanaviñ‍ñāṇappabandhappaṭibaddhā, tattha ca puggalavauhārau| na hi puggalavauhārarahitai dhammappabandhai savanakiriyā labbhati| tassāyaṃ saṅkhaipatthau – yaṃ suttaṃ niddisissāmi, taṃ mayā iti sutanti|

tathā itīti yassa cittasantānassa nānārammaṇappavattiyā nānatthabyañ‍janaggahaṇaṃ hauti, tassa nānākāraniddaisau ākāratthau itisaddauti katvā| itīti hi ayaṃ ākārapañ‍ñatti dhammānaṃ taṃ taṃ pavattiākāraṃ upādāya pañ‍ñāpaitabbasabhāvattā| maiti kattuniddaisau| sutanti visayaniddaisau| sautabbau hi dhammau savanakiriyākattupuggalassa savanakiriyāvasaina pavattiṭṭhānaṃ hauti| aittāvatā nānappakārappavattaina cittasantānaina taṃsamaṅginau kattu visayai gahaṇasan‍niṭṭhānaṃ dassitaṃ hauti|

atha vā itīti puggalakic‍caniddaisau| sutānañhi dhammānaṃ gahitākārassa nidassanassa avadhāraṇassa vā pakāsanabhāvaina itisaddaina tadākārādidhāraṇassa puggalavauhārūpādānadhammabyāpārabhāvatau  puggalakic‍caṃ  nāma niddiṭṭhaṃ hautīti| sutanti viñ‍ñāṇakic‍caniddaisau| puggalavādinaupi hi savanakiriyā viñ‍ñāṇanirapaikkhā na hautīti| maiti ubhayakic‍cayuttapuggalaniddaisau| maiti hi saddappavatti aikantainaiva sattavisaisavisayā, viñ‍ñāṇakic‍cañ‍ca tatthaiva samaudahitabbanti| ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipau – mayā savanakic‍caviñ‍ñāṇasamaṅginā puggalaina viñ‍ñāṇavasaina laddhassavanakic‍cavauhāraina sutanti|

tathā itīti ca maiti ca sac‍cikaṭṭhaparamatthavasaina avij‍jamānapañ‍ñatti| sabbassa hi saddādhigamanīyassa atthassa pañ‍ñattimukhainaiva paṭipaj‍jitabbattā sabbapañ‍ñattīnañ‍ca vij‍jamānādīsu chasvaiva pañ‍ñattīsu avaraudhau, tasmā yau māyāmarīciādayau viya abhūtatthau, anussavādīhi gahaitabbau viya anuttamatthau ca na hauti| sau rūpasaddādikau ruppanānubhavanādikau ca paramatthasabhāvau sac‍cikaṭṭhaparamatthavasaina vij‍jati| yau pana itīti ca maiti ca vuc‍camānau ākārādiaparamatthasabhāvau sac‍cikaṭṭhaparamatthavasaina anupalabbhamānau avij‍jamānapañ‍ñatti nāma, kimaittha taṃ paramatthatau atthi, yaṃ itīti vā maiti vā niddaisaṃ labhaitha| sutanti vij‍jamānapañ‍ñatti| yañhi taṃ sautaina upaladdhaṃ, taṃ paramatthatau vij‍jamānanti|

tathā itīti sautapathamāgatai dhammai upādāya taisaṃ upadhāritākārādīnaṃ pac‍cāmasanavasaina| maiti sasantatipariyāpan‍nai khandhai karaṇādivisaisavisiṭṭhai upādāya vattabbatau upādāpañ‍ñatti| sutanti diṭṭhādīni upanidhāya vattabbatau upanidhāpañ‍ñatti| diṭṭhādisabhāvarahitai saddāyatanai pavattamānaupi sutavauhārau dutiyaṃ, tatiyanti ādikau viya paṭhamādiṃ nissāya ‘‘yaṃ na diṭṭhamutaviñ‍ñātanirapaikkhaṃ, taṃ suta’’nti viñ‍ñaiyyattā diṭṭhādīni upanidhāya vattabbau hauti| asutaṃ na hautīti hi sutanti pakāsitauyamatthauti|

aittha  ca itīti vacanaina asammauhaṃ dīpaiti| paṭividdhā hi atthassa pakāravisaisā itīti idha āyasmatā ānandaina pac‍cāmaṭṭhā, tainassa asammauhau dīpitau| na hi sammūḷhau nānappakārappaṭivaidhasamatthau hauti, laubhappahānādivasaina nānappakārā duppaṭividdhā ca suttatthā niddisīyanti| sutanti vacanaina asammausaṃ dīpaiti sutākārassa yāthāvatau dassiyamānattā yassa hi sutaṃ sammuṭṭhaṃ hauti, na sau kālantarai mayā sutanti paṭijānāti| ic‍cassa asammauhaina sammauhābhāvaina pañ‍ñāya aiva vā savanakālasambhūtāya taduttarikālapañ‍ñāsiddhi, tathā asammausaina satisiddhi| tattha pañ‍ñāpubbaṅgamāya satiyā byañ‍janāvadhāraṇasamatthatā| byañ‍janānañhi paṭivijjhitabbau ākārau nātigambhīrau, yathāsutadhāraṇamaiva tattha karaṇīyanti satiyā  byāpārau adhikau, pañ‍ñā tattha guṇībhūtā hauti pañ‍ñāya pubbaṅgamāti katvā| satipubbaṅgamāya pañ‍ñāya atthappaṭivaidhasamatthatā| atthassa hi paṭivijjhitabbau ākārau gambhīrauti pañ‍ñāya byāpārau adhikau, sati tattha guṇībhūtā hauti satiyā pubbaṅgamāti katvā| tadubhayasamatthatāyaugaina atthabyañ‍janasampan‍nassa dhammakausassa anupālanasamatthatāya dhammabhaṇḍāgārikattasiddhi|

aparau nayau – itīti vacanaina yaunisaumanasikāraṃ dīpaiti| taina vuc‍camānānaṃ ākāranidassanāvadhāraṇatthānaṃ upari vakkhamānānaṃ nānappakārappaṭivaidhajautakānaṃ aviparītasaddhammavisayattā| na hi ayaunisau manasikarautau nānappakārappaṭivaidhau sambhavati| sutanti vacanaina avikkhaipaṃ dīpaiti, nidānapucchāvasaina pakaraṇappattassa vakkhamānassa suttassa savanaṃ na samādhānamantaraina sambhavati vikkhittacittassa savanābhāvatau| tathā hi vikkhittacittau puggalau sabbasampattiyā vuc‍camānaupi ‘‘na mayā sutaṃ, puna bhaṇathā’’ti vadati| yaunisaumanasikāraina caittha attasammāpaṇidhiṃ pubbaikatapuñ‍ñatañ‍ca sādhaiti, sammā appaṇihitattassa pubbai akatapuñ‍ñassa vā tadabhāvatau| avikkhaipaina saddhammassavanaṃ sappurisūpanissayañ‍ca sādhaiti, assutavatau sappurisūpanissayarahitassa  ca tadabhāvatau| na hi vikkhittacittau saddhammaṃ sautuṃ sak‍kauti, na ca sappurisai anupassayamānassa savanaṃ atthi|

aparau nayau – ‘‘yassa cittasantānassa nānākārappavattiyā nānatthabyañ‍janaggahaṇaṃ hauti, tassa nānākāraniddaisau’’ti vuttaṃ| yasmā ca sau bhagavatau vacanassa atthabyañ‍janappabhaidaparicchaidavasaina sakalasāsanasampatiaugāhanaina niravasaisaparahitapāripūrikāraṇabhūtau aivaṃbhaddakau  ākārau na sammā appaṇihitattanau pubbai akatapuñ‍ñassa vā hauti, tasmā itīti iminā bhaddakaina ākāraina pacchimacak‍kadvayasampattimattanau dīpaiti, sutanti savanayaugaina purimacak‍kadvayasampattiṃ| na hi appatirūpai daisai vasatau sappurisūpanissayarahitassa vā savanaṃ atthi| ic‍cassa pacchimacak‍kadvayasiddhiyā āsayasuddhi siddhā hauti, sammā paṇihitattau pubbai ca katapuñ‍ñau visuddhāsayau hauti, tadavisuddhihaitūnaṃ kilaisānaṃ dūrībhāvatau| tathā hi vuttaṃ – ‘‘sammā paṇihitaṃ cittaṃ, saiyyasau naṃ tatau karai’’ti (dha॰ pa॰ 43) ‘‘katapuñ‍ñausi tvaṃ, ānanda, padhānamanuyuñ‍ja, khippaṃ hauhisi anāsavau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.207) ca| purimacak‍kadvayasiddhiyā payaugasuddhi| patirūpadaisavāsaina hi sappurisūpanissayaina ca sādhūnaṃ diṭṭhānugatiāpaj‍janainapi visuddhappayaugau hauti| tāya ca āsayasuddhiyā adhigamabyattisiddhi, pubbai aiva taṇhādiṭṭhisaṃkilaisānaṃ visaudhitattā payaugasuddhiyā  āgamabyattisiddhi| suparisuddhakāyavacīpayaugau hi vippaṭisārābhāvatau avikkhittacittau pariyattiyaṃ visāradau hauti| iti payaugāsayasuddhassa āgamādhigamasampan‍nassa vacanaṃ aruṇuggamanaṃ viya sūriyassa udayatau, yaunisaumanasikārau viya ca kusaladhammassa, arahati bhagavatau vacanassa pubbaṅgamaṃ bhavitunti ṭhānai nidānaṃ ṭhapaintau iti mai sutantiādimāha|

aparau nayau – itīti iminā pubbai vuttanayaina nānappakārappaṭivaidhadīpakaina attanau atthapaṭibhānapaṭisambhidāsampattisabbhāvaṃ  dīpaiti| sutanti iminā itisaddasan‍nidhānatau vakkhamānāpaikkhāya vā sautabbabhaidappaṭivaidhadīpakaina dhammaniruttipaṭisambhidāsampattisabbhāvaṃ dīpaiti| itīti ca idaṃ vuttanayainaiva yaunisaumanasikāradīpakaṃ vacanaṃ bhāsamānau ‘‘aitai mayā dhammā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā’’ti dīpaiti| pariyattidhammā hi ‘‘idha sīlaṃ kathitaṃ, idha samādhi, idha pañ‍ñā, aittakā aittha anusandhiyau’’tiādinā nayaina manasā anupaikkhitā anussavākāraparivitak‍kasahitāya dhammanijjhānakkhantibhūtāya ñātapariñ‍ñāsaṅkhātāya vā diṭṭhiyā tattha tattha vuttarūpārūpadhammai ‘‘iti rūpaṃ, aittakaṃ rūpa’’ntiādinā nayaina suṭṭhu vavatthapaitvā paṭividdhā attanau paraisañ‍ca hitasukhāvahā hauntīti| suttanti idaṃ savanayaugaparidīpakavacanaṃ bhāsamānau ‘‘bahū  mayā dhammā sutā dhātā vacasā paricitā’’ti dīpaiti| sautāvadhānappaṭibaddhā hi pariyattidhammassa savanadhāraṇaparicayā| tadubhayainapi dhammassa svākkhātabhāvaina atthabyañ‍janapāripūriṃ dīpaintau savanai ādaraṃ janaiti| atthabyañ‍janaparipuṇṇañhi dhammaṃ ādaraina assuṇantau mahatā hitā paribāhirau hautīti ādaraṃ janaitvā sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammau sautabbau|

iti mai sutanti iminā pana sakalaina vacanaina āyasmā ānandau tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ attanau adahantau asappurisabhūmiṃ atik‍kamati, sāvakattaṃ paṭijānantau sappurisabhūmiṃ auk‍kamati| tathā asaddhammā cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpaiti, saddhammai cittaṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti| ‘‘kaivalaṃ sutamaivaitaṃ mayā, tassaiva pana bhagavatau vacana’’nti dīpaintau attānaṃ parimaucaiti, satthāraṃ apadisati, jinavacanaṃ appaiti, dhammanaittiṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti|

apica iti mai sutanti attanā uppāditabhāvaṃ appaṭijānantau purimassavanaṃ vivarantau sammukhā paṭiggahitamidaṃ mayā tassa bhagavatau catuvaisāraj‍javisāradassa dasabaladharassa āsabhaṭṭhānaṭṭhāyinau sīhanādanādinau sabbasattuttamassa dhammissarassa dhammarājassa dhammādhipatinau dhammadīpassa  dhammasaraṇassa saddhammavaracak‍kavattinau sammāsambuddhassa| na aittha atthai vā dhammai vā padai vā  byañ‍janai vā kaṅkhā vā vimati vā kātabbāti sabbadaivamanussānaṃ imasmiṃ dhammavinayai assaddhiyaṃ vināsaiti, saddhāsampadaṃ uppādaiti| tainaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘vināsayati assaddhaṃ, saddhaṃ vaḍḍhaiti sāsanai|

iti mai sutamic‍caivaṃ, vadaṃ gautamasāvakau’’ti||

aitthāha – ‘‘kasmā panaittha yathā añ‍ñaisu suttaisu ‘aivaṃ mai sutaṃ, aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā’tiādinā kāladaisai apadisitvāva nidānaṃ bhāsitaṃ, aivaṃ na bhāsita’’nti? aparai tāva āhu – na pana thairaina bhāsitattā| idañhi nidānaṃ na āyasmatā ānandaina paṭhamaṃ bhāsitaṃ khuj‍juttarāya pana bhagavatā upāsikāsu bahussutabhāvaina aitadaggai ṭhapitāya saikkhappaṭisambhidāppattāya ariyasāvikāya sāmāvatippamukhānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ itthisatānaṃ paṭhamaṃ bhāsitaṃ|

tatrāyaṃ  anupubbīkathā – itau kira kappasatasahassamatthakai padumuttarau nāma sammāsambuddhau laukai uppaj‍jitvā pavattitavaradhammacak‍kau haṃsavatiyaṃ viharati| athaikadivasaṃ haṃsavatiyaṃ aikā kuladhītā satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sautuṃ gacchantīhi upāsikāhi saddhiṃ ārāmaṃ gatā| satthāraṃ aikaṃ upāsikaṃ bahussutānaṃ aitadaggai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā adhikāraṃ katvā taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaisi| satthāpi naṃ byākāsi ‘‘anāgatai gautamassa nāma sammāsambuddhassa sāvikānaṃ upāsikānaṃ bahussutānaṃ aggā bhavissatī’’ti| tassā yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivalaukai nibbattitvā puna manussaisūti aivaṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsarantiyā kappasatasahassaṃ atik‍kantaṃ| atha imasmiṃ bhaddakappai amhākaṃ bhagavatau kālai sā daivalaukatau cavitvā ghausakasaiṭṭhissa gaihai dāsiyā kucchismiṃ paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi, uttarātissā nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sā jātakālai khuj‍jā ahausīti khuj‍juttarātvaiva pañ‍ñāyittha| sā aparabhāgai ghausakasaiṭṭhinā rañ‍ñau utainassa sāmāvatiyā din‍nakālai tassā paricārikabhāvaina din‍nā rañ‍ñau utainassa antaipurai vasati|

taina ca samayaina kausambiyaṃ ghausakasaiṭṭhikuk‍kuṭasaiṭṭhipāvārikasaiṭṭhinau  bhagavantaṃ uddissa tayau vihārai kāraitvā janapadacārikaṃ carantai tathāgatai kausambinagaraṃ sampattai buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa vihārai niyyādaitvā mahādānāni pavattaisuṃ, māsamattaṃ atik‍kami| atha naisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘buddhā nāma sabbalaukānukampakā, añ‍ñaisampi aukāsaṃ dassāmā’’ti kausambinagaravāsinaupi  janassa aukāsaṃ akaṃsu| tatau paṭṭhāya nāgarā vīthisabhāgaina gaṇasabhāgaina mahādānaṃ dainti| athaikadivasaṃ satthā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau mālākārajaiṭṭhakassa gaihai nisīdi| tasmiṃ khaṇai khuj‍juttarā sāmāvatiyā pupphāni gahaituṃ aṭṭha kahāpaṇai ādāya taṃ gaihaṃ agamāsi| mālākārajaiṭṭhakau taṃ disvā ‘‘amma uttarai, aj‍ja tuyhaṃ pupphāni dātuṃ khaṇau natthi, ahaṃ buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ parivisāmi, tvampi parivaisanāya sahāyikā hauhi, aivaṃ itau paraisaṃ vaiyyāvac‍cakaraṇatau muc‍cissasī’’ti āha| tatau khuj‍juttarā buddhānaṃ bhattaggai vaiyyāvac‍caṃ akāsi| sā satthārā upanisin‍nakathāvasaina kathitaṃ sabbamaiva dhammaṃ uggaṇhi, anumaudanaṃ pana sutvā sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhāsi|

sā  añ‍ñaisu divasaisu cattārauva kahāpaṇai datvā pupphāni gahaitvā gacchati, tasmiṃ pana divasai diṭṭhasac‍cabhāvaina parasantakai cittaṃ anuppādaitvā aṭṭhapi kahāpaṇai datvā pacchiṃ pūraitvā pupphāni gahaitvā sāmāvatiyā santikaṃ agamāsi| atha naṃ sā pucchi ‘‘amma uttarai, tvaṃ añ‍ñaisu divasaisu na bahūni pupphāni āharasi, aj‍ja pana bahukāni, kiṃ nau rājā uttaritaraṃ pasan‍nau’’ti? sā musā vattuṃ abhabbatāya atītai attanā kataṃ anigūhitvā sabbaṃ kathaisi| atha ‘‘kasmā aj‍ja bahūni āharasī’’ti ca vuttā ‘‘aj‍jāhaṃ sammāsambuddhassa dhammaṃ sutvā amataṃ sacchākāsiṃ, tasmā tumhai na vañ‍caimī’’ti āha| taṃ sutvā ‘‘arai duṭṭhadāsi, aittakaṃ kālaṃ tayā gahitai kahāpaṇai daihī’’ti ataj‍jaitvā pubbahaitunā caudiyamānā ‘‘amma, tayā pītaṃ amataṃ, amhaipi pāyaihī’’ti vatvā ‘‘taina hi maṃ nhāpaihī’’ti  vuttai sauḷasahi gandhaudakaghaṭaihi nhāpaitvā dvai maṭṭhasāṭakai dāpaisi| sā aikaṃ nivāsaitvā aikaṃ pārupitvā āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā āsanai nisīditvā vicitrabījaniṃ ādāya nīcāsanaisu nisin‍nāni pañ‍ca mātugāmasatāni āmantaitvā saikhappaṭisambhidāsu ṭhatvā satthārā daisitaniyāmainaiva tāsaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi| daisanāvasānai tā sabbā sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhahiṃsu| tā sabbāpi khuj‍juttaraṃ vanditvā ‘‘amma, aj‍ja paṭṭhāya tvaṃ kiliṭṭhakammaṃ mā kari, amhākaṃ mātuṭṭhānai ācariyaṭṭhānai ca patiṭṭhāhī’’ti garuṭṭhānai ṭhapayiṃsu|

kasmā panaisā dāsī hutvā nibbattāti? sā kira kassapasammāsambuddhakālai bārāṇasiyaṃ saiṭṭhidhītā hutvā nibbattā| aikāya khīṇāsavatthairiyā upaṭṭhākakulaṃ gatāya ‘‘aitaṃ mai ayyai, pasādhanapaiḷikaṃ daithā’’ti vaiyyāvac‍caṃ kāraisi| thairīpi ‘‘adaintiyā mayi āghātaṃ uppādaitvā nirayai nibbattissati, daintiyā paraisaṃ dāsī hutvā nibbattissati, nirayasantāpatau dāsibhāvau  saiyyau’’ti anuddayaṃ paṭic‍ca tassā vacanaṃ akāsi| sā taina kammaina pañ‍ca jātisatāni paraisaṃ dāsīyaiva hutvā nibbatti|

kasmā pana khuj‍jā ahausi? anuppan‍nai kira buddhai ayaṃ bārāṇasirañ‍ñau gaihai vasantī aikaṃ rājakulūpakaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ thaukaṃ khuj‍jadhātukaṃ disvā attanā  sahavāsīnaṃ mātugāmānaṃ puratau parihāsaṃ karauntī yathāvaj‍jaṃ kaiḷivasaina khuj‍jākāraṃ dassaisi, tasmā khuj‍jā hutvā nibbatti|

kiṃ pana katvā pañ‍ñavantī jātāti? anuppan‍nai kira buddhai ayaṃ bārāṇasirañ‍ñau gaihai vasantī aṭṭha pac‍caikabuddhai rājagaihatau uṇhapāyāsassa pūritai pattai parivattitvā parivattitvā gaṇhantai disvā attanau santakāni aṭṭha dantavalayāni ‘‘idha ṭhapaitvā gaṇhathā’’ti adāsi| tai tathā katvā aulaukaisuṃ| ‘‘tumhākañ‍ñaiva tāni paric‍cattāni, gahaitvā gacchathā’’ti āha| tai nandamūlakapabbhāraṃ agamaṃsu| aj‍jāpi tāni valayāni araugānaiva| sā tassa nissandaina pañ‍ñavantī jātā|

atha naṃ sāmāvatippamukhāni pañ‍ca itthisatāni ‘‘amma, tvaṃ divasai divasai satthu santikaṃ gantvā bhagavatā daisitaṃ dhammaṃ sutvā amhākaṃ daisaihī’’ti vadiṃsu| sā tathā karauntī aparabhāgai tipiṭakadharā jātā| tasmā naṃ  satthā – ‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, mama sāvikānaṃ bahussutānaṃ upāsikānaṃ yadidaṃ khuj‍juttarā’’ti aitadaggai ṭhapaisi| iti upāsikāsu bahussutabhāvaina satthārā aitadaggai ṭhapitā paṭisambhidāppattā khuj‍juttarā ariyasāvikā satthari kausambiyaṃ viharantai kālaina kālaṃ satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā antaipuraṃ gantvā sāmāvatippamukhānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ itthisatānaṃ ariyasāvikānaṃ satthārā daisitaniyāmaina yathāsutaṃ dhammaṃ kathaintī attānaṃ parimaucaitvā satthu santikai sutabhāvaṃ pakāsaintī ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā vuttamarahatāti mai suta’’nti nidānaṃ āraupaisi|

yasmā pana tasmiṃyaiva nagarai bhagavatau sammukhā sutvā tadahaiva tāya tāsaṃ bhāsitaṃ, tasmā ‘‘aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharatī’’ti kāladaisaṃ apadisituṃ payaujanasambhavauva natthi supākaṭabhāvatau| bhikkhuniyau cassā santikai imāni suttāni gaṇhiṃsu| aivaṃ paramparāya bhikkhūsupi tāya āraupitaṃ nidānaṃ pākaṭaṃ ahausi| atha āyasmā ānandau tathāgatassa parinibbānatau aparabhāgai sattapaṇṇiguhāyaṃ ajātasattunā kārāpitai saddhammamaṇḍapai mahākassapappamukhassa  vasīgaṇassa majjhai nisīditvā dhammaṃ saṅgāyantau imaisaṃ suttānaṃ nidānassa  dvaiḷhakaṃ pariharantau tāya āraupitaniyāmainaiva nidānaṃ āraupaisīti|

kaici panaittha bahuppakārai papañ‍cainti| kiṃ taihi? apica nānānayaihi saṅgītikārā dhammavinayaṃ saṅgāyiṃsu| anubuddhā hi dhammasaṅgāhakamahāthairā, tai sammadaiva dhammavinayassa saṅgāyanākāraṃ jānantā katthaci ‘‘aivaṃ mai suta’’ntiādinā, katthaci ‘‘taina samayainā’’tiādinā, katthaci gāthābandhavasaina nidānaṃ ṭhapaintā, katthaci sabbaina sabbaṃ nidānaṃ aṭṭhapaintā vaggasaṅgahādivasaina dhammavinayaṃ saṅgāyiṃsu| tattha idha vuttañhaitantiādinā nidānaṃ ṭhapaitvā saṅgāyiṃsu, kiñ‍ci suttagaiyyādivasaina navaṅgamidaṃ buddhavacanaṃ| yathā caitaṃ, aivaṃ sabbaisampi sammāsambuddhānaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ ‘‘appakañ‍ca naisaṃ ahausi suttaṃ gaiyya’’ntiādi| tattha itivuttakaṅgassa  añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci na pañ‍ñāyati tabbhāvanimittaṃ ṭhapaitvā ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ…pai॰… mai suta’’nti idaṃ vacanaṃ| taināhu aṭṭhakathācariyā ‘‘vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatāti ādinayappavattā dvādasuttarasatasuttantā itivuttaka’’nti| tasmā satthu adhippāyaṃ jānantaihi dhammasaṅgāhakaihi ariyasāvikāya vā imaisaṃ suttānaṃ itivuttakaṅgabhāvañāpanatthaṃ imināva nayaina nidānaṃ ṭhapitanti vaiditabbaṃ|

kimatthaṃ pana dhammavinayasaṅgahai kayiramānai nidānavacanaṃ? nanu bhagavatā bhāsitavacanassaiva saṅgahau kātabbauti? vuc‍catai – daisanāya ṭhitiasammausasaddhaiyyabhāvasampādanatthaṃ| kāladaisadaisakaparisāpadaisaihi upanibandhitvā ṭhapitā hi daisanā ciraṭṭhitikā hauti asammausadhammā saddhaiyyā ca daisakālakattuhaitunimittaihi upanibaddhau viya vauhāravinicchayau| tainaiva ca āyasmatā mahākassapaina brahmajālamūlapariyāyasuttādīnaṃ daisādipucchāsu katāsu tāsaṃ vissaj‍janaṃ karauntaina dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaina ‘‘aivaṃ mai suta’’ntiādinā nidānaṃ bhāsitaṃ| idha pana daisakālassa aggahaṇai kāraṇaṃ vuttamaiva|

apica satthu sampattippakāsanatthaṃ nidānavacanaṃ| tathāgatassa hi bhagavatau pubbaracanānumānāgamatak‍kābhāvatau sammāsambuddhabhāvasiddhi| na hi sammāsambuddhassa  pubbaracanādīhi atthau atthi sabbattha appaṭihatañāṇācāratāya aikappamāṇattā ca ñaiyyadhammaisu| tathā ācariyamuṭṭhidhammamacchariyasāsanasāvakānurāgābhāvatau khīṇāsavabhāvasiddhi| na hi sabbasau khīṇāsavassa tai sambhavantīti suvisuddhassa parānuggahapavatti| aivaṃ daisakasaṃkilaisabhūtānaṃ diṭṭhisīlasampadādūsakānaṃ avij‍jātaṇhānaṃ ac‍cantābhāvasaṃsūcakaihi ñāṇasampadāpahānasampadābhibyañ‍jakaihi  ca sambuddhavisuddhabhāvaihi purimavaisāraj‍jadvayasiddhi, tatau ca antarāyikaniyyānikadhammaisu asammauhabhāvasiddhitau pacchimavaisāraj‍jadvayasiddhīti bhagavatau catuvaisāraj‍jasaman‍nāgamau attahitaparahitapaṭipatti ca nidānavacanaina  pakāsitā hauti, tattha tattha sampattaparisāya ajjhāsayānurūpaṃ ṭhānuppattikappaṭibhānaina dhammadaisanādīpanatau| idha pana anavasaisatau kāmadausappahānaṃ vidhāya daisanādīpanatau cāti yaujaitabbaṃ| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘satthu sampattippakāsanatthaṃ nidānavacana’’nti| aittha ca ‘‘bhagavatā arahatā’’ti imaihi padaihi yathāvuttaatthavibhāvanatā haiṭṭhā dassitā aiva|

tathā sāsanasampattippakāsanatthaṃ nidānavacanaṃ| ñāṇakaruṇāpariggahitasabbakiriyassa hi bhagavatau natthi niratthakā paṭipatti attahitā vā| tasmā paraisaṃyaivatthāya pavattasabbakiriyassa sammāsambuddhassa sakalampi kāyavacīmanaukammaṃ yathāpavattaṃ vuc‍camānaṃ diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaparamatthaihi yathārahaṃ sattānaṃ anusāsanatthaina sāsanaṃ, na kabbaracanā| tayidaṃ satthu caritaṃ kāladaisadaisakaparisāpadaisaihi tattha tattha nidānavacanaihi yathārahaṃ pakāsiyati| idha pana daisakaparisāpadaisaihīti yaujaitabbaṃ| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sāsanasampattippakāsanatthaṃ nidānavacana’’nti|

apica satthunau pamāṇabhāvappakāsanaina sāsanassa pamāṇabhāvadassanatthaṃ nidānavacanaṃ| tañ‍cassa pamāṇabhāvadassanaṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayānusāraina ‘‘bhagavatā arahatā’’ti imaihi padaihi vibhāvitanti vaiditabbaṃ| idamaittha nidānavacanappayaujanassa mukhamattanidassananti|

nidānavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

1. aikakanipātau

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. laubhasuttavaṇṇanā

1. idāni  aikadhammaṃ, bhikkhavai, pajahathātiādinā nayaina bhagavatā nikkhittassa suttassa vaṇṇanāya aukāsau anuppattau| sā panaisā atthavaṇṇanā yasmā suttanikkhaipaṃ vicāraitvā vuc‍camānā pākaṭā hauti, tasmā suttanikkhaipaṃ tāva vicāraissāma| cattārau hi suttanikkhaipā – attajjhāsayau, parajjhāsayau, pucchāvasikau, aṭṭhuppattikauti| yathā hi anaikasataanaikasahassabhaidānipi suttantāni saṃkilaisabhāgiyādipaṭṭhānanayaina sauḷasavidhataṃ nātivattanti, aivaṃ attajjhāsayādisuttanikkhaipavasaina catubbidhataṃ nātivattantīti| tattha yathā attajjhāsayassa aṭṭhuppattiyā ca parajjhāsayapucchāvasikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsaggabhaidau sambhavati attajjhāsayau ca parajjhāsayau ca, attajjhāsayau ca pucchāvasikau ca, aṭṭhuppattikau ca parajjhāsayau ca, aṭṭhuppattikau ca pucchāvasikau cāti ajjhāsayapucchānusandhisambhavatau; aivaṃ yadipi aṭṭhuppattiyā attajjhāsayainapi saṃsaggabhaidau sambhavati, attajjhāsayādīhi pana puratau ṭhitaihi aṭṭhuppattiyā saṃsaggau natthīti niravasaisau paṭṭhānanayau na sambhavati| tadantaugadhattā vā sambhavantānaṃ saisanikkhaipānaṃ mūlanikkhaipavasaina cattārau suttanikkhaipā vuttāti vaiditabbaṃ|

tatrāyaṃ vacanatthau – nikkhipīyatīti nikkhaipau, suttaṃ aiva nikkhaipau suttanikkhaipau| atha vā nikkhipanaṃ nikkhaipau, suttassa nikkhaipau suttanikkhaipau, suttadaisanāti atthau| attanau ajjhāsayau attajjhāsayau, sau assa atthi kāraṇabhūtauti attajjhāsayau, attanau ajjhāsayau aitassāti vā attajjhāsayau| parajjhāsayaipi aisaiva nayau| pucchāya vasauti pucchāvasau| sau aitassa atthīti pucchāvasikau| suttadaisanāya vatthubhūtassa atthassa uppatti atthuppatti, atthuppatti aiva aṭṭhuppatti tha-kārassa ṭha-kāraṃ katvā, sā aitassa atthīti aṭṭhuppattikau| atha vā nikkhipīyati suttaṃ aitaināti nikkhaipau, attajjhāsayādi aiva| aitasmiṃ pana atthavikappai attanau ajjhāsayau attajjhāsayau| paraisaṃ ajjhāsayau parajjhāsayau| pucchīyatīti pucchā, pucchitabbau atthau, pucchāvasaina pavattaṃ dhammappaṭiggāhakānaṃ vacanaṃ pucchāvasaṃ, tadaiva nikkhaipasaddāpaikkhāya pucchāvasikauti pul‍liṅgavasaina vuttaṃ| tathā aṭṭhuppatti aiva aṭṭhuppattikauti aivamaittha atthau vaiditabbau|

apica paraisaṃ indriyaparipākādikāraṇanirapaikkhattā attajjhāsayassa visuṃ suttanikkhaipabhāvau yuttau, kaivalaṃ attanau ajjhāsayainaiva dhammatantiṭhapanatthaṃ pavattitadaisanattā| parajjhāsayapucchāvasikānaṃ pana paraisaṃ ajjhāsayapucchānaṃ daisanāpavattihaitubhūtānaṃ uppattiyaṃ pavattitānaṃ kathaṃ aṭṭhuppattiyaṃ anavaraudhau, pucchāvasikaṭṭhuppattikānaṃ vā parajjhāsayānuraudhaina pavattitānaṃ kathaṃ parajjhāsayai anavaraudhauti? na caudaitabbamaitaṃ| paraisañhi abhinīhāraparipucchādivinimuttassaiva suttadaisanākāraṇuppādassa aṭṭhuppattibhāvaina gahitattā parajjhāsayapucchāvasikānaṃ visuṃ gahaṇaṃ| tathā hi brahmajāladhammadāyādasuttādīnaṃ (dī॰ na॰ 1.1 ādayau) vaṇṇāvaṇṇaāmisuppādādidaisanānimittaṃ aṭṭhuppatti vuc‍cati| paraisaṃ pucchaṃ vinā ajjhāsayamaiva nimittaṃ katvā daisitau parajjhāsayau, pucchāvasaina daisitau pucchāvasikauti pākaṭauyamatthauti|

yāni bhagavā paraihi anajjhiṭṭhau kaivalaṃ attanau ajjhāsayainaiva kathaiti, saiyyathidaṃ – ākaṅkhaiyyasuttaṃ, tuvaṭṭakasuttantiaivamādīni (su॰ ni॰ 921 ādayau; ma॰ ni॰ 1.64 ādayau), taisaṃ attajjhāsayau nikkhaipau|

yāni pana ‘‘paripak‍kā khau rāhulassa vimuttiparipācanīyā dhammā, yaṃnūnāhaṃ rāhulaṃ uttariṃ āsavānaṃ khayai vinaiyya’’nti aivaṃ paraisaṃ ajjhāsayaṃ khantiṃ abhinīhāraṃ bujjhanabhāvañ‍ca aulaukaitvā parajjhāsayavasaina kathitāni, saiyyathidaṃ – rāhulauvādasuttaṃ, dhammacak‍kappavattanasuttantiaivamādīni (ma॰ ni॰ 2.107 ādayau; 3.416 ādayau; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.59; mahāva॰ 19-20), taisaṃ parajjhāsayau nikkhaipau|

bhagavantaṃ pana  upasaṅkamitvā daivā manussā catassau parisā cattārau vaṇṇā ca tathā tathā pañhaṃ pucchanti ‘‘baujjhaṅgā baujjhaṅgāti, bhantai, vuc‍canti, nīvaraṇā  nīvaraṇāti vuc‍cantī’’tiādinā, aivaṃ puṭṭhaina bhagavatā yāni kathitāni baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttādīni (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.186) taisaṃ pucchāvasikau nikkhaipau|

yāni pana tāni uppan‍naṃ kāraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca kathitāni, saiyyathidaṃ – dhammadāyādaṃ, puttamaṃsūpamaṃ, dārukkhandhūpamantiaivamādīni (ma॰ ni॰ 1.29; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.63), taisaṃ aṭṭhuppattikau nikkhaipau|

aivamimaisu catūsu suttanikkhaipaisu imassa suttassa parajjhāsayau nikkhaipau| parajjhāsayavasaina haitaṃ nikkhittaṃ| kaisaṃ ajjhāsayaina? laubhai ādīnavadassīnaṃ puggalānaṃ| kaici pana ‘‘attajjhāsayau’’ti vadanti|

tattha aikadhammaṃ, bhikkhavaitiādīsu aikasaddau atthaiva añ‍ñatthai ‘‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti itthaikai abhivadantī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 3.27)| atthi saiṭṭhai ‘‘caitasau aikaudibhāva’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.228; pārā॰ 11)| atthi asahāyai ‘‘aikau vūpakaṭṭhau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.405)| atthi saṅkhāyaṃ ‘‘aikauva khau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau ca samayau ca brahmacariyavāsāyā’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 8.29)| idhāpi saṅkhāyamaiva daṭṭhabbau|

dhamma-saddau pariyattisac‍casamādhipañ‍ñāpakatipuñ‍ñāpattisuñ‍ñatāñaiyyasabhāvādīsu dissati| tathā hissa ‘‘idha bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇātī’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 5.73) pariyatti atthau| ‘‘diṭṭhadhammau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.299) sac‍cāni| ‘‘aivaṃdhammā tai bhagavantau ahaisu’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.13; 3.142) samādhi| ‘‘sac‍caṃ dhammau dhiti cāgau, savai paic‍ca na saucatī’’tiādīsu (jā॰ 1.1.57) pañ‍ñā| ‘‘jātidhammānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jatī’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.398) pakati| ‘‘dhammau havai rakkhati dhammacāri’’ntiādīsu  (jā॰ 1.10.102) puñ‍ñaṃ| ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ añ‍ñataraina vadaiyya pārājikaina vā saṅghādisaisaina vā pācittiyaina vā’’tiādīsu (pārā॰ 444) āpatti| ‘‘tasmiṃ khau pana samayai dhammā hauntī’’tiādīsu (dha॰ sa॰ 121) suñ‍ñatā| ‘‘sabbai dhammā sabbākāraina buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇamukhai āpāthaṃ āgacchantī’’tiādīsu (mahāni॰ 156; cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 85) ñaiyyau| ‘‘kusalā dhammā akusalā dhammā’’tiādīsu (dha॰ sa॰ tikamātikā 1) sabhāvau atthau| idhāpi sabhāvau| tasmā  aikadhammanti aikaṃ saṃkilaisasabhāvanti adhippāyau| aikau ca sau dhammau cāti aikadhammau, taṃ aikadhammaṃ|

bhikkhavaiti bhikkhū ālapati| kimatthaṃ pana bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaintau bhikkhū ālapati, na dhammamaiva daisaitīti? satijananatthaṃ| bhikkhū hi añ‍ñaṃ cintaintāpi dhammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhantāpi kammaṭṭhānaṃ manasi karauntāpi nisin‍nā haunti| tai paṭhamaṃ anālapitvā dhammai daisiyamānai ‘‘ayaṃ daisanā kiṃnidānā, kiṃpac‍cayā’’ti sal‍lakkhaituṃ na sak‍kaunti| ālapitai pana satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā sal‍lakkhaituṃ sak‍kaunti, tasmā satijananatthaṃ ‘‘bhikkhavai’’ti ālapati| taina ca taisaṃ bhikkhanasīlatādiguṇayaugasiddhaina vacanaina hīnādhikajanasaivitaṃ vuttiṃ pakāsaintau uddhatadīnabhāvaniggahaṃ karauti| ‘‘bhikkhavai’’ti iminā karuṇāvipphārasaummahadayanayananipātapubbaṅgamaina vacanaina tai attanau mukhābhimukhai karauntau taina ca kathaitukamyatādīpakaina vacanaina naisaṃ sautukamyataṃ janaiti| tainaiva ca sambaudhanatthaina sādhukaṃ savanamanasikāraipi niyaujaiti| sādhukaṃ savanamanasikārāyattā hi sāsanasampatti|

añ‍ñaisupi daivamanussaisu parisapariyāpan‍naisu vij‍jamānaisu kasmā bhikkhū aiva āmantaisīti? jaiṭṭhasaiṭṭhāsan‍nasadāsan‍nihitabhāvatau| sabbaparisasādhāraṇā hi bhagavatau dhammadaisanā, parisāya ca jaiṭṭhā bhikkhū paṭhamuppan‍nattā, saiṭṭhā anagāriyabhāvaṃ ādiṃ katvā satthu cariyānuvidhāyakattā sakalasāsanapaṭiggāhakattā ca, āsan‍nā tattha nisin‍naisu samīpavuttiyā, sadāsan‍nihitā satthusantikāvacarattā| apica tai dhammadaisanāya bhājanaṃ  yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipattisabbhāvatau, visaisatau ca aikac‍cai bhikkhū sandhāya ayaṃ daisanāti tai aiva ālapi|

pajahathāti aittha pahānaṃ nāma tadaṅgappahānaṃ, vikkhambhanappahānaṃ, samucchaidappahānaṃ, paṭippassaddhippahānaṃ, nissaraṇappahānanti pañ‍cavidhaṃ| tattha yaṃ dīpālaukainaiva tamassa paṭipakkhabhāvatau alaubhādīhi laubhādikassa, nāmarūpaparicchaidādivipassanāñāṇaihi tassa tassa anatthassa pahānaṃ| saiyyathidaṃ – paric‍cāgaina laubhādimalassa, sīlaina pāṇātipātādidussīlyassa, saddhādīhi assaddhiyādikassa, nāmarūpavavatthānaina sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā, pac‍cayapariggahaina ahaituvisamahaitudiṭṭhīnaṃ, tassaiva aparabhāgaina kaṅkhāvitaraṇaina kathaṃkathībhāvassa, kalāpasammasanaina ‘‘ahaṃ  mamā’’ti gāhassa, maggāmaggavavatthānaina amaggai maggasañ‍ñāya, udayadassanaina ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā, vayadassanaina sassatadiṭṭhiyā, bhayadassanaina sabhayaisu abhayasañ‍ñāya, ādīnavadassanaina assādasañ‍ñāya, nibbidānupassanaina abhiratisañ‍ñāya, muc‍citukamyatāñāṇaina  amuc‍citukamyatāya upaikkhāñāṇaina anupaikkhāya, anulaumaina dhammaṭṭhitiyā, nibbānaina paṭilaumabhāvassa, gautrabhunā saṅkhāranimittaggāhassa pahānaṃ, aitaṃ tadaṅgappahānaṃ nāma|

yaṃ pana upacārappanābhaidaina samādhinā pavattibhāvanivāraṇatau ghaṭappahārainaiva udakapiṭṭhai saivālassa taisaṃ taisaṃ nīvaraṇādidhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, aitaṃ vikkhambhanappahānaṃ nāma| yaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyamaggānaṃ bhāvitattā taṃtaṃmaggavatau attanau santānai ‘‘diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāyā’’tiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 277; vibha॰ 628) nayaina vuttassa samudayapakkhiyassa kilaisagaṇassa ac‍cantaṃ appavattibhāvaina samucchindanaṃ, idaṃ samucchaidappahānaṃ nāma| yaṃ pana phalakkhaṇai paṭippassaddhattaṃ kilaisānaṃ, aitaṃ paṭippassaddhippahānaṃ nāma| yaṃ pana sabbasaṅkhatanissaṭattā pahīnasabbasaṅkhataṃ nibbānaṃ, aitaṃ nissaraṇappahānaṃ nāma| aivaṃ pañ‍cavidhai pahānai anāgāmikabhāvakarassa pahānassa adhippaitattā idha samucchaidappahānanti vaiditabbaṃ| tasmā pajahathāti paric‍cajatha, samucchindathāti atthau|

ahanti bhagavā attānaṃ niddisati| vauti ayaṃ vausaddau pac‍cattaupayaugakaraṇasāmivacanapadapūraṇasampadānaisu  dissati| tathā hi ‘‘kac‍ci, pana vau anuruddhā, samaggā sammaudamānā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.326) pac‍cattai āgatau| ‘‘gacchatha, bhikkhavai, paṇāmaimi vau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.157) upayaugai| ‘‘na vau mama santikai vatthabba’’ntiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.157) karaṇai| ‘‘sabbaisaṃ vau, sāriputta, subhāsita’’ntiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.345) sāmivacanai| ‘‘yai hi vau ariyā parisuddhakāyakammantā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.35) padapūraṇai| ‘‘vanapatthapariyāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.190) sampadānai| idhāpi sampadānai aiva daṭṭhabbau|

pāṭibhaugauti paṭibhū| sau hi dhāraṇakaṃ paṭic‍ca dhanikassa, dhanikaṃ paṭic‍ca dhāraṇakassa paṭinidhibhūtau dhanikasantakassa tatau haraṇādisaṅkhātaina bhuñ‍janaina  bhaugauti paṭibhaugau, paṭibhaugau aiva pāṭibhaugau| anāgāmitāyāti anāgāmibhāvatthāya| paṭisandhiggahaṇavasaina hi kāmabhavassa anāgamanatau anāgāmī| yau yassa dhammassa adhigamaina anāgāmīti vuc‍cati, saphalau sau tatiyamaggau anāgāmitā nāma| iti bhagavā vainaiyyadamanakusalau vainaiyyajjhāsayānukūlaṃ tatiyamaggādhigamaṃ lahunā upāyaina aikadhammapūraṇatāmattaina thiraṃ katvā dassaisi  yathā taṃ sammāsambuddhau| bhin‍nabhūmikāpi hi paṭighasaṃyaujanādayau tatiyamaggavajjhā kilaisā kāmarāgappahānaṃ nātivattantīti|

kasmā panaittha bhagavā attānaṃ pāṭibhaugabhāvai ṭhapaisi? taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anāgāmimaggādhigamāya ussāhajananatthaṃ| passati hi bhagavā ‘‘mayā ‘aikadhammaṃ, bhikkhavai, pajahatha, ahaṃ vau pāṭibhaugau anāgāmitāyā’ti vuttai imai bhikkhū addhā taṃ aikadhammaṃ pahāya sak‍kā tatiyabhūmiṃ samadhigantuṃ, yatau dhammassāmi paṭhamamāha ‘ahaṃ pāṭibhaugau’ti ussāhajātā tadatthāya paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissantī’’ti| tasmā ussāhajananatthaṃ anāgāmitāya taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ attānaṃ pāṭibhaugabhāvai ṭhapaisi|

katamaṃ aikadhammanti aittha katamanti pucchāvacanaṃ| pucchā ca nāmaisā pañ‍cavidhā – adiṭṭhajautanāpucchā, diṭṭhasaṃsandanāpucchā, vimaticchaidanāpucchā, anumatipucchā, kathaitukamyatāpucchāti| tattha pakatiyā lakkhaṇaṃ añ‍ñātaṃ hauti adiṭṭhaṃ atulitaṃ atīritaṃ avibhūtaṃ avibhāvitaṃ, tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya tulanāya tīraṇāya vibhūtatthāya vibhāvanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati, ayaṃ adiṭṭhajautanāpucchā| pakatiyā lakkhaṇaṃ ñātaṃ hauti diṭṭhaṃ tulitaṃ tīritaṃ vibhūtaṃ vibhāvitaṃ| sau añ‍ñaihi paṇḍitaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati, ayaṃ diṭṭhasaṃsandanāpucchā| pakatiyā saṃsayapakkhandau hauti vimatipakkhandau dvaiḷhakajātau – ‘‘aivaṃ nu khau, na nu khau, kiṃ nu khau, kathaṃ nu khau’’ti, sau vimaticchaidanatthāya pañhaṃ pucchati, ayaṃ vimaticchaidanāpucchā| bhagavā hi anumatiggahaṇatthaṃ pañhaṃ pucchati – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’tiādinā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.59; mahāva॰ 21), ayaṃ anumatipucchā| bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ kathaitukamyatāya pañhaṃ pucchati – ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, āhārā bhūtānaṃ  vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya| katamai cattārau’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.11) ayaṃ kathaitukamyatāpucchā|

tattha purimā tissau pucchā buddhānaṃ natthi| kasmā? tīsu hi addhāsu kiñ‍ci saṅkhataṃ addhāvimuttaṃ vā asaṅkhataṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ atulitaṃ atīritaṃ avibhūtaṃ avibhāvitaṃ nāma natthi| taina naisaṃ adiṭṭhajautanāpucchā natthi| yaṃ pana taihi attanau ñāṇaina paṭividdhaṃ, tassa añ‍ñaina samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā saddhiṃ saṃsandanakic‍caṃ natthi, taina naisaṃ diṭṭhasaṃsandanāpucchāpi natthi| yasmā pana buddhā bhagavantau akathaṃkathī  tiṇṇavicikicchā sabbadhammaisu vigatasaṃsayā, taina naisaṃ vimaticchaidanāpucchāpi natthi| itarā pana dvai pucchā atthi, tāsu ayaṃ kathaitukamyatāpucchāti vaiditabbā|

idāni tāya pucchāya puṭṭhamatthaṃ sarūpatau dassaintau ‘‘laubhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikadhamma’’ntiādimāha| tattha lubbhanti taina, sayaṃ vā lubbhati, lubbhanamattamaiva vā tanti laubhau| svāyaṃ ārammaṇaggahaṇalakkhaṇau mak‍kaṭālaipau viya, abhisaṅgarasau tattakapālai pakkhittamaṃsapaisi  viya, aparic‍cāgapac‍cupaṭṭhānau tailañ‍janarāgau viya, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu assādadassanapadaṭṭhānau, taṇhānadibhāvaina vaḍḍhamānau yattha samuppan‍nau, sīghasautā nadī viya mahāsamuddaṃ apāyamaiva taṃ sattaṃ gahaitvā gacchatīti daṭṭhabbau| kiñ‍cāpi ayaṃ laubhasaddau sabbalaubhasāmañ‍ñavacanau, idha pana kāmarāgavacanauti vaiditabbau| sau hi anāgāmimaggavajjhau|

puna bhikkhavaiti ālapanaṃ dhammassa paṭiggāhakabhāvaina abhimukhībhūtānaṃ tattha ādarajananatthaṃ| pajahathāti iminā pahānābhisamayau vihitau, sau ca pariñ‍ñāsacchikiriyābhāvanābhisamayaihi saddhiṃ aiva pavattati, na visunti catusac‍cādhiṭṭhānāni cattāripi sammādiṭṭhiyā kic‍cāni vihitānaiva haunti| yathā ca ‘‘laubhaṃ pajahathā’’ti vuttai pahānaikaṭṭhabhāvatau dausādīnampi pahānaṃ atthatau vuttamaiva hauti, aivaṃ samudayasac‍cavisayai sammādiṭṭhikic‍cai pahānābhisamayai vuttai tassā sahakārīkāraṇabhūtānaṃ sammāsaṅkappādīnaṃ saisamaggaṅgānampi samudayasac‍cavisayakic‍caṃ atthatau vuttamaiva hautīti paripuṇṇau ariyamaggabyāpārau idha  kathitauti daṭṭhabbau| iminā nayaina satipaṭṭhānādīnampi baudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ byāpārassa idha vuttabhāvau yathārahaṃ vitthāraitabbau|

apicaittha laubhaṃ pajahathāti aitaina pahānapariñ‍ñā vuttā| sā ca tīraṇapariñ‍ñādhiṭṭhānā, tīraṇapariñ‍ñā ca ñātapariñ‍ñādhiṭṭhānāti avinābhāvaina tissaupi pariñ‍ñā baudhitā haunti| aivamaittha saha phalaina catusac‍cakammaṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ katvā pakāsitanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā laubhaṃ pajahathāti saha phalaina ñāṇadassanavisuddhi daisitā| sā ca paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhisan‍nissayā…pai॰… cittavisuddhisīlavisuddhisan‍nissayā cāti nānantarikabhāvaina saha phalaina sabbāpi satta visuddhiyau vibhāvitāti vaiditabbaṃ|

aivamaitāya visuddhik‍kamabhāvanāya pariñ‍ñāttayasampādanaina laubhaṃ pajahitukāmaina –

‘‘anatthajananau  laubhau, laubhau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhati||

‘‘luddhau atthaṃ na jānāti, luddhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhatamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ laubhau sahatai naraṃ’’|| (itivu॰ 88)|

rattau  khau, āvusau, rāgaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau pāṇampi hanati, adin‍nampi ādiyati, sandhimpi chindati, nil‍laupampi harati, aikāgārikampi karauti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati, paradārampi gacchati, musāpi bhaṇati| tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ avaidayataṃ taṇhānugatānaṃ paritassitaṃ vipphanditamaiva (a॰ ni॰ 3.54)|

‘‘taṇhādutiyau purisau, dīghamaddhāna saṃsaraṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, saṃsāraṃ nātivattati’’|| (itivu॰ 15, 105)|

‘‘natthi rāgasamau aggi, natthi dausasamau kali’’|| (dha॰ pa॰ 202, 251)|

‘‘kāmarāgaina ḍayhāmi, cittaṃ mai pariḍayhati’’|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.212)|

‘‘yai rāgarattānupatanti sautaṃ, sayaṃkataṃ mak‍kaṭakauva jāla’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 347) ca –

aivamādisuttapadānusāraina  nānānayaihi laubhassa ādīnavaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā tassa pahānāya paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

apica cha dhammā kāmarāgassa pahānāya saṃvattanti, asubhanimittassa uggahau, asubhabhāvanānuyaugau, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, kalyāṇamittatā, sappāyakathāti| dasavidhañhi asubhanimittaṃ uggaṇhantassāpi kāmarāgau pahīyati, kāyagatāsatibhāvanāvasaina saviñ‍ñāṇakai uddhumātakādivasaina aviñ‍ñāṇakai asubhai asubhabhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttassāpi, manacchaṭṭhaisu indriyaisu saṃvaraṇavasaina satikavāṭaina pihitadvārassāpi, catun‍naṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ vā ālaupānaṃ aukāsai sati udakaṃ pivitvā yāpanasīlatāya bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunaupi| tainaivāha –

‘‘cattārau pañ‍ca ālaupai, abhutvā udakaṃ pivai|

alaṃ phāsuvihārāya, pahitattassa bhikkhunau’’ti|| (thairagā॰ 983)|

asubhakammaṭṭhānabhāvanāratai kalyāṇamittai saivantassāpi, ṭhānanisaj‍jādīsu dasaasubhanissitasappāyakathāyapi pahīyati| tainaivāha –

‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, asubhanimittaṃ, tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau, ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa anuppādāya uppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa pahānāyā’’ti|

aivaṃ pubbabhāgai kāmarāgasaṅkhātassa  laubhassa pahānāya paṭipan‍nau vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā tatiyamaggaina taṃ anavasaisatau samucchindati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘laubhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikadhammaṃ pajahatha, ahaṃ vau pāṭibhaugau anāgāmitāyā’’ti|

aitthāha ‘‘kau panaittha laubhau pahīyati, kiṃ atītau, atha anāgatau, udāhu pac‍cuppan‍nau’’ti? kiñ‍caittha – na tāva atītau laubhau pahīyaiyya, na anāgatau vā taisaṃ abhāvatau| na hi niruddhaṃ anuppan‍naṃ vā atthīti vuc‍cati, vāyāmau ca aphalau āpaj‍jati| atha pac‍cuppan‍nau, aivampi aphalau vāyāmau tassa sarasabhaṅgattā, saṃkiliṭṭhā ca maggabhāvanā  āpaj‍jati, cittavippayuttau vā laubhau siyā, na cāyaṃ nayau icchitauti| vuc‍catai – na vuttanayaina atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nau laubhau pahīyati| saiyyathāpi idha taruṇarukkhau asañ‍jātaphalau, taṃ purisau kuṭhāriyā mūlai chindaiyya, tassa rukkhassa chaidai asati yāni phalāni nibbattaiyyuṃ, tāni rukkhassa chin‍nattā ajātāni aiva na jāyaiyyuṃ, aivamaiva ariyamaggādhigamai asati uppaj‍janārahau laubhau ariyamaggādhigamaina pac‍cayaghātassa katattā na uppaj‍jati| ayañhi aṭṭhakathāsu ‘‘bhūmiladdhuppan‍nau’’ti vuc‍cati| vipassanāya hi ārammaṇabhūtā pañ‍cakkhandhā tassa uppaj‍janaṭṭhānatāya bhūmi nāma| sā bhūmi taina laddhāti katvā bhūmiladdhuppan‍nau| ārammaṇādhiggahituppan‍nau avikkhambhituppan‍nau asamūhatuppan‍nauti ca ayamaiva vuc‍cati|

tatthāti  tasmiṃ suttai| aitanti aitaṃ atthajātaṃ| idāni gāthābandhavasaina vuc‍camānaṃ| iti vuc‍catīti kaina pana vuc‍cati? bhagavatā va| añ‍ñaisu hi tādisaisu ṭhānaisu saṅgītikāraihi upanibandhagāthā haunti, idha pana bhagavatā va gāthārucikānaṃ puggalānaṃ ajjhāsayavasaina vuttamaivatthaṃ saṅgahaitvā gāthā bhāsitā|

tattha yaina laubhaina luddhāsai, sattā gacchanti duggatinti yaina ārammaṇaggahaṇalakkhaṇaina tatau aiva abhisaṅgarasaina laubhaina luddhā ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu giddhā gadhitā| saiti hi nipātamattaṃ| akkharacintakā pana īdisaisu ṭhānaisu sai-kārāgamaṃ icchanti| tathā luddhattā aiva kāyasucaritādīsu kiñ‍ci sucaritaṃ akatvā kāyaduc‍caritādīni ca upacinitvā  rūpādīsu sattavisattatāya sattāti laddhanāmā pāṇinau dukkhassa nibbattiṭṭhānatāya duggatīti saṅkhaṃ gataṃ nirayaṃ tiracchānayauniṃ paittivisayañ‍ca paṭisandhiggahaṇavasaina gacchanti upapaj‍janti|

taṃ laubhaṃ sammadañ‍ñāya, pajahanti vipassinauti taṃ yathāvuttaṃ laubhaṃ sabhāvatau samudayatau atthaṅgamatau assādatau ādīnavatau nissaraṇatauti imaihi ākāraihi sammā aviparītaṃ haitunā ñāyaina añ‍ñāya ñātatīraṇapariñ‍ñāsaṅkhātāya pañ‍ñāya jānitvā rūpādikai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anic‍cādīhi vividhaihi ākāraihi passanatau vipassinau avasiṭṭhakilaisai vipassanāpañ‍ñāpubbaṅgamāya maggapañ‍ñāya samucchaidappahānavasaina pajahanti, na puna attanau santānai uppaj‍jituṃ dainti| pahāya  na punāyanti, imaṃ laukaṃ kudācananti aivaṃ sahajaikaṭṭhapahānaikaṭṭhaihi avasiṭṭhakilaisaihi saddhiṃ taṃ laubhaṃ anāgāmimaggaina pajahitvā puna pacchā imaṃ kāmadhātusaṅkhātaṃ laukaṃ paṭisandhiggahaṇavasaina kadācipi na āgacchanti aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ suppahīnattā| iti bhagavā anāgāmiphalaina daisanaṃ niṭṭhāpaisi|

ayampi atthauti nidānāvasānatau pabhuti yāva gāthāpariyausānā iminā suttaina pakāsitau atthau| api-saddau idāni vakkhamānasuttatthasampiṇḍanau| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva| imasmiṃ suttai samudayasac‍caṃ sarūpainaiva āgataṃ, pahānāpadaisaina maggasac‍caṃ| itaraṃ sac‍cadvayañ‍ca tadubhayahaitutāya niddhāraitabbaṃ| gāthāya pana dukkhasamudayamaggasac‍cāni yathārutavasainaiva ñāyanti, itaraṃ niddhāraitabbaṃ| aisaiva nayau itau paraisupi suttaisu|

paramatthadīpaniyā khuddakanikāya-aṭṭhakathāya

itivuttakavaṇṇanāya paṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. dausasuttavaṇṇanā

2. vuttañhaitaṃ  …pai॰… dausanti dutiyasuttaṃ| tatrāyaṃ apubbapadavaṇṇanā| yathā aittha, aivaṃ itau paraisupi sabbattha apubbapadavaṇṇanaṃyaiva karissāma| yasmā idaṃ suttaṃ dausabahulānaṃ puggalānaṃ ajjhāsayaṃ aulaukaitvā  dausavūpasamanatthaṃ daisitaṃ, tasmā ‘‘dausaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikadhammaṃ pajahathā’’ti āgataṃ| tattha dausanti ‘‘anatthaṃ mai acarīti āghātau jāyatī’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 960) nayaina suttai vuttānaṃ navan‍naṃ, ‘‘atthaṃ mai nācarī’’tiādīnañ‍ca tappaṭipakkhatau siddhānaṃ navan‍namaivāti aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ khāṇukaṇṭakādinā aṭṭhānaina saddhiṃ aikūnavīsatiyā añ‍ñatarāghātavatthusambhavaṃ āghātaṃ| sau hi dussanti taina, sayaṃ vā dussati, dussanamattamaiva vā tanti dausauti vuc‍cati| sau caṇḍik‍kalakkhaṇau pahaṭāsīvisau viya, visappanarasau visanipātau viya, attanau nissayadahanarasau vā dāvaggi viya, dussanapac‍cupaṭṭhānau  laddhaukāsau viya sapattau, yathāvuttaāghātavatthupadaṭṭhānau visasaṃsaṭṭhapūtimuttaṃ viya daṭṭhabbau| pajahathāti samucchindatha| tattha yai imai –
