‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, āghātapaṭivinayā, yattha bhikkhunau uppan‍nau āghātau sabbasau paṭivinaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalai āghātau jāyaitha, maittā tasmiṃ puggalai bhāvaitabbā…pai॰… karuṇā…pai॰… upaikkhā, asatiamanasikārau tasmiṃ puggalai āpaj‍jitabbau, aivaṃ tasmiṃ puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalai āghātau jāyaitha, kammassakatā tasmiṃ puggalai adhiṭṭhātabbā ‘kammassakau ayamāyasmā kammadāyādau…pai॰… bhavissatī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 5.161) –

aivaṃ pañ‍ca āghātappaṭivinayā vuttāyaiva|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, āghātapaṭivinayā, yattha bhikkhunau uppan‍nau āghātau sabbasau paṭivinaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? idhāvusau, aikac‍cau puggalau aparisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti parisuddhavacīsamācārau; aivarūpaipi, āvusau, puggalai āghātau paṭivinaitabbau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 5.162) –

aivamādināpi  nayaina pañ‍ca āghātapaṭivinayā vuttā, taisu yaina kainaci āghātapaṭivinayavidhinā pac‍cavaikkhitvā| apica yau –

‘‘ubhataudaṇḍakaina caipi, bhikkhavai, kakacaina caurā aucarakā aṅgamaṅgāni aukantaiyyuṃ, tatrāpi yau manau padūsaiyya, na mai sau taina sāsanakarau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.232) satthu auvādau|

‘‘tassaiva  taina pāpiyau, yau kuddhaṃ paṭikujjhati|

kuddhaṃ appaṭikujjhantau, saṅgāmaṃ jaiti duj‍jayaṃ||

‘‘ubhin‍namatthaṃ carati, attanau ca parassa ca|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammati|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.188)|

‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā sapattakantā sapattakaraṇā kaudhanaṃ āgacchanti itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā| katamai satta? idha, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati, ‘ahau vatāyaṃ dubbaṇṇau assā’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa vaṇṇavatāya nandati| kaudhanauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau kaudhābhibhūtau kaudhaparaitau kiñ‍cāpi sau hauti sunhātau suvilittau kappitakaisamassu audātavatthavasanau, atha khau sau dubbaṇṇauva hauti kaudhābhibhūtau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau sapattakantau sapattakaraṇau kaudhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati ‘ahau vatāyaṃ dukkhaṃ sayaiyyā’ti…pai॰… na pacuratthau assāti…pai॰… na bhaugavā assāti…pai॰… na yasavā assāti…pai॰… na mittavā assāti…pai॰… kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyāti| taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa sugatigamanai nandati| kaudhanauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau kaudhābhibhūtau kaudhaparaitau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati kaudhābhibhūtau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 7.64)|

‘‘kuddhau  atthaṃ na jānāti, kuddhau dhammaṃ na passati…pai॰…|| (a॰ ni॰ 7.64)|

‘‘kaudhaṃ jahai vippajahaiyya mānaṃ, saṃyaujanaṃ sabbamatik‍kamaiyya|| (dha॰ pa॰ 221)|

‘‘anatthajananau kaudhau, kaudhau cittappakaupanau…pai॰…|| (a॰ ni॰ 7.64)|

‘‘kaudhaṃ  chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kaudhaṃ chaitvā na saucati|

kaudhassa visamūlassa, madhuraggassa brāhmaṇā’’ti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.187)|

‘‘aikāparādhaṃ  khama bhūripañ‍ña,

na paṇḍitā kaudhabalā bhavantī’’ti|| –

aivamādinā nayaina dausai ādīnavai vuttappaṭipakkhatau dausappahānai ānisaṃsai ca pac‍cavaikkhitvā pubbabhāgai dausaṃ tadaṅgappahānādivasaina pajahitvā vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā tatiyamaggaina sabbasau dausaṃ samucchindatha, pajahathāti taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ tattha niyaujanaṃ| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘dausaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikadhammaṃ pajahathā’’ti| duṭṭhāsaiti āghātaina dūsitacittatāya paduṭṭhā| saisamaittha yaṃ vattabbaṃ, taṃ paṭhamasuttavaṇṇanāyaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

dutiyasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. mauhasuttavaṇṇanā

3. tatiyai mauhanti añ‍ñāṇaṃ| tañhi dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇantiādinā nayaina vibhāgaina anaikappabhaidampi muyhanti| taina sayaṃ vā muyhati muyhanamattamaiva vā tanti mauhauti vuc‍cati| sau cittassa andhabhāvalakkhaṇau, añ‍ñāṇalakkhaṇau vā, asampaṭivaidharasau, ārammaṇasabhāvacchādanarasau  vā, asammāppaṭipattipac‍cupaṭṭhānau, andhakārapac‍cupaṭṭhānau vā, ayaunisaumanasikārapadaṭṭhānau, sabbākusalānaṃ mūlanti daṭṭhabbau| idhāpi pajahathāti padassa –

‘‘mūḷhau atthaṃ na jānāti, mūḷhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhatamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ mauhau sahatai naraṃ’’|| (itivu॰ 88)|

‘‘anatthajananau mauhau…pai॰…|| (itivu॰ 88)|

‘‘avij‍jā, bhikkhavai, pubbaṅgamā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā’’ (itivu॰ 40)|

‘‘mauhasambandhanau  laukau, bhabbarūpauva dissati’’| (udā॰ 70)|

‘‘mauhau  nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya’’ (a॰ ni॰ 3.34)|

‘‘mūḷhau khau, brāhmaṇa, mauhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavatī’’ti ca –

ādinā nayaina ‘‘yau kauci dhammau kāmacchandādisaṃkilaisadhammaihi nibbattaitabbau, atthatau sabbau sau mauhahaitukau’’ti ca mauhai ādīnavaṃ tappaṭipakkhatau mauhappahānai ānisaṃsañ‍ca pac‍cavaikkhitvā kāmacchandādippahānak‍kamainaiva pubbabhāgai tadaṅgādivasaina mauhaṃ pajahantā tatiyamaggaina yathāvuttalaubhadausaikaṭṭhaṃ mauhaṃ samucchaidavasaina pajahathāti atthau daṭṭhabbau| anāgāmimaggavajjhau aiva hi mauhau idhādhippaitauti| mūḷhāsaiti kusalākusalasāvaj‍jānavaj‍jādibhaidai attanau hitāhitai sammūḷhā| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

tatiyasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. kaudhasuttavaṇṇanā

4. catutthai kaudhanti dausaṃ| dausau aiva hi kaudhapariyāyaina bujjhanakānaṃ puggalānaṃ ajjhāsayavasaina  aivaṃ vuttau| tasmā dutiyasuttai vuttanayainaivaittha atthau vaiditabbau| apica kujjhanalakkhaṇau kaudhau, āghātakaraṇarasau, cittassa byāpattibhāvapac‍cupaṭṭhānau, caitasau pūtibhāvauti daṭṭhabbauti ayampi visaisau vaiditabbau|

catutthasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. makkhasuttavaṇṇanā

5. pañ‍camai makkhanti paraguṇamakkhanaṃ| yadipi hi sau gūthaṃ gahaitvā paraṃ paharantau viya attanau karaṃ paṭhamataraṃ makkhatiyaiva, tathāpi paraisaṃ guṇamakkhanādhippāyaina pavattaitabbattā ‘‘paraguṇamakkhanau’’ti vuc‍cati| tathā hi  sau udakapuñchanamiva nhātassa sarīragataṃ udakaṃ paraisaṃ guṇai makkhaiti puñchati vināsaiti, paraihi vā katānaṃ mahantānampi kārānaṃ khaipanatau dhaṃsanatau makkhauti vuc‍cati| sau paraguṇamakkhanalakkhaṇau, taisaṃ vināsanarasau, tadavacchādanapac‍cupaṭṭhānau| atthatau pana paraisaṃ guṇamakkhanākāraina pavattau daumanassasahagatacittuppādauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| pajahathāti tattha vuttappabhaidaṃ dausaṃ, dausai ca vuttanayaṃ ādīnavaṃ, pahānai cassa ānisaṃsaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā pubbabhāgai tadaṅgādivasaina pajahantā vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā tatiyamaggaina anavasaisaṃ samucchindathāti atthau| makkhāsaiti makkhitā makkhitaparaguṇā, paraisaṃ guṇānaṃ makkhitārau, tatau aiva attanaupi dhaṃsitaguṇāti atthau| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

pañ‍camasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. mānasuttavaṇṇanā

6. chaṭṭhai mānanti jātiādivatthukaṃ caitasau un‍namanaṃ| sau hi ‘‘saiyyauhamasmī’’tiādinā nayaina mañ‍ñanti taina, sayaṃ vā mañ‍ñati, mānanaṃ sampaggahauti vā mānauti vuc‍cati| svāyaṃ saiyyauhamasmīti mānau, sadisauhamasmīti mānau, hīnauhamasmīti mānauti aivaṃ tividhau| puna saiyyassa saiyyauhamasmīti mānau, saiyyassa sadisau, saiyyassa hīnau; sadisassa saiyyau, sadisassa sadisau, sadisassa hīnau; hīnassa saiyyau, hīnassa sadisau, hīnassa hīnauhamasmīti mānauti aivaṃ navavidhaupi un‍natilakkhaṇau, ahaṃkārarasau, sampaggaharasau  vā, uddhumātabhāvapac‍cupaṭṭhānau, kaitukamyatāpac‍cupaṭṭhānau vā, diṭṭhivippayuttalaubhapadaṭṭhānau ummādau viyāti daṭṭhabbau| pajahathāti tassa sabbassapi attuk‍kaṃsanaparavambhananimittatā, garuṭṭhāniyaisu abhivādanapac‍cupaṭṭhānaañ‍jalikammasāmīcikammādīnaṃ akaraṇai kāraṇatā, jātimadapurisamadādibhāvaina pamādāpattihaitubhāvauti aivamādibhaidaṃ ādīnavaṃ tappaṭipakkhatau niratimānatāya ānisaṃsañ‍ca pac‍cavaikkhitvā rājasabhaṃ anuppattau caṇḍālau viya sabrahmacārīsu nīcacittataṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhapaitvā pubbabhāgai tadaṅgādivasaina taṃ  pajahantā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā anāgāmimaggaina samucchindathāti  atthau| anāgāmimaggavajjhau aiva hi mānau idhādhippaitau| mattāsaiti jātimadapurisamadādivasaina mānaina pamādāpattihaitubhūtaina mattā attānaṃ paggahaitvā carantā| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

imaisu pana paṭipāṭiyā chasu suttaisu gāthāsu vā anāgāmiphalaṃ pāpaitvā daisanā niṭṭhāpitā| tattha yai imai avihā atappā sudassā sudassī akaniṭṭhāti upapattibhavavasaina pañ‍ca anāgāminau, taisu avihaisu upapan‍nā avihā nāma| tai antarāparinibbāyī, upahac‍caparinibbāyī, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī, uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmīti pañ‍cavidhā, tathā atappā, sudassā, sudassinau| akaniṭṭhaisu pana uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī parihāyati| tattha yau avihādīsu uppaj‍jitvā āyuvaimajjhaṃ anatik‍kamitvā arahattappattiyā kilaisaparinibbānaina parinibbāyati, ayaṃ antarāparinibbāyī nāma| yau pana avihādīsu āditau pañ‍cakappasatādibhaidaṃ āyuvaimajjhaṃ atik‍kamitvā parinibbāyati, ayaṃ upahac‍caparinibbāyī nāma| yau asaṅkhāraina adhimattappayaugaṃ akatvā appadukkhaina akasiraina parinibbāyati, ayaṃ asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī nāma| yau pana sasaṅkhāraina adhimattappayaugaṃ katvā dukkhaina kicchaina kasiraina parinibbāyati, ayaṃ sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī nāma| itarau pana avihādīsu uddhaṃvāhitabhāvaina uddhamassa taṇhāsautaṃ, vaṭṭasautaṃ, maggasautamaiva vāti uddhaṃsautau| avihādīsu uppaj‍jitvā arahattaṃ pattuṃ asak‍kauntau tattha tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā paṭisandhiggahaṇavasaina akaniṭṭhaṃ gacchatīti akaniṭṭhagāmī|

aittha ca uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī, uddhaṃsautau na akaniṭṭhagāmī, na uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī, na uddhaṃsautau na akaniṭṭhagāmīti catuk‍kaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| kathaṃ? yau avihatau paṭṭhāya cattārau daivalaukai saudhaitvā akaniṭṭhaṃ gantvā parinibbāyati, ayaṃ uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī nāma| yau pana haiṭṭhā tayau daivalaukai saudhaitvā sudassīdaivalaukai ṭhatvā parinibbāyati, ayaṃ uddhaṃsautau na akaniṭṭhagāmī nāma| yau itau akaniṭṭhamaiva gantvā parinibbāyati, ayaṃ na uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī nāma| yau pana haiṭṭhā catūsu daivalaukaisu tattha tatthaiva parinibbāyati, ayaṃ na uddhaṃsautau, na akaniṭṭhagāmī nāmāti|

tattha  avihaisu uppaj‍jitvā kappasatatau uddhaṃ parinibbāyikau, dvin‍naṃ kappasatānaṃ matthakai parinibbāyikau, pañ‍cakappasatai asampattai parinibbāyikauti tayau antarāparinibbāyinau| vuttañhaitaṃ ‘‘upapan‍naṃ vā samanantarā appattaṃ vā vaimajjha’’nti (pu॰ pa॰ 36)| vā-saddaina hi pattamattaupi saṅgahitauti| aivaṃ tayau antarāparinibbāyinau, aikau upahac‍caparinibbāyī aikau uddhaṃsautau| taisu asaṅkhāraparinibbāyinau pañ‍ca, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyinau pañ‍cāti dasa haunti| tathā atappāsudassāsudassīsūti cattārau dasakā cattārīsaṃ akaniṭṭhai pana uddhaṃsautassa abhāvatau tayau antarāparinibbāyinau, aikau upahac‍caparinibbāyīti asaṅkhāraparinibbāyinau cattārau, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyinau cattārauti aṭṭha, aivamaitai aṭṭhacattārīsaṃ anāgāminau| tai sabbaipi imaisu suttaisu avisaisavacanaina gahitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

chaṭṭhasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. sabbapariñ‍ñāsuttavaṇṇanā

7. sattamai sabbanti anavasaisaṃ| anavasaisavācakau hi ayaṃ sabba-saddau| sau yaina yaina sambandhaṃ gacchati, tassa tassa anavasaisataṃ dīpaiti; yathā ‘‘sabbaṃ rūpaṃ, sabbā vaidanā, sabbasak‍kāyapariyāpan‍naisu dhammaisū’’ti| sau panāyaṃ sabba-saddau sappadaisanippadaisavisayatāya duvidhau| tathā haisa sabbasabbaṃ, padaisasabbaṃ, āyatanasabbaṃ, sak‍kāyasabbanti catūsu visayaisu diṭṭhappayaugau| tattha ‘‘sabbai dhammā sabbākāraina buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇamukhai āpāthamāgacchantī’’tiādīsu (cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 85) sabbasabbasmiṃ āgatau| ‘‘sabbaisaṃ vau, sāriputtā, subhāsitaṃ pariyāyainā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.345) padaisasabbasmiṃ| ‘‘sabbaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, cakkhuñ‍caiva rūpañ‍ca…pai॰…| manañ‍caiva dhammai cā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.23-25) aittha  āyatanasabbasmiṃ| ‘‘sabbadhammamūlapariyāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.1) sak‍kāyasabbasmiṃ| tattha sabbasabbasmiṃ āgatau nippadaisavisayau, itaraisu tīsupi āgatau sappadaisavisayau| idha pana sak‍kāyasabbasmiṃ vaiditabbau| vipassanāya ārammaṇabhūtā taibhūmakadhammā hi idha ‘‘sabba’’nti anavasaisatau gahitā|

anabhijānanti  ‘‘imai dhammā kusalā, imai akusalā, imai sāvaj‍jā, imai anavaj‍jā’’tiādinā ‘‘imai pañ‍cakkhandhā, imāni dvādasāyatanāni, imā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau, idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau ariyasac‍ca’’nti ca ādinā sabbai abhiñ‍ñaiyyai dhammai aviparītasabhāvatau anabhijānantau abhivisiṭṭhaina ñāṇaina na jānantau| aparijānanti na parijānantau| yau hi sabbaṃ taibhūmakadhammajātaṃ parijānāti, sau tīhi pariñ‍ñāhi parijānāti – ñātapariñ‍ñāya, tīraṇapariñ‍ñāya, pahānapariñ‍ñāya| tattha katamā ñātapariñ‍ñā? sabbaṃ taibhūmakaṃ nāmarūpaṃ – ‘‘idaṃ rūpaṃ, aittakaṃ rūpaṃ, na itau bhiyyau| idaṃ nāmaṃ, aittakaṃ nāmaṃ, na itau bhiyyau’’ti bhūtappasādādippabhaidaṃ rūpaṃ, phassādippabhaidaṃ nāmañ‍ca, lakkhaṇarasapac‍cupaṭṭhānapadaṭṭhānatau vavatthapaiti| tassa avij‍jādikañ‍ca pac‍cayaṃ pariggaṇhāti| ayaṃ ñātapariñ‍ñā| katamā tīraṇapariñ‍ñā? aivaṃ ñātaṃ katvā taṃ sabbaṃ tīraiti anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatauti dvācattālīsāya ākāraihi| ayaṃ tīraṇapariñ‍ñā| katamā pahānapariñ‍ñā? aivaṃ tīrayitvā aggamaggaina sabbasmiṃ chandarāgaṃ pajahati| ayaṃ pahānapariñ‍ñā|

diṭṭhivisuddhikaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhiyaupi ñātapariñ‍ñā| maggāmaggapaṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhiyau kalāpasammasanādianulaumapariyausānā vā pañ‍ñā tīraṇapariñ‍ñā| ariyamaggaina pajahanaṃ pahānapariñ‍ñā| yau sabbaṃ parijānāti, sau imāhi tīhi pariñ‍ñāhi parijānāti| idha pana virāgappahānānaṃ paṭikkhaipavasaina visuṃ gahitattā ñātapariñ‍ñāya tīraṇapariñ‍ñāya ca vasaina parijānanā vaiditabbā| yau panaivaṃ na parijānāti, taṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘aparijāna’’nti|

tattha cittaṃ avirājayanti tasmiṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyavisaisai pariñ‍ñaiyyai  attanau cittasantānaṃ na virājayaṃ, na viraj‍jantau; yathā tattha rāgau na hauti, aivaṃ virāgānupassanaṃ na uppādaintauti atthau| appajahanti vipassanāpañ‍ñāsahitāya maggapañ‍ñāya tattha pahātabbayuttakaṃ kilaisavaṭṭaṃ anavasaisatau  na pajahantau| yathā caitaṃ, aivaṃ abhijānanādayaupi missakamaggavasaina vaiditabbā| pubbabhāgai hi nānācittavasaina ñātatīraṇapahānapariñ‍ñāhi kamaina abhijānanādīni sampādaitvā maggakālai aikakkhaṇainaiva kic‍cavasaina taṃ sabbaṃ nipphādaintaṃ aikamaiva ñāṇaṃ pavattatīti| abhabbau dukkhakkhayāyāti nibbānāya sakalassa vaṭṭadukkhassa khaipanāya na bhabbau, nālaṃ na samatthauti atthau|

sabbañ‍ca khauti aittha ca-saddau byatiraikai, khau-saddau avadhāraṇai| tadubhayaina abhijānanāditau  laddhabbaṃ visaisaṃ dukkhakkhayassa ca aikantakāraṇaṃ dīpaiti| abhijānanādīsu yaṃ vattabbaṃ, taṃ vuttamaiva| tattha pana paṭikkhaipavasaina vuttaṃ, idha vidhānavasaina vaiditabbaṃ| ayamaiva visaisau| apica abhijānanti upādānakkhandhapañ‍cakasaṅkhātaṃ sak‍kāyasabbaṃ sarūpatau pac‍cayatau ca ñāṇassa abhimukhīkaraṇavasaina abhijānantau hutvā abhāvākārādipariggahaina taṃ anic‍cādilakkhaṇaihi paricchij‍jamānavasaina parijānantau| virājayanti sammadaivassa anic‍catādiavabaudhaina uppan‍nabhayādīnavanibbidādiñāṇānubhāvaina attanau cittaṃ virattaṃ karauntau tattha aṇumattampi rāgaṃ anuppādaintau| pajahanti vuṭṭhānagāminivipassanāsahitāya maggapañ‍ñāya samudayapakkhiyaṃ kilaisavaṭṭaṃ pajahantau samucchindantau| bhabbau dukkhakkhayāyāti aivaṃ kilaisamalappahānainaiva sabbassa kammavaṭṭassa parikkhīṇattā anavasaisavipākavaṭṭakhaipanāya sakalasaṃsāravaṭṭadukkhaparikkhayabhūtāya vā anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā bhabbau aikantainaitaṃ pāpuṇitunti aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau|

yau sabbaṃ sabbatau ñatvāti yau yuttayaugau āraddhavipassakau sabbaṃ taibhūmakadhammajātaṃ sabbatau sabbabhāgaina kusalādikkhandhādivibhāgatau dukkhādipīḷanādivibhāgatau ca| atha vā sabbatauti sabbasmā kakkhaḷaphusanādilakkhaṇāditau anic‍cāditau cāti sabbākāratau jānitvā vipassanāpubbaṅgamaina  maggañāṇaina paṭivijjhitvā, vipassanāñāṇainaiva vā jānanahaitu| sabbatthaisu na raj‍jatīti sabbaisu atītādivasaina anaikabhaidabhin‍naisu sak‍kāyadhammaisu na raj‍jati, ariyamaggādhigamaina rāgaṃ na janaiti| imināssa taṇhāgāhassa abhāvaṃ dassaintau taṃ nimittattā diṭṭhamānaggāhānaṃ ‘‘aitaṃ mama aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’ti imassa micchāgāhattayassapi  abhāvaṃ dassaiti| sa vaiti aittha sa-iti nipātamattaṃ| vai-ti byattaṃ, aikaṃsaināti vā aitasmiṃ atthai nipātau| sabbapariñ‍ñāti sabbaparijānanatau, yathāvuttassa sabbassa abhisamayavasaina parijānanatau| sauti yathāvuttau yaugāvacarau, ariyau aiva vā| sabbadukkhamupac‍cagāti sabbaṃ vaṭṭadukkhaṃ ac‍cagā atik‍kami, samatik‍kamīti atthau|

sattamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. mānapariñ‍ñāsuttavaṇṇanā

8. aṭṭhamai apubbaṃ natthi, kaivalaṃ mānavasaina daisanā pavattā| gāthāsu pana mānupaitā ayaṃ pajāti kammakilaisaihi pajāyatīti pajāti laddhanāmā imai sattā mañ‍ñanalakkhaṇaina mānaina  upaitā upagatā| mānaganthā bhavai ratāti kimikīṭapaṭaṅgādiattabhāvaipi mānaina ganthitā mānasaṃyaujanaina saṃyuttā| tatau aiva dīgharattaṃ paribhāvitāhaṃkāravasaina ‘‘aitaṃ mamā’’ti saṅkhāraisu ajjhausānabahulattā tattha nic‍casukhaattādivipal‍lāsavasaina ca kāmādibhavai ratā| mānaṃ aparijānantāti mānaṃ tīhi pariñ‍ñāhi na parijānantā| arahattamaggañāṇaina vā anatik‍kamantā, ‘‘mānaṃ apariñ‍ñāyā’’ti kaici paṭhanti| āgantārau punabbhavanti puna āyātiṃ upapattibhavaṃ| punappunaṃ bhavanatau vā punabbhavasaṅkhātaṃ saṃsāraṃ aparāparaṃ parivattanavasaina gantārau upagantārau haunti, bhavatau na parimuc‍cantīti atthau| yai ca mānaṃ pahantvāna, vimuttā mānasaṅkhayaiti yai pana arahattamaggaina sabbasau mānaṃ pajahitvā mānassa ac‍cantasaṅkhayabhūtai arahattaphalai nibbānai vā tadaikaṭṭhasabbakilaisavimuttiyā vimuttā suṭṭhu muttā| tai mānaganthābhibhunau, sabbadukkhamupac‍cagunti  tai parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā arahantau sabbasau mānaganthaṃ mānasaṃyaujanaṃ samucchaidappahānaina abhibhavitvā ṭhitā, anavasaisaṃ vaṭṭadukkhaṃ atik‍kamiṃsūti atthau| aivamaitasmiṃ sattamasuttai ca arahattaṃ kathitanti|

aṭṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9-10. laubhadausapariñ‍ñāsuttadvayavaṇṇanā

9-10. navamadasamaisu  apubbaṃ natthi| daisanāvilāsavasaina tathā bujjhanakānaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ ajjhāsayavasaina vā tathā daisitānīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

navamadasamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

paṭhamavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. dutiyavaggau

1-3. mauhapariñ‍ñādisuttavaṇṇanā

11-13. dutiyavaggaipi  paṭhamādīni tīṇi suttāni vuttanayānaiva, tathā daisanākāraṇampi vuttamaiva|

4. avij‍jānīvaraṇasuttavaṇṇanā

14. catutthai – ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai’’tiādīsu na-kārau paṭisaidhatthau| ahanti bhagavā attānaṃ niddisati| añ‍ñanti idāni vattabbaavij‍jānīvaraṇatau añ‍ñaṃ| aikanīvaraṇampīti aikanīvaraṇadhammampi| samanupassāmīti dvai samanupassanā – diṭṭhisamanupassanā ca ñāṇasamanupassanā ca| tattha ‘‘rūpaṃ attatau samanupassatī’’tiādinā (a॰ ni॰ 4.200; paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.130) āgatā ayaṃ diṭṭhisamanupassanā nāma| ‘‘anic‍catau samanupassati, nau nic‍catau’’tiādinā (paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.35) pana āgatā ayaṃ ñāṇasamanupassanā nāma| idhāpi ñāṇasamanupassanāva adhippaitā| ‘‘samanupassāmī’’ti ca padassa na-kāraina sambandhau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – ‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇasaṅkhātaina samantacakkhunā sabbadhammai hatthāmalakaṃ viya aulaukaintaupi añ‍ñaṃ aikanīvaraṇampi na samanupassāmī’’ti|

yaina nīvaraṇaina nivutā pajā dīgharattaṃ sandhāvanti saṃsarantīti yaina nīvaraṇakasabhāvattā nīvaraṇaina dhammasabhāvaṃ jānituṃ passituṃ paṭivijjhituṃ adatvā  chādaitvā pariyaunandhitvā  ṭhānaina andhakāraina nivutā sattā anādimatasaṃsārai aparimāṇai kappai mahantaisu caiva khuddakaisu ca bhavādīsu aparāparuppattivasaina sabbatau dhāvanti caiva saṃsaranti, ca| ārammaṇantarasaṅkamanavasaina vā sandhāvanaṃ, bhavantarasaṅkamanavasaina saṃsaraṇaṃ| kilaisānaṃ balavabhāvaina vā sandhāvanaṃ, dubbalabhāvaina saṃsaraṇaṃ| khaṇikamaraṇavasaina vā aikajātiyaṃ sandhāvanaṃ, vauhāramaraṇavasaina anaikāsu jātīsu saṃsaraṇaṃ| cittavasaina vā sandhāvanaṃ, ‘‘cittamassa vidhāvatī’’ti hi vuttaṃ, kammavasaina saṃsaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ sandhāvanasaṃsaraṇānaṃ visaisau vaiditabbau|

yathayidanti  yathā idaṃ| ya-kārau padasandhikarau, sandhivasaina rassattaṃ| avij‍jānīvaraṇanti aittha pūraituṃ ayuttaṭṭhaina kāyaduc‍caritādi avindiyaṃ nāma, aladdhabbanti atthau| taṃ avindiyaṃ vindatīti avij‍jā| viparītatau kāyasucaritādi vindiyaṃ nāma, taṃ vindiyaṃ na vindatīti avij‍jā| khandhānaṃ rāsaṭṭhaṃ, āyatanānaṃ āyatanaṭṭhaṃ, dhātūnaṃ suñ‍ñaṭṭhaṃ, indriyānaṃ ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaṃ, sac‍cānaṃ tathaṭṭhaṃ dukkhādīnaṃ pīḷanādivasaina vuttaṃ catubbidhaṃ atthaṃ aviditaṃ karautītipi avij‍jā| antavirahitai saṃsārai sattai javāpaitīti vā avij‍jā, paramatthatau vā avij‍jamānaisu itthipurisādīsu javati pavattati, vij‍jamānaisu khandhādīsu na javati, na pavattatīti avij‍jā| apica cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ vatthārammaṇānaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādapaṭic‍casamuppan‍nānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ chādanataupi avij‍jā| avij‍jāva nīvaraṇanti avij‍jānīvaraṇaṃ|

avij‍jānīvaraṇaina hi, bhikkhavai, nivutā pajā dīgharattaṃ sandhāvanti saṃsarantīti idaṃ purimassaiva daḷhīkaraṇatthaṃ vuttaṃ| purimaṃ vā – ‘‘yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, avij‍jānīvaraṇa’’nti aivaṃ aupammadassanavasaina vuttaṃ, idaṃ nīvaraṇānubhāvadassanavasaina| kasmā panaittha avij‍jāva aivaṃ vuttā, na añ‍ñai dhammāti? ādīnavapaṭicchādanaina kāmacchandādīnaṃ visaisappac‍cayabhāvatau| tathā hi tāya paṭicchāditādīnavai visayai kāmacchandādayau pavattanti|

natthañ‍ñauti  ādikā gāthā vuttassa avuttassa ca atthassa saṅgaṇhanavasaina bhāsitā| tattha nivutāti nivāritā paliguṇṭhitā, paṭicchāditāti atthau| ahaurattanti divā caiva rattiñ‍ca, sabbakālanti vuttaṃ hauti| yathā  mauhaina āvutāti yaina pakāraina avij‍jānīvaraṇasaṅkhātaina mauhaina āvutā paṭicchāditā suviñ‍ñaiyyampi ajānantiyau pajā saṃsārai saṃsaranti, tathārūpau añ‍ñau aikadhammaupi aikanīvaraṇampi natthīti yaujaitabbaṃ| yai ca mauhaṃ pahantvāna, tamaukhandhaṃ padālayunti yai pana ariyasāvakā pubbabhāgai tadaṅgādippahānavasaina, haiṭṭhimamaggaihi vā taṃtaṃmaggavajjhaṃ mauhaṃ pajahitvā aggamaggaina vajirūpamañāṇaina mauhasaṅkhātamaiva tamaurāsiṃ padālayiṃsu, anavasaisatau samucchindiṃsu| na tai puna saṃsarantīti tai arahantau –

‘‘khandhānañ‍ca paṭipāṭi, dhātuāyatanāna ca|

abbaucchin‍naṃ vattamānā, saṃsārauti pavuc‍catī’’ti|| –

aivaṃ vuttai imasmiṃ saṃsārai na saṃsaranti na paribbhamanti| kiṃ kāraṇā? haitu taisaṃ na vij‍jati, yasmā saṃsārassa haitu mūlakāraṇaṃ avij‍jā, sā taisaṃ na vij‍jati, sabbasau natthi samucchin‍nattāti|

catutthasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. taṇhāsaṃyaujanasuttavaṇṇanā

15. pañ‍camai yassa vij‍jati, taṃ puggalaṃ dukkhaihi, kammaṃ vā vipākaihi, bhavayaunigativiñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisattāvāsai vā bhavantarādīhi saṃyaujaitīti saṃyaujanaṃ| taṇhāyanaṭṭhaina taṇhā, tasati sayaṃ paritasati, tasanti vā aitāyāti taṇhā| sañ‍ñuttāti cakkhādīsu abhinivaisavatthūsu baddhā| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva| kāmañ‍caittha avij‍jāyapi saṃyaujanabhāvau taṇhāya ca nīvaraṇabhāvau atthiyaiva, tathāpi avij‍jāya paṭicchāditādīnavaihi bhavaihi taṇhā sattai saṃyaujaitīti imassa visaisassa dassanatthaṃ purimasuttai avij‍jā nīvaraṇabhāvaina, idha ca taṇhā saṃyaujanabhāvainaiva vuttā| kiñ‍ca nīvaraṇasaṃyaujanappadhānassa  dassanatthaṃ| yathā hi nīvaraṇabhāvaina avij‍jā saṃkilaisadhammānaṃ padhānabhūtā pubbaṅgamā ca, aivaṃ saṃyaujanabhāvaina naisaṃ taṇhāti tadadhīnappadhānabhāvaṃ dassaituṃ suttadvayai aivamaitai dhammā vuttā| apica visaisaina avij‍jā nibbānasukhaṃ nivāraitīti  ‘‘nīvaraṇa’’nti vuttā, taṇhā saṃsāradukkhaina sattai saṃyaujaitīti ‘‘saṃyaujana’’nti|

dassanagamanantarāyakaraṇatau vā vij‍jācaraṇavipakkhatau dvayaṃ dvidhā vuttaṃ| vij‍jāya hi ujuvipac‍canīkabhūtā avij‍jā nibbānadassanassa aviparītadassanassa ca visaisatau antarāyakarā, caraṇadhammānaṃ ujuvipac‍canīkabhūtā taṇhā gamanassa sammāpaṭipattiyā antarāyakarāti; aivamayaṃ avij‍jāya nivutau andhīkatau taṇhāya saṃvutau baddhau assutavā puthuj‍janau andhau viya baddhau mahākantāraṃ, saṃsārakantāraṃ nātivattati| anatthuppattihaitudvayadassanatthampi dvayaṃ dvidhā vuttaṃ| avij‍jāgatau hi puggalau bālabhāvaina atthaṃ parihāpaiti, anatthañ‍ca attanau karauti, akusalau viya āturau asappāyakiriyāya| jānantaupi bālau bālabhāvaina atthaṃ parihāpaiti, anatthañ‍ca karauti jānantau viya raugī asappāyasaivī| mak‍kaṭālaipaupamasuttaṃ caitassa atthassa sādhakaṃ|

paṭic‍casamuppādassa mūlakāraṇadassanatthampaittha dvayaṃ dvidhā vuttaṃ| visaisaina hi sammauhassa balavabhāvatau  avij‍jākhaittaṃ atītau addhā, patthanāya balavabhāvatau taṇhākhaittaṃ anāgatau addhā| tathā hi bālajanau sammauhabahulau atītamanusaucati, tassa avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti sabbaṃ naitabbaṃ| patthanābahulau anāgataṃ pajappati, tassa taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānantiādi sabbaṃ naitabbaṃ| tainaiva tāsaṃ pubbantāharaṇaina aparantapaṭisandhānaina cassa yathāk‍kamaṃ mūlakāraṇatā dassitāti vaiditabbanti|

gāthāsu taṇhādutiyauti taṇhāsahāyau| taṇhā hi nirudakakantārai marīcikāya udakasañ‍ñā viya pipāsābhibhūtaṃ appaṭikāradukkhābhibhūtampi sattaṃ assādasandassanavasaina sahāyakic‍caṃ karauntī bhavādīsu anibbindaṃ katvā paribbhamāpaiti, tasmā taṇhā purisassa  ‘‘dutiyā’’ti vuttā| nanu ca añ‍ñaipi kilaisādayau bhavābhinibbattiyā pac‍cayāva? sac‍camaitaṃ, na pana tathā visaisappac‍cayau yathā taṇhā| tathā hi sā kusalaihi vinā  akusalaihi, kāmāvacarādikusalaihi ca vinā rūpāvacarādikusalaihi bhavanibbattiyā visaisappac‍cayau, yatau samudayasac‍canti vuc‍catīti| itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvanti itthabhāvau ca añ‍ñathābhāvau ca itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvau| sau aitassa atthīti itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvau saṃsārau, taṃ tattha itthabhāvau manussattaṃ, añ‍ñathābhāvau tatau avasiṭṭhasattāvāsā| itthabhāvau vā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nau attabhāvau, añ‍ñathābhāvau anāgatattabhāvau| aivarūpau vā añ‍ñaupi attabhāvau itthabhāvau, na aivarūpau añ‍ñathābhāvau| taṃ itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ saṃsāraṃ khandhadhātuāyatanapaṭipāṭiṃ nātivattati, na atik‍kamati|

aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, taṇhaṃ dukkhassa sambhavanti aitaṃ sakalavaṭṭadukkhassa sambhavaṃ samudayaṃ taṇhaṃ ādīnavaṃ ādīnavatau ñatvāti atthau| atha vā aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvāti aitaṃ yathāvuttaṃ saṃsāranātivattanaṃ ādīnavaṃ dausaṃ ñatvā| taṇhaṃ dukkhassa sambhavanti taṇhañ‍ca vuttanayaina vaṭṭadukkhassa padhānakāraṇanti ñatvā| vītataṇhau anādānau, satau bhikkhu, paribbajaiti aivaṃ tīhi pariñ‍ñāhi parijānantau vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā maggapaṭipāṭiyā taṇhaṃ vigamaintau aggamaggaina sabbasau vītataṇhau vigatataṇhau, tatau aiva catūsu upādānaisu kassacipi abhāvaina āyatiṃ paṭisandhisaṅkhātassa vā ādānassa abhāvaina anādānau, sativaipul‍lappattiyā sabbattha sataukāritāya satau bhin‍nakilaisau bhikkhu paribbajai caraiyya, khandhaparinibbānaina vā saṅkhārappavattitau apagacchaiyyāti atthau|

pañ‍camasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. paṭhamasaikhasuttavaṇṇanā

16. chaṭṭhai  saikhassāti aittha kainaṭṭhaina saikhau? saikkhadhammapaṭilābhatau saikhau| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘kittāvatā  nu khau, bhantai, saikhau hautīti? idha, bhikkhu, saikhāya sammādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti…pai॰… saikhaina sammāsamādhinā  saman‍nāgatau hauti| aittāvatā khau, bhikkhu, saikhau hautī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.13)|

apica sikkhatīti saikhau| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘sikkhatīti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā saikhauti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca sikkhati? adhisīlampi sikkhati, adhicittampi sikkhati, adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhati| sikkhatīti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā saikhauti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 3.86)|

yaupi kalyāṇaputhuj‍janau anulaumappaṭipadāya paripūrakārī sīlasampan‍nau indriyaisu guttadvārau bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū jāgariyānuyaugamanuyuttau pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati – ‘‘aj‍ja vā svai vā añ‍ñataraṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ adhigamissāmī’’ti, saupi vuc‍cati sikkhatīti saikhauti| imasmiṃ atthai na paṭivijjhantauva saikhau adhippaitau, atha khau kalyāṇaputhuj‍janaupi| appattaṃ mānasaṃ aitaināti appattamānasau| mānasanti ‘‘antalikkhacarau pāsau, yvāyaṃ carati mānasau’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.151; mahāva॰ 33) aittha rāgau mānasanti vuttau| ‘‘cittaṃ manau mānasa’’nti (dha॰ sa॰ 63, 65) aittha cittaṃ| ‘‘appattamānasau saikhau, kālaṃ kayirā janai sutā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.159) aittha arahattaṃ| idhāpi arahattamaiva adhippaitaṃ| taina appattaarahattassāti vuttaṃ hauti|

anuttaranti saiṭṭhaṃ, asadisanti atthau| catūhi yaugaihi khaimaṃ anupaddutanti yaugakkhaimaṃ, arahattamaiva adhippaitaṃ| patthayamānassāti dvai patthanā taṇhāpatthanā, kusalacchandapatthanā ca| ‘‘patthayamānassa hi jappitāni, pavaidhitaṃ vāpi pakappitaisū’’ti (su॰ ni॰ 908; mahāni॰ 137) aittha taṇhāpatthanā|

‘‘chin‍naṃ  pāpimatau sautaṃ, viddhastaṃ vinaḷīkataṃ|

pāmauj‍jabahulā hautha, khaimaṃ patthaitha bhikkhavau’’ti|| (ma॰ ni॰ 1.352)|

aittha kattukamyatākusalacchandapatthanā, ayamaiva idhādhippaitā| taina patthayamānassāti taṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ gantukāmassa tan‍nin‍nassa tappauṇassa tappabbhārassāti atthau| viharatauti aikaṃ iriyāpathadukkhaṃ añ‍ñaina iriyāpathaina vicchinditvā aparipatantaṃ attabhāvaṃ haratau| atha vā ‘‘sabbai  saṅkhārā anic‍cāti adhimuc‍cantau saddhāya viharatī’’tiādinā niddaisanayaina caittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| ajjhattikanti niyakajjhattasaṅkhātai ajjhattai bhavaṃ ajjhattikaṃ| aṅganti kāraṇaṃ| iti karitvāti aivaṃ katvā| na añ‍ñaṃ aikaṅgampi samanupassāmīti aittha ayaṃ saṅkhaipatthau – bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ attanau santānai samuṭṭhitaṃ kāraṇanti katvā añ‍ñaṃ aikakāraṇampi na samanupassāmi yaṃ aivaṃ bahūpakāraṃ, yathayidaṃ yaunisau manasikārauti upāyamanasikārau, pathamanasikārau, anic‍cādīsu anic‍cādinayainaiva manasikārau, anic‍cānulaumikaina vā cittassa āvaṭṭanā anvāvaṭṭanā ābhaugau saman‍nāhārau manasikārau| ayaṃ yaunisau manasikārau|

idāni yaunisau manasikārassa ānubhāvaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘yaunisau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu manasi karauntau akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha yaunisau manasi karauntauti ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau ariyasac‍caṃ, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau ariyasac‍caṃ, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’’nti catūsu ariyasac‍caisu yaunisau manasikāraṃ pavattaintau|

tatrāyaṃ atthavibhāvanā – yadipi idaṃ suttaṃ avisaisaina saikkhapuggalavasaina āgataṃ, catumaggasādhāraṇavasaina pana saṅkhaipainaiva kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathayissāma| yau catusac‍cakammaṭṭhānikau yaugāvacarau ‘‘taṇhāvaj‍jā  taibhūmakā khandhā dukkhaṃ, taṇhā samudayau, ubhin‍naṃ appavatti niraudhau, niraudhasampāpakau maggau’’ti aivaṃ pubbai aiva ācariyasantikai uggahitacatusac‍cakammaṭṭhānau| sau aparaina samayaina vipassanāmaggaṃ samāruḷhau samānau taibhūmakai khandhai ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti yaunisau manasi karauti, upāyaina pathaina saman‍nāharati caiva vipassati ca| vipassanā hi idha manasikārasīsaina vuttā| yā panāyaṃ tassa dukkhassa samuṭṭhāpikā purimabhavikā taṇhā, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti yaunisau manasi karauti| yasmā pana idaṃ dukkhaṃ, ayañ‍ca samudayau idaṃ ṭhānaṃ patvā nirujjhanti na pavattanti, tasmā yadidaṃ nibbānaṃ nāma, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti yaunisau manasi karauti| niraudhasampāpakaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti yaunisau manasi karauti, upāyaina pathaina saman‍nāharati caiva vipassati ca|

tatrāyaṃ  upāyau – abhinivaisau nāma khandhai hauti, na vivaṭṭai, tasmā ayamatthau – ‘‘imasmiṃ kāyai pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātū’’tiādinā (dī॰ ni॰ 2.378) nayaina cattāri mahābhūtāni tadanusāraina upādārūpāni ca pariggahaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ rūpakkhandhau’’ti vavatthapaiti| taṃ vavatthāpayatau uppan‍nai tadārammaṇai cittacaitasikadhammai ‘‘imai cattārau arūpakkhandhā’’ti vavatthapaiti| tatau ‘‘imai pañ‍cakkhandhā dukkha’’nti vavatthapaiti| tai pana saṅkhaipatau nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍cāti dvai bhāgā haunti| idañ‍ca nāmarūpaṃ sahaitu sappac‍cayaṃ uppaj‍jati, tassa ayaṃ avij‍jābhavataṇhādikau haitu, ayaṃ āhārādikau pac‍cayauti haituppac‍cayai vavatthapaiti| sau taisaṃ pac‍cayānañ‍ca pac‍cayuppan‍nānañ‍ca yāthāvasarasalakkhaṇaṃ vavatthapaitvā ‘‘imai dhammā ahutvā bhavanti, hutvā nirujjhanti, tasmā anic‍cā’’ti anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ āraupaiti, ‘‘udayabbayapaṭipīḷitattā dukkhā’’ti dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ āraupaiti, ‘‘avasavattanatau anattā’’ti anattalakkhaṇaṃ āraupaiti|

aivaṃ tilakkhaṇāni āraupaitvā vipassantau udayabbayañāṇuppattiyā uppan‍nai aubhāsādikai vipassanupak‍kilaisai ‘amaggau’ti udayabbayañāṇamaiva ‘‘ariyamaggassa upāyabhūtau pubbabhāgamaggau’’ti maggāmaggaṃ vavatthapaitvā  puna udayabbayañāṇaṃ paṭipāṭiyā bhaṅgañāṇādīni ca uppādaintau sautāpattimaggādayau pāpuṇāti| tasmiṃ khaṇai cattāri sac‍cāni aikappaṭivaidhainaiva paṭivijjhati, aikābhisamayaina abhisamaiti| tattha dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñāpaṭivaidhaina paṭivijjhantau, samudayaṃ pahānappaṭivaidhaina paṭivijjhantau sabbaṃ akusalaṃ pajahati, niraudhaṃ sacchikiriyāpaṭivaidhaina paṭivijjhantau maggaṃ bhāvanāpaṭivaidhaina paṭivijjhantau sabbaṃ kusalaṃ bhāvaiti| ariyamaggau hi nippariyāyatau kucchitasalanādiatthaina kusalau, tasmiñ‍ca bhāvitai sabbaipi kusalā anavaj‍jabaudhipakkhiyadhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchantīti| aivaṃ yaunisau manasi karauntau akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti| tathā hi vuttaṃ – ‘‘idaṃ dukkhanti yaunisau manasi karauti, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti yaunisau manasi karautī’’tiādi (ma॰ ni॰ 1.21)| aparampi vuttaṃ ‘‘yaunisau manasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissatī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.55)|

yaunisau  manasikārauti gāthāya ayaṃ saṅkhaipatthau – sikkhati, sikkhāpadāni tassa atthi, sikkhanasīlauti vā saikhau| saṃsārai bhayaṃ ikkhatīti bhikkhu| tassa saikhassa bhikkhunau uttamatthassa arahattassa pattiyā adhigamāya yathā yaunisau manasikārau, aivaṃ bahukārau bahūpakārau añ‍ñau kauci dhammau natthi| kasmā? yasmā yaunisau upāyaina manasikāraṃ purakkhatvā padahaṃ catubbidhasammappadhānavasaina padahantau, khayaṃ dukkhassa pāpuṇai saṃkilaisavaṭṭadukkhassa parikkhayaṃ pariyausānaṃ nibbānaṃ pāpuṇai adhigacchaiyya, tasmā yaunisau manasikārau bahukārauti|

chaṭṭhasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. dutiyasaikhasuttavaṇṇanā

17. sattamai bāhiranti ajjhattasantānatau bahi bhavaṃ| kalyāṇamittatāti yassa sīlādiguṇasampan‍nau aghassa ghātā, hitassa vidhātā sabbākāraina upakārakau mittau hauti, sau puggalau kalyāṇamittau, tassa bhāvau kalyāṇamittatā| tatrāyaṃ kalyāṇamittau pakatiyā saddhāsampan‍nau hauti sīlasampan‍nau sutasampan‍nau cāgasampan‍nau vīriyasampan‍nau satisampan‍nau samādhisampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau| tattha saddhāsampattiyā saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ, taina sammāsambaudhihaitubhūtaṃ sattaisu hitasukhaisitaṃ na paric‍cajati, sīlasampattiyā sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau hauti garu ca bhāvanīyau caudakau pāpagarahī vattā vacanakkhamau, sutasampattiyā khandhāyatanasac‍capaṭic‍casamuppādādikānaṃ gambhīrānaṃ kathānaṃ kattā hauti, cāgasampattiyā appicchau hauti santuṭṭhau pavivittau asaṃsaṭṭhau, vīriyasampattiyā attanau paraisañ‍ca hitappaṭipattiyaṃ āraddhavīriyau hauti, satisampattiyā upaṭṭhitassati hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā, samādhisampattiyā avikkhittau hauti samāhitau aikaggacittau, pañ‍ñāsampattiyā aviparītaṃ pajānāti| sau  satiyā kusalākusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ gatiyau samanvaisantau pañ‍ñāya sattānaṃ hitasukhaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānitvā samādhinā tattha abyaggacittau hutvā vīriyaina sattai ahitatau nisaidhaitvā aikantahitai niyaujaiti| tainaivāha –

‘‘piyau garu bhāvanīyau, vattā ca vacanakkhamau|

gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, nau cāṭṭhānai niyaujakau’’ti|| (naitti॰ 113)|

kalyāṇamittau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaitīti kalyāṇamittau puggalau kalyāṇamittaṃ nissāya kammassakatāñāṇaṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍naṃ saddhaṃ phātiṃ karauti, saddhājātau upasaṅkamati upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ suṇāti| taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati, taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina gharāvāsaṃ pahāya pabbaj‍jaṃ anutiṭṭhati, catupārisuddhisīlaṃ sampādaiti, yathābalaṃ dhutadhammai samādāya vattati, dasakathāvatthulābhī hauti, āraddhavīriyau viharati upaṭṭhitassati sampajānau pubbarattāpararattaṃ baudhipakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttau, nacirassaiva vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā ariyamaggādhigamaina  sabbaṃ akusalaṃ samucchindati, sabbañ‍ca kusalaṃ bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gamaintau vaḍḍhaiti| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa ‘yaṃ sīlavā bhavissati, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharissati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu, bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhissati, sikkhāpadaisu’|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ…pai॰… kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa ‘yaṃ yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā aikantanibbidāya…pai॰… nibbānāya saṃvattati| saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā, santuṭṭhikathā, pavivaikakathā, asaṃsaggakathā, vīriyārambhakathā, sīlakathā, samādhikathā…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā| aivarūpāya kathāya nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ  …pai॰… kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa ‘yaṃ āraddhavīriyau viharissati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu’|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ…pai॰… kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa ‘yaṃ pañ‍ñavā bhavissati, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā’’’ti (udā॰ 31)|

aivaṃ sakalavaṭṭadukkhaparimuc‍cananimittaṃ kalyāṇamittatāti vaiditabbaṃ| tainaivāha –

‘‘mamañhi, ānanda, kalyāṇamittaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti, jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍cantī’’tiādi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.129)|

taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘kalyāṇamittau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

gāthāya sappatissauti patissavasaṅkhātaina saha patissaināti sappatissau, kalyāṇamittassa auvādaṃ sirasā sampaṭicchakau subbacauti atthau| atha vā hitasukhai patiṭṭhāpanaina pati isaitīti patissau, auvādadāyakau| garuādarayaugaina taina patissaina saha vattatīti sappatissau, garūsu garucittīkārabahulau| sagāravauti chabbidhainapi gāravaina yuttau| karaṃ mittānaṃ vacananti kalyāṇamittānaṃ auvādaṃ karauntau yathauvādaṃ paṭipaj‍jantau| sampajānauti sattaṭṭhāniyaina sampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau| patissatauti kammaṭṭhānaṃ phātiṃ, gamaituṃ samatthāya satiyā patissatau sataukārī| anupubbaināti sīlādivisuddhipaṭipāṭiyā, tattha ca vipassanāpaṭipāṭiyā caiva maggapaṭipāṭiyā ca| sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayanti kāmarāgasaṃyaujanādīnaṃ sabbaisaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ khaipanatau sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayasaṅkhātassa ariyamaggassa pariyausānabhūtaṃ arahattaṃ, tassa ārammaṇabhūtaṃ nibbānamaiva vā| pāpuṇai adhigacchaiyyāti atthau| iti imaisu dvīsu suttaisu ariyamaggādhigamassa satthārā padhānaṅgaṃ nāma gahitanti vaiditabbaṃ|

sattamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. saṅghabhaidasuttavaṇṇanā

18. aṭṭhamai  aikadhammauti katarauyaṃ suttanikkhaipau? aṭṭhuppattikau| tatrāyaṃ saṅkhaipakathā – daivadattau hi ajātasattuṃ duggahaṇaṃ gāhāpaitvā tassa pitaraṃ rājānaṃ bimbisāraṃ taina mārāpaitvāpi abhimārai payaujaitvāpi silāpavijjhanaina lauhituppādakammaṃ katvāpi na tāvatā pākaṭau jātau, nāḷāgiriṃ vissaj‍jaitvā pana pākaṭau jātau| atha mahājanau ‘‘aivarūpampi nāma pāpaṃ gahaitvā rājā vicaratī’’ti kaulāhalaṃ akāsi, mahāghausau ahausi| taṃ sutvā rājā attanā dīyamānāni pañ‍ca thālipākasatāni pacchindāpaisi, upaṭṭhānampissa nāgamāsi| nāgarāpi kulaṃ upagatassa kaṭacchubhattampissa nādaṃsu| sau parihīnalābhasak‍kārau kauhañ‍ñaina jīvitukāmau satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ca vatthūni yācitvā ‘‘alaṃ, daivadatta, yau icchati, sau  ārañ‍ñikau hautū’’tiādinā (pārā॰ 409; cūḷava॰ 343) bhagavatā paṭikkhittau taihi pañ‍cahi vatthūhi bālaṃ lūkhappasan‍naṃ janaṃ sañ‍ñāpaintau pañ‍casatai vaj‍jiputtakai salākaṃ gāhāpaitvā saṅghaṃ bhinditvāva tai ādāya gayāsīsaṃ agamāsi| atha dvai aggasāvakā satthu āṇāya tattha gantvā dhammaṃ daisaitvā tai ariyaphalai patiṭṭhāpaitvā ānayiṃsu| yai panassa saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamantassa laddhiṃ raucaitvā tathaiva paggayha ṭhitā saṅghai bhij‍jantai bhin‍nai ca samanuñ‍ñā ahaisuṃ, taisaṃ taṃ dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya ahausi|

daivadattaupi na cirassaiva raugābhibhūtau bāḷhagilānau maraṇakālai ‘‘satthāraṃ vandissāmī’’ti mañ‍cakasivikāya nīyamānau jaitavanapaukkharaṇitīrai ṭhapitau pathaviyā vivarai din‍nai patitvā avīcimhi nibbatti, yaujanasatikau cassa attabhāvau ahausi kappaṭṭhiyau tālakkhandhaparimāṇaihi ayasūlaihi vinividdhau| daivadattapakkhikāni ca pañ‍camattāni kulasatāni tassa laddhiyaṃ ṭhitāni saha bandhavaihi nirayai nibbattāni| aikadivasaṃ bhikkhū dhammasabhāyaṃ kathaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisuṃ ‘‘āvusau, daivadattaina saṅghaṃ bhindantaina bhāriyaṃ kammaṃ kata’’nti| atha satthā dhammasabhaṃ upagantvā ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā’’ti pucchitvā ‘‘imāya nāmā’’ti vuttai saṅghabhaidai ādīnavaṃ dassaintau  imaṃ suttaṃ abhāsi| kaici pana bhaṇanti ‘‘daivadattassa tappakkhikānañ‍ca tathā nirayai nibbattabhāvaṃ disvā saṅghabhaidai ādīnavaṃ dassaintau bhagavā attanau ajjhāsayainaiva imaṃ suttaṃ daisaisī’’ti|

tattha aikadhammauti aikau akusalau mahāsāvaj‍jadhammau| laukaiti sattalaukai| uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jatīti aittha bhaidasaṃvattanikaisu bhaṇḍanādīsu saṅghai uppan‍naisupi ‘‘dhammau adhammau’’tiādīsu aṭṭhārasabhaidakaravatthūsu yassa kassaci dīpanavasaina vauharantaisupi tattha rucijananatthaṃ anussāvaintaisupi anussāvaitvā salākāya gāhitāyapi saṅghabhaidau uppaj‍jamānau nāma hauti, salākāya pana gāhitāya cattārau vā atiraikā vā yadā āvaiṇikaṃ uddaisaṃ vā saṅghakammaṃ vā karaunti, tadā saṅghabhaidau uppaj‍jati nāma| katai pana tasmiṃ saṅghabhaidau uppan‍nau nāma? kammaṃ, uddaisau, vauhārau, anussāvanā, salākaggāhauti imaisu hi pañ‍casu saṅghassa bhaidakāraṇaisu kammaṃ vā uddaisau vā pamāṇaṃ, vauhārānussāvanasalākaggāhā pana pubbabhāgāti|

bahujanāhitāyātiādīsu 0.mahājanassa jhānamaggādisampattinivāraṇaina ahitāya, saggasampattinivāraṇaina asukhāya, apāyūpapattihaitubhāvaina anatthāya| akusaladhammavasaina vā ahitāya, hitamattassapi abhāvā sugatiyampi nibbattanakakāyikacaitasikadukkhāya uppaj‍jatīti  sambandhau| daivamanussānanti idaṃ ‘‘bahunau janassā’’ti vuttaisu uk‍kaṭṭhapuggalaniddaisau| aparau nayau – bahujanāhitāyāti bahujanassa mahatau sattakāyassa ahitatthāya, diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaanatthāyāti atthau| asukhāyāti diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaasukhatthāya, duvidhadukkhatthāyāti atthau| anatthāyāti paramatthapaṭikkhaipāya| nibbānañhi paramatthau, tatau uttariṃ atthau natthi| ahitāyāti maggapaṭikkhaipāya| nibbānasampāpakamaggatau hi uttariṃ hitaṃ nāma natthi| dukkhāyāti ariyasukhavirādhanaina vaṭṭadukkhatāya| yai hi ariyasukhatau viraddhā taṃ adhigantuṃ abhabbā, tai vaṭṭadukkhai paribbhamanti, ariyasukhatau ca uttariṃ sukhaṃ nāma natthi| vuttañhaitaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ samādhi pac‍cuppan‍nasukhau caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.355; a॰ ni॰ 5.27)|

idāni  ‘‘saṅghabhaidau’’ti sarūpatau dassaitvā tassa ahitādīnaṃ aikantahaitubhāvaṃ pakāsaituṃ ‘‘saṅghai khau pana, bhikkhavai, bhin‍nai’’tiādimāha| tattha bhin‍naiti nimittatthai bhummaṃ yathā ‘‘adhanānaṃ dhanai ananuppadīyamānai’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.91), bhaidahaitūti atthau| añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ bhaṇḍanānīti catun‍naṃ parisānaṃ tappakkhikānañ‍ca ‘‘aisau dhammau, naisau dhammau’’ti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vivadanāni| bhaṇḍanañhi kalahassa pubbabhāgau| paribhāsāti ‘‘idañ‍cidañ‍ca vau anatthaṃ karissāmā’’ti bhayuppādanavasaina taj‍janā| parikkhaipāti jātiādivasaina paritau khaipā, dasahi ak‍kausavatthūhi khuṃsanavambhanā| paric‍cajanāti ukkhaipaniyakammakaraṇādivasaina nissāraṇā| tatthāti tasmiṃ saṅghabhaidai, tan‍nimittai vā bhaṇḍanādikai| appasan‍nāti ratanattayaguṇānaṃ anabhiñ‍ñā| na pasīdantīti ‘‘dhammacārinau samacārinau’’tiādinā yvāyaṃ bhikkhūsu pasādanākārau, tathā na pasīdanti, taisaṃ vā sautabbaṃ saddhātabbaṃ na mañ‍ñanti| tathā ca dhammai satthari ca appasan‍nāva haunti| aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattanti puthuj‍janānaṃ aviruḷhasaddhānaṃ pasādañ‍ñathattaṃ|

gāthāyaṃ  āpāyikautiādīsu apāyai nibbattanārahatāya āpāyikau| tatthapi avīcisaṅkhātai mahānirayai uppaj‍jatīti nairayikau| aikaṃ antarakappaṃ paripuṇṇamaiva katvā tattha tiṭṭhatīti kappaṭṭhau| saṅghabhaidasaṅkhātai vaggai ratauti vaggaratau| adhammiyatāya adhammau| bhaidakaravatthūhi saṅghabhaidasaṅkhātai aiva ca adhammai ṭhitauti adhammaṭṭhau| yaugakkhaimā padhaṃsatīti yaugakkhaimatau hitatau padhaṃsati parihāyati, catūhi vā yaugaihi anupaddutattā yaugakkhaimaṃ nāma arahattaṃ nibbānañ‍ca, tatau panassa dhaṃsanai vattabbamaiva natthi| diṭṭhisīlasāmañ‍ñatau saṃhataṭṭhaina saṅghaṃ, tatau aiva aikakammādividhānayaugaina samaggaṃ sahitaṃ| bhaitvānāti pubbai vuttalakkhaṇaina saṅghabhaidaina  bhinditvā| kappanti āyukappaṃ| sau panaittha antarakappauva| nirayamhīti avīcimahānirayamhi|

aṭṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9. saṅghasāmaggīsuttavaṇṇanā

19. navamai  aikadhammauti aikau kusaladhammau anavaj‍jadhammau| ‘‘ayaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau’’tiādinā sacai saṅghai vivādau uppaj‍jaiyya, tattha dhammakāmaina viñ‍ñunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau, panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yadidaṃ vivādau vaḍḍhamānau saṅgharājiyā vā saṅghabhaidāya vā saṃvattaiyyā’’ti| sacai taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ attanā paggahaitvā ṭhitau, aggiṃ ak‍kantaina viya sahasā tatau auramitabbaṃ| atha paraihi taṃ paggahitaṃ sayañ‍caitaṃ sak‍kauti vūpasamaituṃ, ussāhajātau hutvā dūrampi gantvā tathā paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ, yathā taṃ vūpasammati| sacai pana sayaṃ na sak‍kauti, sau ca vivādau uparūpari vaḍḍhataiva, na vūpasammati| yai tattha patirūpā sikkhākāmā sabrahmacārinau, tai ussāhaitvā yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ yathā vūpasammati, tathā vūpasamaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ vūpasamaintassa yau saṅghasāmaggikarau kusalau dhammau, ayamaittha aikadhammauti adhippaitau| sau hi ubhataupakkhiyānaṃ dvaiḷhakajātānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, taisaṃ anuvattanavasaina ṭhitānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ  taisaṃ ārakkhadaivatānaṃ yāvadaiva brahmānampi uppaj‍janārahaṃ ahitaṃ dukkhāvahaṃ saṃkilaisadhammaṃ apanaitvā mahatau puñ‍ñarāsissa kusalābhisandassa haitubhāvatau sadaivakassa laukassa hitasukhāvahau hauti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘aikadhammau, bhikkhavai, laukai uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati bahujanahitāyā’’tiādi| tassatthau anantarasuttai vuttavipariyāyaina vaiditabbau| saṅghasāmaggīti saṅghassa samaggabhāvau bhaidābhāvau aikakammatā aikuddaisatā ca|

gāthāyaṃ sukhā saṅghassa sāmaggīti sukhassa pac‍cayabhāvatau sāmaggī sukhāti vuttā| yathā ‘‘sukhau buddhānamuppādau’’ti (dha॰ pa॰ 194)| samaggānañ‍canuggahauti samaggānaṃ sāmaggianumaudanaina anuggaṇhanaṃ sāmaggianurūpaṃ, yathā tai sāmaggiṃ na vijahanti, tathā gahaṇaṃ ṭhapanaṃ anubalappadānanti atthau| saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvānāti bhin‍naṃ saṅghaṃ rājipattaṃ vā samaggaṃ sahitaṃ katvā| kappanti āyukappamaiva| saggamhi maudatīti kāmāvacaradaivalaukai añ‍ñai  daivai  dasahi ṭhānaihi abhibhavitvā dibbasukhaṃ anubhavantau icchitanipphattiyāva maudati pamaudati lalati kīḷatīti|

navamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

10. paduṭṭhacittasuttavaṇṇanā

20. dasamassa kā uppatti? aṭṭhuppattiyaiva| aikadivasaṃ kira bhikkhū dhammasabhāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nā kathaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisuṃ – ‘‘āvusau, idhaikac‍cau bahuṃ puñ‍ñakammaṃ karauti, aikac‍cau bahuṃ pāpakammaṃ, aikac‍cau ubhayavaumissakaṃ karauti| tattha vaumissakārinau kīdisau abhisamparāyau’’ti? atha satthā dhammasabhaṃ upaganvā pañ‍ñattavarabuddhāsanai nisin‍nau taṃ kathaṃ sutvā ‘‘bhikkhavai, maraṇāsan‍nakālai saṃkiliṭṭhacittassa duggati pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti dassaintau imāya aṭṭhuppattiyā idaṃ suttaṃ daisaisi|

tattha idhāti daisāpadaisai nipātau| svāyaṃ katthaci padaisaṃ upādāya vuc‍cati ‘‘idhaiva tiṭṭhamānassa, daivabhūtassa mai satau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.369)| katthaci sāsanaṃ upādāya ‘‘idhaiva, bhikkhavai, samaṇau idha  dutiyau samaṇau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.139; a॰ ni॰ 4.241)| katthaci padapūraṇamattai ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhuttāvī assaṃ pavāritau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.30)| katthaci laukaṃ upādāya vuc‍cati ‘‘idha tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jatī’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 3.61)| idhāpi laukai aiva daṭṭhabbau| aikac‍canti aikaṃ, añ‍ñataranti atthau| puggalanti sattaṃ| sau hi yathāpac‍cayaṃ kusalākusalānaṃ tabbipākānañ‍ca pūraṇatau maraṇavasaina galanatau ca puggalauti vuc‍cati| paduṭṭhacittanti padausaina āghātaina duṭṭhacittaṃ| atha vā paduṭṭhacittanti dausaina rāgādinā padūsitacittaṃ| aittha ca aikac‍canti idaṃ paduṭṭhacittassa puggalassa visaisanaṃ| yassa hi paṭisandhidāyakakammaṃ aukāsamakāsi, sau tathā vuttau| yassa ca akusalappavattitau cittaṃ nivattaitvā kusalavasaina autāraituṃ na sak‍kā, aivaṃ āsan‍namaraṇau| aivanti idāni vattabbākāraṃ dassaiti| caitasāti attanau cittaina caitaupariyañāṇaina| caitauti tassa puggalassa cittaṃ| paric‍cāti paricchinditvā pajānāmi| nanu ca yathākammupagañāṇassāyaṃ visayauti? sac‍camaitaṃ, tadā pavattamānaakusalacittavasaina panaitaṃ vuttaṃ|

imamhi  cāyaṃ samayaiti imasmiṃ kālai, imāyaṃ vā pac‍cayasāmaggiyaṃ, ayaṃ puggalau javanavīthiyā aparabhāgai kālaṃ karaiyya caiti atthau| na hi javanakkhaṇai kālaṃkiriyā atthi| yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayaiti yathā ābhataṃ kiñ‍ci āharitvā ṭhapitaṃ, aivaṃ attanau kammunā nikkhittau nirayai ṭhapitau aivāti atthau| kāyassa bhaidāti upādin‍nakkhandhaparic‍cāgā| paraṃ maraṇāti tadanantaraṃ abhinibbattakkhandhaggahaṇai| atha vā kāyassa bhaidāti jīvitindriyassa upacchaidā| paraṃ maraṇāti cutitau uddhaṃ|

apāyantiādi sabbaṃ nirayassaiva vaivacanaṃ| nirayau hi ayasaṅkhātā sukhā apaitauti apāyau; saggamaukkhahaitubhūtā vā puñ‍ñasammatā ayā apaitautipi apāyau| dukkhassa gati paṭisaraṇanti duggati; dausabahulattā vā duṭṭhaina kammunā nibbattā gatītipi duggati| vivasā nipatanti aittha duk‍kaṭakammakārinau, vinassantā vā aittha nipatanti sambhij‍jamānaṅgapac‍caṅgāti vinipātau| natthi aittha assādasañ‍ñitau ayauti nirassādaṭṭhaina nirayau| atha vā apāyaggahaṇaina  tiracchānayauni vuc‍cati| tiracchānayauni hi apāyau sugatitau apaitattā, na duggati mahaisakkhānaṃ nāgarājādīnaṃ sambhavatau| duggatiggahaṇaina paittivisayau| sau hi apāyau caiva duggati ca sugatitau apaitattā dukkhassa ca gatibhūtattā, na vinipātau asurasadisaṃ avinipātattā| vinipātaggahaṇaina asurakāyau| sau hi yathāvuttaina atthaina apāyau caiva duggati ca, sabbasampattisamussayaihi vinipatitattā vinipātauti ca vuc‍cati| nirayaggahaṇaina avīciādianaikappakārau nirayauva vuc‍cati| idha pana sabbapadaihipi nirayauva vuttau| upapaj‍jantīti paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhanti|

gāthāsu paṭhamagāthā saṅgītikālai dhammasaṅgāhakatthairaihi ṭhapitā| ñatvānāti pubbakālakiriyā| ñāṇapubbakañhi byākaraṇaṃ| haituatthau vā tvā-saddau yathā ‘‘sīhaṃ disvā bhayaṃ hautī’’ti, jānanahaitūti atthau| buddhau, bhikkhūnaṃ santikaiti buddhau bhagavā attanau santikai bhikkhūnaṃ aitaṃ paratau dvīhi gāthāhi vuc‍camānaṃ atthaṃ byākāsi| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

dasamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

dutiyavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. tatiyavaggau

1. pasan‍nacittasuttavaṇṇanā

21. tatiyavaggassa  paṭhamai pasan‍nacittanti ratanattayasaddhāya kammaphalasaddhāya ca pasan‍namānasaṃ| sugatinti sundaraṃ gatiṃ, sukhassa vā gatinti sugatiṃ| sagganti rūpādisampattīhi suṭṭhu agganti saggaṃ| laukanti laukiyanti aittha puñ‍ñapāpaphalāni, luj‍janaṭṭhainaiva vā laukaṃ| aittha ca sugatiggahaṇaina manussagatipi saṅgayhati, saggaggahaṇaina daivagati aiva| saisaṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayamaiva|

paṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. maittasuttavaṇṇanā

22. dutiyai mā, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñānanti aittha māti paṭisaidhai nipātau| puñ‍ñasaddau ‘‘kusalānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ samādānahaitu aivamidaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatī’’tiādīsu  (dī॰ ni॰ 3.380) puñ‍ñaphalai āgatau| ‘‘avij‍jāgatauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau puñ‍ñañ‍cai saṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharautī’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.51) kāmarūpāvacarasucaritai| ‘‘puñ‍ñūpagaṃ bhavati viñ‍ñāṇa’’ntiādīsu sugativisaisabhūtai upapattibhavai| ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūni – dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthū’’tiādīsu (itivu॰ 60; a॰ ni॰ 8.36) kusalacaitanāyaṃ| idha pana taibhūmakakusaladhammai vaiditabbau| bhāyitthāti aittha duvidhaṃ bhayaṃ ñāṇabhayaṃ, sāraj‍jabhayanti| tattha ‘‘yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā uc‍caisu vimānaisu ciraṭṭhitikā, taipi tathāgatassa dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā yaibhuyyaina bhayaṃ saṃvaigaṃ santāsaṃ āpaj‍jantī’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 4.33) āgataṃ ñāṇabhayaṃ| ‘‘ahudaiva bhayaṃ, ahu chambhitattaṃ, ahu laumahaṃsau’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 2.318) āgataṃ sāraj‍jabhayaṃ| idhāpi sāraj‍jabhayamaiva| ayañhaittha  atthau – bhikkhavai, dīgharattaṃ kāyavacīsaṃyamau vattapaṭivattapūraṇaṃ aikāsanaṃ, aikasaiyyaṃ, indriyadamau, dhutadhammaihi cittassa niggahau, satisampajañ‍ñaṃ, kammaṭṭhānānuyaugavasaina vīriyārambhauti aivamādīni yāni bhikkhunā, nirantaraṃ pavattaitabbāni puñ‍ñāni, taihi mā bhāyittha, mā bhayaṃ santāsaṃ āpaj‍jittha, aikac‍cassa diṭṭhadhammasukhassa uparaudhabhayaina samparāyikanibbānasukhadāyakaihi puñ‍ñaihi mā bhāyitthāti| nissak‍kai hi idaṃ sāmivacanaṃ|

idāni tatau abhāyitabbabhāvai kāraṇaṃ dassaintau ‘‘sukhassaita’’ntiādimāha| tattha sukhasaddau ‘‘sukhau buddhānaṃ uppādau, sukhā virāgatā laukai’’tiādīsu (dha॰ pa॰ 194) sukhamūlai āgatau| ‘‘yasmā ca khau, mahāli, rūpaṃ sukhaṃ sukhānupatitaṃ sukhāvak‍kanta’’ntiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.60) sukhārammaṇai| ‘‘yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, na sukaraṃ akkhānaina pāpuṇituṃ yāva sukhā saggā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 3.255) sukhapac‍cayaṭṭhānai| ‘‘sukhau puñ‍ñassa uc‍cayau’’tiādīsu (dha॰ pa॰ 118) sukhahaitumhi| ‘‘diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārā aitai dhammā’’tiādīsu  (ma॰ ni॰ 1.82) abyāpaj‍jai| ‘‘nibbānaṃ paramaṃ sukha’’ntiādīsu (dha॰ pa॰ 204; ma॰ ni॰ 2.215) nibbānai| ‘‘sukhassa ca pahānā’’tiādīsu (cūḷani॰ khaggavisāṇasuttaniddaisa 125) sukhavaidanāyaṃ| ‘‘adukkhamasukhaṃ santaṃ, sukhamic‍caiva bhāsita’’ntiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.253; itivu॰ 53) upaikkhāvaidanāyaṃ| ‘‘dvaipi mayā, ānanda, vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.89) iṭṭhasukhai| ‘‘sukhau vipākau puñ‍ñāna’’ntiādīsu (paiṭakau॰ 23) sukhavipākai| idhāpi iṭṭhavipākai aiva daṭṭhabbau| iṭṭhassātiādīsu aisitabbatau aniṭṭhapaṭikkhaipatau ca iṭṭhassa, kamanīyatau manasmiñ‍ca kamanatau pavisanatau kantassa, piyāyitabbatau santappanatau ca piyassa, mānanīyatau manassa pavaḍḍhanatau ca manāpassāti atthau vaiditabbau| yadidaṃ puñ‍ñānīti ‘‘puñ‍ñānī’’ti yadidaṃ vacanaṃ, aitaṃ sukhassa iṭṭhassa vipākassa adhivacanaṃ nāmaṃ, sukhamaiva taṃ  yadidaṃ puñ‍ñanti phalaina kāraṇassa abhaidūpacāraṃ vadati| taina katūpacitānaṃ puñ‍ñānaṃ avassaṃbhāviphalaṃ sutvā appamattaina sak‍kac‍caṃ puñ‍ñāni kātabbānīti puñ‍ñakiriyāyaṃ niyaujaiti, ādarañ‍ca naisaṃ tattha uppādaiti|

idāni attanā sunaittakālai kataina puñ‍ñakammaina dīgharattaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ bhavantarapaṭicchan‍naṃ uḷāratamaṃ puñ‍ñavipākaṃ udāharitvā tamatthaṃ pākaṭaṃ karauntau ‘‘abhijānāmi khau panāha’’ntiādimāha| tattha abhijānāmīti abhivisiṭṭhaina ñāṇaina jānāmi, pac‍cakkhatau bujjhāmi| dīgharattanti cirakālaṃ| puñ‍ñānanti dānādikusaladhammānaṃ| satta vassānīti satta saṃvaccharāni| maittacittanti mij‍jatīti maittā, siniyhatīti atthau| mittai bhavā, mittassa vā aisā pavattītipi maittā| lakkhaṇāditau pana hitākārappavattilakkhaṇā, hitūpasaṃhārarasā, āghātavinayapac‍cupaṭṭhānā, sattānaṃ manāpabhāvadassanapadaṭṭhānā| byāpādūpasamau aitissā sampatti, sinaihāsambhavau vipatti| sā aitassa atthīti maittacittaṃ| bhāvaitvāti maittāsahagataṃ cittaṃ, cittasīsaina samādhi vuttauti maittāsamādhiṃ  maittābrahmavihāraṃ uppādaitvā caiva vaḍḍhaitvā ca| satta saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappaiti satta mahākappai| saṃvaṭṭa-vivaṭṭaggahaṇainaiva hi saṃvaṭṭaṭṭhāyi-vivaṭṭaṭṭhāyinaupi gahitā| imaṃ laukanti kāmalaukaṃ| saṃvaṭṭamānai sudanti saṃvaṭṭamānai| sudanti nipātamattaṃ vinassamānaiti atthau| ‘‘saṃvattamānai suda’’nti ca paṭhanti| kappaiti kālai| kappasīsaina hi kālau vuttau| kālai khīyamānai kappaupi khīyataiva| yathāha –

‘‘kālau ghasati bhūtāni, sabbānaiva sahattanā’’ti|| (jā॰ 1.2.190)|

‘‘ābhassarūpagau haumī’’ti vuttattā taijausaṃvaṭṭavasainaittha kappavuṭṭhānaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| ābhassarūpagauti tattha paṭisandhiggahaṇavasaina ābhassarabrahmalaukaṃ upagacchāmīti ābhassarūpagau haumi| vivaṭṭamānaiti saṇṭhahamānai, jāyamānaiti atthau| suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍jāmīti kassaci sattassa tattha nibbattassa abhāvatau suñ‍ñaṃ, yaṃ paṭhamajjhānabhūmisaṅkhātaṃ brahmavimānaṃ āditau nibbattaṃ, taṃ paṭisandhiggahaṇavasaina upapaj‍jāmi upaimi| brahmāti kāmāvacarasattaihi saiṭṭhaṭṭhaina tathā tathā brūhitaguṇatāya brahmavihāratau nibbattaṭṭhaina ca brahmā| brahmapārisaj‍jabrahmapurauhitaihi mahantau brahmāti mahābrahmā| tatau aiva tai abhibhavitvā ṭhitattā  abhibhū| taihi kainaci guṇaina na abhibhūtauti anabhibhūtau| añ‍ñadatthūti aikaṃsavacanai nipātau| dasauti dassanasīlau, sau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ dassanasamatthau, abhiñ‍ñāṇaina passitabbaṃ passāmīti atthau| saisabrahmānaṃ iddhipādabhāvanābalaina attanau cittañ‍ca mama vasai vattaimīti vasavattī haumīti yaujaitabbaṃ| tadā kira baudhisattau aṭṭhasamāpattilābhīpi samānau tathā sattahitaṃ attanau pāramiparipūraṇañ‍ca aulaukaintau tāsu aiva dvīsu jhānabhūmīsu nikantiṃ uppādaitvā maittābrahmavihāravasaina aparāparaṃ saṃsari| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sattavassāni…pai॰… vasavattī’’ti|

aivaṃ bhagavā rūpāvacarapuñ‍ñassa vipākamahantataṃ pakāsaitvā idāni kāmāvacarapuñ‍ñassāpi taṃ dassaintau ‘‘chattiṃsakkhattu’’ntiādimāha| tattha sak‍kau ahausinti chattiṃsa vārai añ‍ñattha anupapaj‍jitvā  nirantaraṃ sak‍kau daivānamindau tāvatiṃsadaivarājā ahausi| rājā ahausintiādīsu  catūhi acchariyadhammaihi catūhi ca saṅgahavatthūhi laukaṃ rañ‍jaitīti rājā| cak‍karatanaṃ vattaiti, catūhi sampatticak‍kaihi vattati, taihi ca paraṃ vattaiti, parahitāya ca iriyāpathacak‍kānaṃ vattau aitasmiṃ atthīti cak‍kavattī| rājāti caittha sāmañ‍ñaṃ, cak‍kavattīti visaisaṃ| dhammaina caratīti dhammikau| ñāyaina samaina vattatīti atthau| dhammainaiva raj‍jaṃ labhitvā rājā jātauti dhammarājā| parahitadhammacaraṇaina vā dhammikau, attahitadhammacaraṇaina dhammarājā, caturantāya issarauti cāturantau, catusamuddantāya catubbidhadīpavibhūsitāya ca pathaviyā issarauti atthau| ajjhattaṃ kaupādipac‍catthikai, bahiddhā ca sabbarājānau adaṇḍaina asatthaina vijaisīti vijitāvī| janapadai thāvarabhāvaṃ dhuvabhāvaṃ pattau, na sak‍kā kainaci tatau cālaituṃ janapadau vā tamhi thāvariyappattau anuyuttau sakammaniratau acalau asampavaidhīti janapadatthāvariyappattau|

cak‍karatanaṃ, hatthiratanaṃ, assaratanaṃ, maṇiratanaṃ, itthiratanaṃ, gahapatiratanaṃ, pariṇāyakaratananti imaihi sattahi ratanaihi samupaitauti sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| taisu hi rājā cak‍kavatti cak‍karatanaina ajitaṃ jināti, hatthiassaratanaihi vijitai sukhainaiva anuvicarati, pariṇāyakaratanaina vijitamanurakkhati, saisaihi upabhaugasukhamanubhavati| paṭhamaina cassa ussāhasattiyaugau, pacchimaina mantasattiyaugau, hatthiassagahapatiratanaihi pabhūsattiyaugau suparipuṇṇau hauti, itthimaṇiratanaihi tividhasattiyaugaphalaṃ| sau itthimaṇiratanaihi paribhaugasukhamanubhavati, saisaihi upabhaugasukhaṃ| visaisatau cassa purimāni tīṇi adausakusalamūlajanitakammānubhāvaina sampaj‍janti, majjhimāni alaubhakusalamūlajanitakammānubhāvaina, pacchimamaikaṃ amauhakusalamūlajanitakammānubhāvaināti vaiditabbaṃ padaisaraj‍jassāti  khuddakaraj‍jassa|

aitadahausīti attanau sampattiyau pac‍cavaikkhantassa pacchimai cak‍kavattikālai aitaṃ ‘‘kissa nu khau mai idaṃ kammassa phala’’ntiādikaṃ ahausi| sabbatthakamaiva tasmiṃ tasmimpi bhavai aitadahausiyaiva| tatthāyaṃ cak‍kavattikālavasaina yaujanā| aivaṃmahiddhikauti maṇiratanahatthiratanādippamukhāya kausavāhanasampattiyā janapadatthāvariyappattiyā ca aivaṃmahiddhikau| aivaṃmahānubhāvauti cak‍karatanādisaman‍nāgamaina kassacipi pīḷaṃ akarauntauva sabbarājūhi sirasā sampaṭicchitasāsanavaihāsagamanādīhi aivaṃ mahānubhāvau| dānassāti an‍nādidaiyyadhammaparic‍cāgassa| damassāti cakkhādiindriyadamanassa caiva samādhānavasaina rāgādikilaisadamanassa ca| saṃyamassāti kāyavacīsaṃyamassa| tattha yaṃ samādhānavasaina kilaisadamanaṃ, taṃ bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, tañ‍ca khau maittābrahmavihārabhūtaṃ idhādhippaitaṃ| tasmiñ‍ca upacārappanābhaidaina duvidhai yaṃ appanāppattaṃ, tainassa yathāvuttāsu dvīsu jhānabhūmīsu upapatti ahausi| itaraina tividhaināpi yathārahaṃ pattacak‍kavattiādibhāvauti vaiditabbaṃ|

iti bhagavā attānaṃ kāyasakkhi katvā puñ‍ñānaṃ vipākamahantataṃ pakāsaitvā idāni tamaivatthaṃ gāthābandhaina dassaintau ‘‘puñ‍ñamaivā’’tiādimāha| tattha puñ‍ñamaiva sau sikkhaiyyāti yau atthakāmau kulaputtau, sau puñ‍ñaphalanibbattanatau, attanau santānaṃ punanatau ca ‘‘puñ‍ña’’nti laddhanāmaṃ tividhaṃ kusalamaiva sikkhaiyya nivaisaiyya upacinaiyya pasavaiyyāti atthau| āyatagganti vipulaphalatāya uḷāraphalatāya āyataggaṃ, piyamanāpaphalatāya vā āyatiṃ uttamanti āyataggaṃ, āyaina vā yaunisaumanasikārādippac‍cayaina uḷāratamaina agganti āyataggaṃ| takārau padasandhikarau| atha vā āyaina puñ‍ñaphalaina aggaṃ padhānanti āyataggaṃ| tatau aiva sukhudrayaṃ sukhavipākanti atthau|

katamaṃ pana taṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, kathañ‍ca naṃ sikkhaiyyāti āha ‘‘dānañ‍ca samacariyañ‍ca, maittacittañ‍ca bhāvayai’’ti| tattha samacariyanti kāyavisamādīni vaj‍jaitvā kāyasamādicaritaṃ, suvisuddhaṃ sīlanti atthau| bhāvayaiti attanau santānai uppādaiyya vaḍḍhaiyya| aitai dhammaiti aitai dānādikai sucaritadhammai| sukhasamuddayaiti sukhānisaṃsai, ānisaṃsaphalampi naisaṃ sukhamaivāti dassaiti| abyāpaj‍jaṃ sukhaṃ laukanti kāmacchandādibyāpādavirahitattā abyāpaj‍jaṃ niddukkhaṃ, parapīḷābhāvai pana vattabbaṃ natthi| jhānasamāpattivasaina sukhabahulattā sukhaṃ, aikantasukhañ‍ca brahmalaukaṃ jhānapuñ‍ñānaṃ, itarapuñ‍ñānaṃ pana tadañ‍ñaṃ sampattibhavasaṅkhātaṃ sukhaṃ laukaṃ paṇḍitau sappañ‍ñau upapaj‍jati upaiti| iti imasmiṃ suttai gāthāsu ca vaṭṭasampatti aiva kathitā|

dutiyasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. ubhayatthasuttavaṇṇanā

23. tatiyai bhāvitauti uppāditau ca vaḍḍhitau ca| bahulīkatauti punappunaṃ katau| atthauti hitaṃ| tañhi araṇīyatau upagantabbatau atthauti vuc‍cati| samadhigayha tiṭṭhatīti sammā pariggahaitvā avijahitvā vattati| diṭṭhadhammikanti diṭṭhadhammau vuc‍cati pac‍cakkhabhūtau attabhāvau, diṭṭhadhammai bhavaṃ diṭṭhadhammikaṃ, idhalaukapariyāpan‍nanti atthau| samparāyikanti dhammavasaina samparaitabbatau  samparāyau, paralaukau, samparāyai bhavaṃ samparāyikaṃ, paralaukapariyāpan‍nanti vuttaṃ hauti|

kau panaisa diṭṭhadhammikau nāma atthau, kau vā samparāyikauti? saṅkhaipaina tāva yaṃ idhalaukasukhaṃ, yañ‍caitarahi idhalaukasukhāvahaṃ, ayaṃ diṭṭhadhammikau atthau| saiyyathidaṃ – gahaṭṭhānaṃ tāva idha yaṃ kiñ‍ci vittūpakaraṇaṃ, anākulakammantatā, āraugyasaṃvidhānaṃ, vatthuvisadakiriyāyaugavihitāni sippāyatanavij‍jāṭṭhānāni saṅgahitaparijanatāti aivamādi| pabbajitānaṃ pana yai imai jīvitaparikkhārā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā| taisaṃ akicchalābhau, tattha ca saṅkhāya paṭisaivanā, saṅkhāya parivaj‍janā, vatthuvisadakiriyā, appicchatā, santuṭṭhi, pavivaikau, asaṃsaggauti aivamādi| patirūpadaisavāsasappurisūpanissayasaddhammassavanayaunisaumanasikārādayau  pana ubhayaisaṃ sādhāraṇā ubhayānurūpā cāti vaiditabbā|

appamādauti aittha appamādau pamādappaṭipakkhatau vaiditabbau| kau panaisa pamādau nāma? pamaj‍janākārau| vuttaṃ haitaṃ –

‘‘tattha katamau pamādau? kāyaduc‍caritai vā vacīduc‍caritai vā manauduc‍caritai vā pañ‍casu vā kāmaguṇaisu cittassa vaussaggau vaussaggānuppādanaṃ kusalānaṃ vā dhammānaṃ bhāvanāya asak‍kac‍cakiriyatā asātac‍cakiriyatā anaṭṭhitakiriyatā aulīnavuttitā nikkhittachandatā nikkhittadhuratā anāsaivanā abhāvanā abahulīkammaṃ anadhiṭṭhānaṃ ananuyaugaupamādau| yau aivarūpau pamādau pamaj‍janā pamaj‍jitattaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati pamādau’’ti (vibha॰ 846)|

tasmā vuttappaṭipakkhatau appamādau vaiditabbau| atthatau hi sau satiyā avippavāsau, nic‍caṃ upaṭṭhitassatiyā aitaṃ nāmaṃ| aparai pana ‘‘satisampajañ‍ñayaugaina pavattā cattārau arūpinau khandhā appamādau’’ti vadanti|

‘‘bhāvitau bahūlīkatau’’ti vuttaṃ, kathaṃ panāyaṃ appamādau bhāvaitabbauti? na appamādabhāvanā nāma visuṃ aikabhāvanā atthi| yā hi kāci puñ‍ñakiriyā kusalakiriyā, sabbā sā appamādabhāvanātvaiva vaiditabbā| visaisatau pana vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ saraṇagamanaṃ kāyikavācasikasaṃvarañ‍ca  upādāya sabbā sīlabhāvanā, sabbā samādhibhāvanā, sabbā pañ‍ñābhāvanā, sabbā kusalabhāvanā, anavaj‍jabhāvanā, appamādabhāvanāti vaiditabbā| ‘‘appamādau’’ti hi idaṃ mahantaṃ atthaṃ dīpaiti, mahantaṃ atthaṃ pariggahaitvā tiṭṭhati| sakalampi taipiṭakaṃ buddhavacanaṃ āharitvā appamādapadassa atthaṃ katvā kathaintau dhammakathikau ‘‘atitthaina pakkhandau’’ti na vattabbau| kasmā? appamādapadassa mahantabhāvatau| tathā hi sammāsambuddhau kusinārāyaṃ yamakasālānamantarai parinibbānasamayai nipan‍nau abhisambaudhitau paṭṭhāya pañ‍cacattālīsāya vassaisu attanā bhāsitaṃ dhammaṃ  aikaina padaina saṅgahaitvā dassaintau – ‘‘appamādaina sampādaithā’’ti bhikkhūnaṃ auvādamadāsi| tathā ca vuttaṃ –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici jaṅgalānaṃ pāṇānaṃ padajātāni, sabbāni tāni hatthipadai samaudhānaṃ gacchanti, hatthipadaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati yadidaṃ mahantaṭṭhaina; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbaitai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā, appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyatī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.300)|

gāthāsu appamādaṃ pasaṃsantīti dānādipuñ‍ñakiriyāsu appamādaṃ appamaj‍janaṃ paṇḍitā sappañ‍ñā buddhādayau pasaṃsanti, vaṇṇainti thaumainti| kasmā? yasmā appamattau ubhau atthai adhigaṇhāti paṇḍitau| kai pana tai ubhau atthāti āha – ‘‘diṭṭhai dhammai ca yau atthau, yau catthau samparāyikau’’ti, aivamaittha padayaujanā vaiditabbā| idhāpi diṭṭhai dhammai ca yau atthauti gahaṭṭhassa tāva ‘‘anavaj‍jāni kammāni, anākulā ca kammantā’’tiādinā nayaina vuttau kasigaurakkhādividhinā laddhabbau atthau, pabbajitassa pana avippaṭisārādiatthau vaiditabbau| yau catthau samparāyikauti pana ubhayaisampi dhammacariyāva vuttāti vaiditabbā| atthābhisamayāti duvidhassapi atthassa hitassa paṭilābhā, laddhabbaina samiti saṅgati samaudhānanti samayau, lābhau| samayau aiva abhisamayau, abhimukhabhāvaina vā samayau abhisamayauti aivamaittha abhisamayau vaiditabbau| dhitisampan‍nattā dhīrau| tatiyaina caittha attha-saddaina paramatthassa nibbānassāpi saṅgahau vaiditabbau| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva| iti imasmiṃ suttai vaṭṭasampatti aiva kathitā| gāthāyaṃ pana vivaṭṭassapi saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| tathā hi vuttaṃ –

‘‘appamādau amatapadaṃ, pamādau mac‍cunau padaṃ|

appamattā na mīyanti, yai pamattā yathā matā||

‘‘aivaṃ  visaisatau ñatvā, appamādamhi paṇḍitā|

appamādai pamaudanti, ariyānaṃ gaucarai ratā||

‘‘tai  jhāyinau sātatikā, nic‍caṃ daḷhaparak‍kamā|

phusanti dhīrā nibbānaṃ, yaugakkhaimaṃ anuttara’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 21-23)|

tasmā  ‘‘atthābhisamayā’’ti aittha laukuttaratthavasainapi atthau vaiditabbau|

tatiyasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. aṭṭhipuñ‍jasuttavaṇṇanā

24. catutthai aikapuggalassāti aittha puggalauti ayaṃ vauhārakathā| buddhassa hi bhagavatau duvidhā daisanā sammutidaisanā ca paramatthadaisanā cāti| tattha ‘‘puggalau, sattau, itthī, purisau, khattiyau, brāhmaṇau, daivau, mārau’’ti aivarūpā sammutidaisanā| ‘‘anic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ, anattā, khandhā, dhātu, āyatanā, satipaṭṭhānā’’ti aivarūpā paramatthadaisanā| tattha bhagavā yai sammutivasaina daisanaṃ sutvā visaisamadhigantuṃ samatthā, naisaṃ sammutidaisanaṃ daisaiti| yai pana paramatthavasaina daisanaṃ sutvā visaisamadhigantuṃ samatthā, taisaṃ paramatthadaisanaṃ daisaiti|

tatthāyaṃ upamā – yathā hi daisabhāsākusalau tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ atthasaṃvaṇṇanakau ācariyau yai damiḷabhāsāya vuttai atthaṃ jānanti, taisaṃ damiḷabhāsāya ācikkhati| yai andhakabhāsādīsu añ‍ñatarāya, taisaṃ tāya tāya bhāsāya| aivaṃ tai māṇavakā chaikaṃ byattaṃ ācariyamāgamma khippamaiva sippaṃ uggaṇhanti| tattha ācariyau viya buddhau bhagavā, tayau vaidā viya kathaitabbabhāvai ṭhitāni tīṇi piṭakāni, daisabhāsākausal‍lamiva sammutiparamatthakausal‍laṃ, nānādaisabhāsā māṇavakā viya sammutiparamatthavasaina paṭivijjhanasamatthā vainaiyyā, ācariyassa damiḷabhāsādiācikkhanaṃ viya bhagavatau sammutiparamatthavasaina daisanā vaiditabbā| āha caittha –

‘‘duvai sac‍cāni akkhāsi, sambuddhau vadataṃ varau|

sammutiṃ paramatthañ‍ca, tatiyaṃ nūpalabbhati||

‘‘saṅkaitavacanaṃ  sac‍caṃ, laukasammutikāraṇā|

paramatthavacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, dhammānaṃ bhūtakāraṇā||

‘‘tasmā vauhārakusalassa, laukanāthassa satthunau|

sammutiṃ vauharantassa, musāvādau na jāyatī’’ti||

apica aṭṭhahi kāraṇaihi bhagavā puggalakathaṃ kathaiti – hirauttappadīpanatthaṃ, kammassakatādīpanatthaṃ, pac‍cattapurisakāradīpanatthaṃ,, ānantariyadīpanatthaṃ, brahmavihāradīpanatthaṃ, pubbainivāsadīpanatthaṃ, dakkhiṇāvisuddhidīpanatthaṃ, laukasammutiyā appahānatthaṃ, cāti| ‘‘khandhadhātuāyatanāni hiriyanti auttappantī’’ti hi vuttai mahājanau na jānāti, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati, paṭisattu vā hauti – ‘‘kimidaṃ khandhadhātuāyatanāni hiriyanti auttappanti nāmā’’ti? ‘‘itthī hiriyati auttappati, purisau, khattiyau, brāhmaṇau, daivau, mārau’’ti pana vuttai jānāti, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati, na paṭisattu vā hauti| tasmā bhagavā hirauttappadīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘khandhā kammassakā, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| tasmā kammassakatādīpanatthampi puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘vaiḷuvanādayau mahāvihārā khandhaihi kārāpitā, dhātūhi āyatanaihī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| tathā ‘‘khandhā mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupainti, pitaraṃ, arahantaṃ, ruhiruppādakammaṃ, saṅghabhaidakammaṃ karaunti, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| ‘‘khandhā maittāyanti, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| ‘‘khandhā pubbainivāsaṃ anussaranti, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi aisaiva nayau| tasmā bhagavā pac‍cattapurisakāradīpanatthaṃ ānantariyadīpanatthaṃ brahmavihāradīpanatthaṃ pubbainivāsadīpanatthañ‍ca puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

‘‘khandhā dānaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti, dhātuyau āyatanānī’’ti vuttaipi mahājanau na jānāti, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati, paṭisattu vā hauti ‘‘kimidaṃ khandhā dhātuyau āyatanāni paṭiggaṇhanti nāmā’’ti? ‘‘puggalā paṭiggaṇhantī’’ti pana vuttai jānāti, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati, na paṭisattu vā hauti| tasmā bhagavā dakkhiṇāvisuddhidīpanatthaṃ puggalakathaṃ kathaiti|

laukasammutiñ‍ca  buddhā bhagavantau na pajahanti, laukasamañ‍ñāya laukaniruttiyā laukābhilāpai ṭhitāyaiva dhammaṃ daisainti| tasmā bhagavā laukasammutiyā appahānatthampi puggalakathaṃ kathaiti| sau idhāpi laukavauhāravasaina daisaitabbamatthaṃ dassaintau ‘‘aikapuggalassā’’tiādimāha|

tattha aikapuggalassāti aikasattassa| kappanti mahākappaṃ| yadipi ac‍cantasaṃyaugai idaṃ upayaugavacanaṃ, yattha pana sattānaṃ sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ sambhavati, tassa vasaina gahaitabbaṃ| aṭṭhikaṅkalauti aṭṭhibhāgau| ‘‘aṭṭhikhalau’’tipi  paṭhanti, aṭṭhisañ‍cayauti atthau| aṭṭhipuñ‍jauti aṭṭhisamūhau| aṭṭhirāsīti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| kaici pana ‘‘kaṭippamāṇatau haiṭṭhā samūhau kaṅkalau nāma, tatau upari yāva tālappamāṇaṃ puñ‍jau, tatau upari rāsī’’ti vadanti| taṃ taisaṃ matimattaṃ| sabbamaitaṃ samūhassaiva pariyāyavacanaṃ vaipul‍lassaiva upamābhāvaina āhaṭattā|

sacai saṃhārakau assāti avippakiraṇavasaina saṃharitvā ṭhapaitā kauci yadi siyāti parikappanavasaina vadati| sambhatañ‍ca na vinassaiyyāti tathā kainaci sambhatañ‍ca taṃ aṭṭhikaṅkalaṃ antaradhānābhāvaina pūtibhūtaṃ cuṇṇavicuṇṇañ‍ca ahutvā sacai na vinassaiyyāti parikappanavasainaiva vadati| ayañhaittha atthau – bhikkhavai, aikassa sattassa kammakilaisaihi aparāparuppattivasaina aikaṃ mahākappaṃ sandhāvantassa saṃsarantassa aivaṃ mahāaṭṭhisañ‍cayau bhavaiyya, ārauhapariṇāhaihi yattakauyaṃ vaipul‍lapabbatau| sacai panassa kauci saṃharitvā ṭhapaitā bhavaiyya, sambhatañ‍ca taṃ sacai avinassantaṃ tiṭṭhaiyyāti| ayañ‍ca nayau nibbutappadīpai viya bhij‍janasabhāvai kaḷaivaranikkhaiparahitai aupapātikattabhāvai sabbaina sabbaṃ anaṭṭhikai ca khuddakattabhāvai vaj‍jaitvā vuttau| kaici pana ‘‘parikappanavasaina imassa nayassa āhaṭattā taisampi yadi siyā aṭṭhikaṅkalau, taināpi sahaiva ayaṃ aṭṭhipuñ‍japarimāṇau vuttau’’ti vadanti| aparai pana ‘‘nayidamaivaṃ labbhamānassaiva aṭṭhipuñ‍jassa vasaina sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina paricchinditvā imassa parimāṇassa vuttatā| tasmā vuttanayainaiva atthau gahaitabbau’’ti|

gāthāsu  mahaisināti mahantai sīlakkhandhādayau aisati gavaisatīti mahaisī, sammāsambuddhau| ‘‘iti vuttaṃ mahaisinā’’ti ca bhagavā ‘‘dasabalasaman‍nāgatau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau’’tiādīsu viya attānaṃ añ‍ñaṃ viya katvā dassaiti| vaipul‍lauti rājagahaṃ parivāraitvā ṭhitaisu pañ‍casu pabbataisu vipulabhāvatau vaipul‍lauti laddhanāmau| tatau aiva mahā, ṭhitadisābhāgavasaina uttarau gijjhakūṭassa| giribbajaiti giribbajapuranāmakassa rājagahassa samīpai|

aittāvatā  bhagavā ‘‘aittakaināpi kālaina anupacchin‍nabhavamūlassa apariñ‍ñātavatthukassa puthuj‍janassa ayamīdisī kaṭasivaḍḍhanā’’ti vaṭṭai ādīnavaṃ dassaitvā idāni yaisaṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā andhaputhuj‍janassa aivaṃ kaṭasivaḍḍhanā, tāni ariyasac‍cāni diṭṭhavatau ariyapuggalassa ayaṃ natthīti dassaintau ‘‘yatau ca ariyasac‍cānī’’tiādimāha|

tattha yatauti yadā| ariyasac‍cānīti araṇīyatau ariyāni, avitathabhāvaina sac‍cāni cāti ariyasac‍cāni, ariyabhāvakarāni vā sac‍cāni ariyasac‍cāni, ariyaihi vā buddhādīhi paṭivijjhitabbāni sac‍cāni ariyasac‍cāni| atha vā ariyassa sac‍cāni ariyasac‍cāni| sadaivakaina hi laukaina saraṇanti araṇīyatau ariyau bhagavā, taina sayambhuñāṇaina diṭṭhattā tassa sac‍cānīti ariyasac‍cāni| sammappañ‍ñāya passatīti sammā haitunā ñāyaina vipassanāpañ‍ñāsahitāya maggapañ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāpahānasacchikiriyābhāvanābhisamayavasaina passati| dukkhantiādi ariyasac‍cānaṃ sarūpadassanaṃ| tattha anaikūpaddavādhiṭṭhānatāya kucchitabhāvatau bālajanaparikappitadhuvasubhasukhattavirahaina tucchabhāvatau ca dukkhaṃ| dukkhaṃ samuppaj‍jati aitaināti dukkhasamuppādau, dukkhasamudayau| dukkhaṃ atik‍kamati aitaina ārammaṇappac‍cayabhūtaina, aittha vāti dukkhassa atik‍kamau, nibbānaṃ| ārakattā kilaisaihi araṇīyatau ca ariyau| sammādiṭṭhiādīnaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ vasaina aṭṭhaṅgikau| māraintau kilaisai gacchati, nibbānatthikaihi maggīyati, sayaṃ vā nibbānaṃ maggatīti maggau| tatau aiva dukkhassa upasamaṃ niraudhaṃ gacchatīti dukkhūpasamagāmī| yatau sammappañ‍ñāya passatīti sambandhau|

sa  sattakkhattuṃ paramaṃ, sandhāvitvāna puggalauti sau aivaṃ catusac‍cadassāvī ariyapuggalau sautāpan‍nau sabbamudindriyau samānau sattavāraparamaṃyaiva bhavādīsu aparāparuppattivasaina sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā| aikabījī, kaulaṃkaulau, sattakkhattuparamauti indriyānaṃ tikkhamajjhimamudubhāvaina tayau hi sautāpan‍nā| taisu sabbamudindriyassa vasainidaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘sa sattakkhattuṃ paramaṃ, sandhāvitvānā’’ti| dukkhassantakarau hautīti vaṭṭadukkhassa antakarau pariyausānakarau hauti| kathaṃ? sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayā anupubbaina aggamaggaṃ adhigantvā niravasaisānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ khaipanāti arahattaphalainaiva daisanāya kūṭaṃ gaṇhi|

catutthasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. musāvādasuttavaṇṇanā

25. pañ‍camai  aikadhammaṃ atītassāti kā uppatti? bhagavatau bhikkhusaṅghassa ca mahālābhasak‍kārau uppaj‍ji, titthiyānaṃ parihāyi| tai hatalābhasak‍kārā nippabhā nittaijā issāpakatā ciñ‍camāṇavikaṃ nāma paribbājikaṃ uyyaujaisuṃ – ‘‘aihi, tvaṃ bhagini, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhassū’’ti| sā bhagavantaṃ catuparisamajjhai dhammaṃ daisaintaṃ upagantvā abhūtaina abbhācikkhitvā sak‍kainassā abhūtabhāvai pakāsitai mahājanaina ‘‘dhī kāḷakaṇṇī’’ti vihāratau nik‍kaḍḍhāpitā pathaviyā vivarai din‍nai avīcijālānaṃ indhanaṃ hutvāva avīcinirayai nibbatti, bhiyyausaumattāya titthiyānaṃ lābhasak‍kārau parihāyi| bhikkhū dhammasabhāyaṃ kathaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisuṃ ‘‘āvusau, ciñ‍camāṇavikā aivaṃ uḷāraguṇaṃ aggadakkhiṇaiyyaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ abhūtaina ak‍kausitvā mahāvināsaṃ pattā’’ti| satthā āgantvā ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā’’ti pucchitvā ‘‘imāya nāmā’’ti vuttai ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, idānaiva, pubbaipi sā maṃ abhūtaina ak‍kausitvā mahāvināsaṃ pattāyaivā’’ti mahāpadumajātakampi vitthāraitvā upari dhammaṃ daisaintau imissā aṭṭhuppattiyā ‘‘aikadhammaṃ atītassā’’ti idaṃ suttaṃ daisaisi|

tattha aikadhammanti aikaṃ vacīsac‍casaṅkhātaṃ dhammaṃ| atītassāti yā sā aṭṭha anariyavauhārai vaj‍jaitvā aṭṭhasu ariyavauhāraisu patiṭṭhāpanatthaṃ ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhaṇai, nālika’’nti ariyaihi ṭhapitā mariyādā, taṃ atik‍kamitvā ṭhitassa| purisau aiva puggalauti purisapuggalau, tassa| akaraṇīyanti kātuṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ| sampajānamusāvādī hi puggalau kiñ‍ci pāpakammaṃ katvā ‘‘idaṃ nāma tayā kata’’nti vuttai ‘‘na mayā kata’’nti  musāvādainaiva pariharissati| aivañ‍ca paṭipaj‍jantau kiñ‍ci pāpakammaṃ karautiyaiva, na tattha laj‍jati sac‍camariyādāya samatik‍kantattā| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘katamaṃ aikadhammaṃ, yadidaṃ, bhikkhavai, sampajānamusāvādau’’ti|

gāthāyaṃ musāvādissāti musā abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ paraisaṃ viñ‍ñāpanavasaina vadanasīlassa| yassa dasasu vacanaisu aikampi sac‍caṃ natthi, aivarūpai vattabbamaiva natthi| jantunauti sattassa| sattau hi jāyanaṭṭhaina ‘‘jantū’’ti vuc‍cati| vitiṇṇaparalaukassāti vissaṭṭhaparalaukassa| īdisau hi manussasampatti daivalaukasampatti avasānai nibbānasampattīti imā tissaupi sampattiyau na passati| natthi pāpanti tassa tādisassa idaṃ nāma pāpaṃ na kattabbanti natthīti|

pañ‍camasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. dānasuttavaṇṇanā

26. chaṭṭhai  aivañ‍caiti aittha aivanti upamākārai nipātau, caiti parikappanai| sattāti rūpādīsu sattā visattā| jānaiyyunti bujjhaiyyuṃ| dānasaṃvibhāgassāti yāya hi caitanāya an‍nādidaiyyadhammaṃ saṃharitvā anukampāpūjāsu añ‍ñataravasaina paraisaṃ dīyati, taṃ dānaṃ| yāya pana attanā paribhuñ‍jitabbabhāvaina gahitavatthussa aikadaisau saṃvibhajitvā dīyati, ayaṃ saṃvibhāgau| vipākanti phalaṃ| yathāhaṃ jānāmīti yathā ahaṃ jānāmi| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – tiracchānagatassapi dānaṃ datvā attabhāvasatai pavattasukhavipac‍canavasaina sataguṇā dakkhiṇā hautīti aivamādinā, bhikkhavai, yaina pakāraina ahaṃ dānassa saṃvibhāgassa ca vipākaṃ kammavipākaṃ ñāṇabalaina pac‍cakkhatau jānāmi, aivaṃ imai sattā yadi jānaiyyunti| na adatvā bhuñ‍jaiyyunti yaṃ bhuñ‍jitabbayuttakaṃ attanau atthi,  tatau paraisaṃ na adatvā macchariyacittaina ca taṇhālaubhavasaina ca bhuñ‍jaiyyuṃ, datvāva bhuñ‍jaiyyuṃ| na ca naisaṃ macchairamalaṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhaiyyāti attanau sampattīnaṃ paraihi sādhāraṇabhāvāsahanalakkhaṇaṃ cittassa pabhassarabhāvadūsakānaṃ upak‍kilaisabhūtānaṃ kaṇhadhammānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ macchairamalaṃ| atha vā yathāvuttamacchairañ‍caiva añ‍ñampi dānantarāyakaraṃ issālaubhadausādimalañ‍ca naisaṃ sattānaṃ cittaṃ yathā dānacaitanā  na pavattati, na vā suparisuddhā hauti, aivaṃ pariyādāya paritau gahaitvā abhibhavitvā na tiṭṭhaiyya| kau hi sammadaiva dānaphalaṃ jānantau attanau cittai macchairamalassa aukāsaṃ dadaiyya|

yaupi naisaṃ assa carimau ālaupauti naisaṃ sattānaṃ yau sabbapacchimakau ālaupau siyā| carimaṃ kabaḷanti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – imai sattā pakatiyā yattakaihi ālaupaihi sayaṃ yāpaiyyuṃ, taisu aikamaiva ālaupaṃ attanau atthāya ṭhapaitvā tadañ‍ñai sabbai ālaupai āgatāgatānaṃ atthikānaṃ datvā yau ṭhapitau ālaupau assa, sau idha carimau ālaupau nāma| tataupi na asaṃvibhajitvā bhuñ‍jaiyyuṃ, sacai naisaṃ paṭiggāhakā assūti naisaṃ sattānaṃ paṭiggāhakā yadi siyuṃ, tataupi yathāvuttacarimālaupataupi saṃvibhajitvāva aikadaisaṃ datvāva bhuñ‍jaiyyuṃ, yathāhaṃ dānasaṃvibhāgassa vipākaṃ pac‍cakkhatau jānāmi, aivaṃ yadi jānaiyyunti| yasmā ca khautiādinā kammaphalassa appac‍cakkhabhāvatau aivamaitai sattā dānasaṃvibhāgaisu na pavattantīti yathādhippaitamatthaṃ kāraṇaina sampaṭipādaiti| aitainaiva taisaṃ tadañ‍ñapuñ‍ñaisu ca appaṭipattiyā apuñ‍ñaisu ca paṭipattiyā kāraṇaṃ dassitanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

gāthāsu yathāvuttaṃ mahaisināti mahaisinā bhagavatā ‘‘tiracchānagatai dānaṃ datvā sataguṇā dakkhiṇā  pāṭikaṅkhitabbā’ tiādinā, idhaiva vā ‘‘aivaṃ cai sattā jānaiyyu’’ntiādinā yathāvuttaṃ, ñāṇacāraina taṃ yathāvuttaṃ cittaṃ ñātanti atthau| vipākaṃ saṃvibhāgassāti saṃvibhāgassapi vipākaṃ, kau pana vādau dānassa| yathā hauti mahapphalanti yathā sau vipākau mahantaṃ phalaṃ hauti, aivaṃ imai sattā yadi jānaiyyunti sambandhau| vinaiyya macchairamalanti macchariyamalaṃ apanaitvā kammaphalasaddhāya ratanattayasaddhāya  ca visaisatau pasan‍naina cittaina yaisu kilaisaihi ārakattā ariyaisu sīlādiguṇasampan‍naisu din‍naṃ appakampi dānaṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti, taisu yuttakālaina daj‍juṃ dadaiyyuṃ|

mahapphalabhāvakaraṇatau dakkhiṇaṃ arahantīti dakkhiṇaiyyā, sammāpaṭipan‍nā, taisu dakkhiṇaiyyaisu| dakkhiṇaṃ paralaukaṃ saddahitvā dātabbaṃ daiyyadhammaṃ yathā  taṃ dānaṃ hauti mahādānaṃ, aivaṃ datvā| atha vā bahunau an‍naṃ datvā, kathaṃ pana an‍naṃ dātabbanti āha ‘‘dakkhiṇaiyyaisu dakkhiṇa’’nti| itau manussattā manussattabhāvatau cutā paṭisandhivasaina saggaṃ gacchanti dāyakā| kāmakāminauti kāmaitabbānaṃ uḷārānaṃ daivabhaugānaṃ paṭiladdharūpavibhavaina kammunā upagamanai sādhukāritāya kāmakāminau sabbakāmasamaṅginau| maudanti yathāruci paricāraintīti atthau|

chaṭṭhasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. maittābhāvanāsuttavaṇṇanā

27. sattamai yāni kānicīti anavasaisapariyādānaṃ| aupadhikāni puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūnīti
