taisaṃ niyamanaṃ| tattha upadhi vuc‍canti khandhā, upadhissa karaṇaṃ sīlaṃ aitaisaṃ, upadhippayaujanāni vā aupadhikāni| sampattibhavai attabhāvajanakāni paṭisandhipavattivipākadāyakāni| puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūnīti puñ‍ñakiriyā ca tā taisaṃ taisaṃ phalānisaṃsānaṃ vatthūni cāti puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūni| tāni pana saṅkhaipatau dānamayaṃ, sīlamayaṃ, bhāvanāmayanti tividhāni haunti| tattha yaṃ vattabbaṃ, taṃ paratau tikanipātavaṇṇanāyaṃ āvi bhavissati| maittāya caitauvimuttiyāti maittābhāvanāvasaina paṭiladdhatikacatuk‍kajjhānasamāpattiyā| ‘‘maittā’’ti hi vuttai upacāraupi labbhati appanāpi, ‘‘caitauvimuttī’’ti pana vuttai appanājhānamaiva labbhati| tañhi nīvaraṇādipac‍canīkadhammatau cittassa suṭṭhu vimuttibhāvaina caitauvimuttīti vuc‍cati| kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasinti maittābrahmavihārassa sauḷasabhāgaṃ aupadhikāni puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūni na agghanti| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – maittāya caitauvimuttiyā yau vipākau, taṃ sauḷasa kauṭṭhāsai katvā  tatau  aikaṃ puna sauḷasa kauṭṭhāsai katvā tattha yau aikakauṭṭhāsau, na taṃ añ‍ñāni aupadhikāni puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūni agghantīti| adhiggahaitvāti abhibhavitvā| bhāsataiti upak‍kilaisavisuddhiyā dippati| tapataiti tatau aiva anavasaisai paṭipakkhadhammai santapati| viraucatīti ubhayasampattiyā viraucati| maittā hi caitauvimutti candālaukasaṅkhātā vigatūpak‍kilaisā juṇhā viya dippati, ātapau viya andhakāraṃ pac‍canīkadhammai vidhamantī  tapati, ausadhitārakā viya vij‍jautamānā viraucati ca|

saiyyathāpīti aupammadassanatthai nipātau| tārakarūpānanti jautīnaṃ| candiyāti candassa ayanti candī, tassā candiyā, pabhāya juṇhāyāti atthau| vassānanti vassānaṃ bahuvasaina laddhavauhārassa utunau| pacchimai māsaiti kattikamāsai| saradasamayaiti saradakālai| assayujakattikamāsā hi laukai ‘‘saradautū’’ti vuc‍canti| viddhaiti ubbiddhai, maighavigamaina dūrībhūtaiti atthau| tainaivāha ‘‘vigatavalāhakai’’ti| daivaiti ākāsai| nabhaṃ abbhussak‍kamānauti udayaṭṭhānatau ākāsaṃ ul‍laṅghantau| tamagatanti tamaṃ| abhivihac‍cāti abhihantvā vidhamitvā| ausadhitārakāti ussan‍nā pabhā aitāya dhīyati, ausadhīnaṃ vā anubalappadāyikattā ausadhīti laddhanāmā tārakā|

aitthāha – kasmā pana bhagavatā samānaipi aupadhikabhāvai maittā itaraihi aupadhikapuñ‍ñaihi visaisaitvā vuttāti? vuc‍catai – saiṭṭhaṭṭhaina niddausabhāvaina ca sattaisu suppaṭipattibhāvatau| saiṭṭhā hi aitai vihārā, sabbasattaisu sammāpaṭipattibhūtāni yadidaṃ maittājhānāni| yathā ca brahmānau niddausacittā viharanti, aivaṃ aitaihi saman‍nāgatā yauginau brahmasamāva hutvā viharanti| tathā himai ‘‘brahmavihārā’’ti vuc‍canti| iti saiṭṭhaṭṭhaina niddausabhāvaina ca sattaisu suppaṭipattibhāvatau maittāva itaraihi aupadhikapuñ‍ñaihi visaisaitvā vuttā|

aivampi kasmā maittāva aivaṃ visaisaitvā vuttā? itaraisaṃ brahmavihārānaṃ adhiṭṭhānabhāvatau dānādīnaṃ sabbaisaṃ kalyāṇadhammānaṃ paripūrikattā ca| ayañhi  sattaisu hitākārappavattilakkhaṇā maittā, hitūpasaṃhārasā, āghātavinayapac‍cupaṭṭhānā| yadi anaudhisau bhāvitā bahulīkatā, atha sukhainaiva karuṇādibhāvanā sampaj‍jantīti maittā itaraisaṃ brahmavihārānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ| tathā hi sattaisu hitajjhāsayatāya sati naisaṃ dukkhāsahanatā, sampattivisaisānaṃ ciraṭṭhitikāmatā, pakkhapātābhāvaina sabbattha samappavattacittatā ca sukhainaiva ijjhanti| aivañ‍ca sakalalaukahitasukhavidhānādhimuttā  mahābaudhisattā ‘‘imassa dātabbaṃ, imassa na dātabba’’nti uttamavicayavasaina  vibhāgaṃ akatvā sabbasattānaṃ niravasaisasukhanidānaṃ dānaṃ dainti, hitasukhatthamaiva naisaṃ sīlaṃ samādiyanti, sīlaparipūraṇatthaṃ naikkhammaṃ bhajanti, taisaṃ hitasukhaisu asammauhatthāya pañ‍ñaṃ pariyaudapainti, hitasukhābhivaḍḍhanatthamaiva daḷhaṃ vīriyamārabhanti, uttamavīriyavasaina vīrabhāvaṃ pattāpi sattānaṃ nānappakāraṃ hitajjhāsayainaiva aparādhaṃ khamanti, ‘‘idaṃ vau dassāma, karissāmā’’tiādinā kataṃ paṭiñ‍ñātaṃ na visaṃvādainti, taisaṃ hitasukhāyaiva acalādhiṭṭhānā haunti| taisu acalāya maittāya pubbakārinau hitajjhāsayainaiva naisaṃ vippakārai udāsīnā haunti, pubbakāritāyapi na pac‍cupakāramāsisantīti| aivaṃ tai pāramiyau pūraitvā yāva dasabalacatu-vaisāraj‍ja-chaasādhāraṇañāṇa-aṭṭhārasāvaiṇikabuddhadhammappabhaidai sabbaipi kalyāṇadhammai paripūrainti| aivaṃ dānādīnaṃ sabbaisaṃ kalyāṇadhammānaṃ pāripūrikā maittāti ca imassa visaisassa dassanatthaṃ sā itaraihi visaisaitvā vuttā|

apica maittāya itaraihi aupadhikapuñ‍ñaihi mahānubhāvatā vailāmasuttaina dīpaitabbā| tattha hi yathā nāma mahatā vailāmassa dānatau aikassa sautāpan‍nassa dānaṃ mahapphalataraṃ vuttaṃ, aivaṃ sautāpan‍nasatatau aikassa sakadāgāmissa dānaṃ…pai॰… pac‍caikabuddhasatatau bhagavatau, tataupi buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa dānaṃ, tataupi cātuddisassa saṅghassa vihāradānaṃ, tataupi saraṇagamanaṃ, tataupi sīlasamādānaṃ, tataupi gaddūhanamattaṃ kālaṃ maittābhāvanā mahapphalatarā vuttā| yathāha –

‘‘yaṃ  gahapati vailāmau brāhmaṇau dānaṃ adāsi mahādānaṃ| yau caikaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍naṃ bhaujaiyya, idaṃ tatau mahapphalataraṃ| yau ca sataṃ diṭṭhisampan‍naṃ bhaujaiyya…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiṃ| yau ca antamasau gaddūhanamattampi maittacittaṃ bhāvaiyya, idaṃ tatau mahapphalatara’’nti (a॰ ni॰ 9.20)|

mahaggatapuñ‍ñabhāvaina panassā parittapuñ‍ñatau  sātisayatāya vattabbamaiva natthi| vuttañhaitaṃ ‘‘yaṃ pamāṇakataṃ kammaṃ, na taṃ tatrāvasissati, na taṃ tatrāvatiṭṭhatī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.556; saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.360)| kāmāvacarakammañhi pamāṇakataṃ nāma, mahaggatakammaṃ pana pamāṇaṃ atik‍kamitvā audhisakānaudhisakapharaṇavasaina vaḍḍhitvā katattā appamāṇakataṃ nāma| kāmāvacarakammaṃ tassa mahaggatakammassa antarā laggituṃ vā taṃ kammaṃ abhibhavitvā attanau vipākassa aukāsaṃ gahaitvā ṭhātuṃ vā na sak‍kauti, atha khau mahaggatakammamaiva taṃ parittakammaṃ mahaughau viya parittaṃ udakaṃ abhibhavitvā attanau aukāsaṃ gahaitvā tiṭṭhati, tassa vipākaṃ paṭibāhitvā sayamaiva brahmasahabyataṃ upanaitīti ayañhi tassa atthauti|

gāthāsu  yauti yau kauci gahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā| maittanti maittājhānaṃ| appamāṇanti bhāvanāvasaina ārammaṇavasaina ca appamāṇaṃ| asubhabhāvanādayau viya hi ārammaṇai aikadaisaggahaṇaṃ akatvā anavasaisapharaṇavasaina anaudhisaupharaṇavasaina ca appamāṇārammaṇatāya paguṇabhāvanāvasaina appamāṇaṃ| tanū saṃyaujanā hauntīti maittājhānaṃ pādakaṃ katvā sammasitvā haiṭṭhimai ariyamaggai adhigacchantassa sukhainaiva paṭighasaṃyaujanādayau pahīyamānā tanū haunti| taināha ‘‘passatau upadhikkhaya’’nti| ‘‘upadhikkhayau’’ti hi nibbānaṃ vuc‍cati| tañ‍cassa sacchikiriyābhisamayavasaina maggañāṇaina passati| atha vā tanū saṃyaujanā hauntīti maittājhānapadaṭṭhānāya vipassanāya anuk‍kamaina upadhikkhayasaṅkhātaṃ arahattaṃ patvā taṃ passatau pagaiva dasapi saṃyaujanā tanū haunti, pahīyantīti atthau| atha vā tanū saṃyaujanā hauntīti paṭighau caiva paṭighasampayuttasaṃyaujanā ca tanukā haunti| passatau upadhikkhayanti taisaṃyaiva kilaisūpadhīnaṃ khayasaṅkhātaṃ  maittaṃ adhigamavasaina passanta ssāti aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau|

aivaṃ kilaisappahānaṃ nibbānādhigamañ‍ca maittābhāvanāya sikhāppattamānisaṃsaṃ dassaitvā idāni añ‍ñai ānisaṃsai dassaituṃ ‘‘aikampi cai’’tiādimāha| tattha  aduṭṭhacittauti maittābalaina suṭṭhu vikkhambhitabyāpādatāya byāpādaina adūsitacittau| maittāyatīti hitapharaṇavasaina maittaṃ karauti| kusalauti atisayaina kusalavā mahāpuñ‍ñau, paṭighādianatthavigamaina vau| khaimī taināti taina maittāyitaina| sabbai ca pāṇaiti casaddau byatiraikai| manasānukampanti cittaina anukampantau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – aikasattavisayāpi tāva maittā mahākusalarāsi, sabbai pana pāṇai attanau piyaputtaṃ viya hitapharaṇaina manasā anukampantau pahūtaṃ bahuṃ anappakaṃ apariyantaṃ catusaṭṭhimahākappaipi attanau vipākappabandhaṃ pavattaituṃ samatthaṃ uḷārapuñ‍ñaṃ ariyau parisuddhacittau puggalau pakarauti nipphādaiti|

sattasaṇḍanti sattasaṅkhātaina saṇḍaina saman‍nāgataṃ bharitaṃ, sattaihi aviraḷaṃ ākiṇṇamanussanti atthau| vijitvāti adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammainaiva vijinitvā| rājisayauti isisadisā dhammikarājānau| yajamānāti dānāni dadamānā| anupariyagāti vicariṃsu|

assamaidhantiādīsu paurāṇakarājakālai kira sassamaidhaṃ, purisamaidhaṃ, sammāpāsaṃ, vācāpaiyyanti cattāri saṅgahavatthūni ahaisuṃ, yaihi rājānau laukaṃ saṅgaṇhiṃsu| tattha nipphan‍nasassatau dasamabhāgaggahaṇaṃ sassamaidhaṃ nāma, sassasampādanai, maidhāvitāti atthau| mahāyaudhānaṃ chamāsikaṃ bhattavaitanānuppadānaṃ purisamaidhaṃ nāma, purisasaṅgaṇhanai maidhāvitāti atthau| daliddamanussānaṃ  pautthakai laikhaṃ gahaitvā tīṇi vassāni vinā vaḍḍhiyā sahassadvisahassamattadhanānuppadānaṃ sammāpāsaṃ nāma| tañhi sammā manussai pāsaiti hadayai bandhitvā viya ṭhapaiti, tasmā ‘‘sammāpāsa’’nti vuc‍cati| ‘‘tāta mātulā’’tiādinā pana saṇhavācāya saṅgahaṇaṃ vācāpaiyyaṃ nāma, paiyyavaj‍jaṃ piyavācatāti atthau| aivaṃ catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi saṅgahitaṃ raṭṭhaṃ iddhañ‍caiva hauti phītañ‍ca pahūtaan‍napānaṃ khaimaṃ  nirabbudaṃ| manussā mudā maudamānā urai puttai nac‍caintā apārutagharā viharanti| idaṃ gharadvāraisu aggaḷānaṃ abhāvatau ‘‘niraggaḷa’’nti vuc‍cati| ayaṃ paurāṇikā pavaiṇi, ayaṃ paurāṇikā pakati|

aparabhāgai pana auk‍kākarājakālai brāhmaṇā imāni cattāri saṅgahavatthūni imañ‍ca raṭṭhasampattiṃ parivattaintā uddhammūlaṃ katvā assamaidhaṃ purisamaidhantiādikai pañ‍ca yañ‍ñai nāma akaṃsu| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā brāhmaṇadhammiyasuttai –

‘‘taisaṃ āsi vipal‍lāsau, disvāna aṇutau aṇuṃ…pai॰…||

‘‘tai tattha mantai ganthaitvā, auk‍kākaṃ tadupāgamu’’nti|| (su॰ ni॰ 301-304)|

tattha assamaittha maidhanti bādhaintīti assamaidhau| dvīhi pariyañ‍ñaihi yajitabbassa aikavīsatiyūpassa aikasmiṃ pacchimadivasai aiva sattanavutipañ‍capasusataghātabhīsanassa ṭhapaitvā bhūmiñ‍ca purisai ca avasaisasabbavibhavadakkhiṇassa yañ‍ñassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| purisamaittha maidhanti bādhaintīti purisamaidhau| catūhi pariyañ‍ñaihi yajitabbassa saddhiṃbhūmiyā assamaidhai vuttavibhavadakkhiṇassa yañ‍ñassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sammamaittha pāsanti khipantīti sammāpāsau| yugacchiggaḷai pavaisanadaṇḍakasaṅkhātaṃ sammaṃ khipitvā tassa patitaukāsai vaidiṃ katvā saṃhārimaihi yūpādīhi sarassatinadiyā nimuggaukāsatau pabhuti paṭilaumaṃ gacchantaina yajitabbassa satrayāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ vājamaittha pivantīti vājapaiyyau| aikaina pariyañ‍ñaina sattarasahi pasūhi yajitabbassa baiḷuvayūpassa sattarasakadakkhiṇassa yañ‍ñassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| natthi aittha aggaḷauti niraggaḷau| navahi pariyañ‍ñaihi yajitabbassa saddhiṃ bhūmiyā purisaihi ca assamaidhai vuttavibhavadakkhiṇassa sabbamaidhapariyāyanāmassa assamaidhavikappassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

candappabhāti candappabhāya| tāragaṇāva sabbaiti yathā sabbaipi tārāgaṇā candimasaubhāya sauḷasimpi  kalaṃ nāgghanti, aivaṃ tai assamaidhādayau  yañ‍ñā maittacittassa vuttalakkhaṇaina subhāvitassa sauḷasimpi kalaṃ nānubhavanti, na pāpuṇanti, nāgghantīti atthau|

idāni  aparaipi diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikai maittābhāvanāya ānisaṃsai dassaituṃ ‘‘yau na hantī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha yauti maittābrahmavihārabhāvanānuyuttau puggalau| na hantīti tainaiva maittābhāvanānubhāvaina dūravikkhambhitabyāpādatāya na kañ‍ci sattaṃ hiṃsati, laiḍḍudaṇḍādīhi na vibādhati vā| na ghātaitīti paraṃ samādapaitvā na sattai hanāpaiti na vibādhāpaiti ca| na jinātīti sārambhaviggāhikakathādivasaina na kañ‍ci jināti sārambhassaiva abhāvatau, jānikaraṇavasaina vā aḍḍakaraṇādinā na kañ‍ci jināti| na jāpayaiti paraipi payaujaitvā paraisaṃ dhanajāniṃ na kārāpaiyya| maittaṃsauti maittāmayacittakauṭṭhāsau, maittāya vā aṃsau avijahanaṭṭhaina avayavabhūtauti maittaṃsau| sabbabhūtaisūti sabbasattaisu| tatau aiva vairaṃ tassa na kainacīti akusalavairaṃ tassa kainacipi kāraṇaina natthi, puggalavairasaṅkhātau viraudhau kainaci purisaina saddhiṃ tassa maittāvihārissa natthīti|

aivamaitasmiṃ aikakanipātai paṭipāṭiyā tairasasu suttaisu sikkhāsuttadvayai cāti pan‍narasasu suttaisu vivaṭṭaṃ kathitaṃ, nīvaraṇasuttaṃ saṃyaujanasuttaṃ appamādasuttaṃ aṭṭhisañ‍cayasuttanti aitaisu catūsu suttaisu vaṭṭavivaṭṭaṃ kathitaṃ| itaraisu pana vaṭṭamaiva kathitanti|

sattamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

paramatthadīpaniyā

khuddakanikāya-aṭṭhakathāya

itivuttakassa aikakanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. dukanipātau

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. dukkhavihārasuttavaṇṇanā

28. dukanipātassa  paṭhamai dvīhīti gaṇanaparicchaidau| dhammaihīti paricchin‍nadhammanidassanaṃ| dvīhi dhammaihīti dvīhi akusaladhammaihi| saman‍nāgatauti yuttau| diṭṭhaiva dhammaiti imasmiṃyaiva attabhāvai| dukkhaṃ viharatīti catūsupi iriyāpathaisu kilaisadukkhaina caiva kāyikacaitasikadukkhaina ca dukkhaṃ viharati| savighātanti cittūpaghātaina caiva kāyūpaghātaina ca savighātaṃ| saupāyāsanti kilaisūpāyāsaina caiva sarīrakhaidaina ca balavaāyāsavasaina saupāyāsaṃ| sapariḷāhanti kilaisapariḷāhaina caiva kāyapariḷāhaina ca sapariḷāhaṃ| kāyassa bhaidāti upādin‍nakkhandhaparic‍cāgā| paraṃ maraṇāti tadanantaraṃ abhinibbattakkhandhaggahaṇai| atha vā kāyassa bhaidāti jīvitindriyupacchaidā| paraṃ maraṇāti cutitau uddhaṃ| duggati pāṭikaṅkhāti duggatisaṅkhātānaṃ catun‍naṃ apāyānaṃ añ‍ñatarā gati icchitabbā, avassaṃbhāvinīti atthau|

aguttadvārauti apihitadvārau| kattha pana aguttadvārauti āha ‘‘indriyaisū’’ti| taina manacchaṭṭhānaṃ indriyānaṃ asaṃvaramāha| paṭiggahaṇaparibhaugavasaina bhaujanai mattaṃ na jānātīti bhaujanai amattañ‍ñū| ‘‘indriyaisu aguttadvāratāya bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutāyā’’tipi paṭhanti|

kathaṃ  indriyaisu aguttadvāratā, kathaṃ vā guttadvāratāti? kiñ‍cāpi hi cakkhundriyai saṃvarau vā asaṃvarau vā natthi| na hi cakkhupasādaṃ nissāya sati vā muṭṭhassac‍caṃ vā uppaj‍jati| apica yadā rūpārammaṇaṃ cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchati, tadā bhavaṅgai dvikkhattuṃ uppaj‍jitvā niruddhai kiriyāmanaudhātu āvaj‍janakic‍caṃ sādhayamānā uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhati, tatau  cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dassanakic‍caṃ, tatau vipākamanaudhātu sampaṭicchanakic‍caṃ, tatau vipākāhaitukamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu santīraṇakic‍caṃ, tatau kiriyāhaitukamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vauṭṭhabbanakic‍caṃ sādhayamānā uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhati, tadanantaraṃ javanaṃ javati| tathāpi naiva bhavaṅgasamayai, na āvaj‍janādīnaṃ añ‍ñatarasamayai  saṃvarau vā asaṃvarau vā atthi, javanakkhaṇai pana sacai dussīlyaṃ vā muṭṭhassac‍caṃ vā añ‍ñāṇaṃ vā akkhanti vā kausaj‍jaṃ vā uppaj‍jati, asaṃvarau hauti| aivaṃ hauntaupi sau ‘‘cakkhudvārai asaṃvarau’’ti vuc‍cati| kasmā? yasmā tasmiṃ sati dvārampi aguttaṃ hauti bhavaṅgampi āvaj‍janādīni vīthicittānipi| yathā kiṃ? yathā nagarai catūsu dvāraisu asaṃvutaisu kiñ‍cāpi antaugharadvārakauṭṭhakagabbhādayau susaṃvutā tathāpi antaunagarai sabbaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ arakkhitaṃ agaupitamaiva hauti| nagaradvāraihi pavisitvā caurā yadicchanti, taṃ haraiyyuṃ| aivamaiva javanai dussīlyādīsu uppan‍naisu tasmiṃ asaṃvarai satidvārampi aguttaṃ hauti, bhavaṅgampi āvaj‍janādīni vīthicittānipi| tasmiṃ pana asati javanai sīlādīsu uppan‍naisu dvārampi guttaṃ hauti bhavaṅgampi āvaj‍janādīni vīthicittānipi| yathā kiṃ? yathā nagaradvāraisu saṃvutaisu kiñ‍cāpi antaugharadvārādayau asaṃvutā, tathāpi antaunagarai sabbaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ surakkhitaṃ sugaupitamaiva hauti| nagaradvāraisu hi pihitaisu caurānaṃ pavaisau natthi| aivamaiva javanai sīlādīsu uppan‍naisu dvārampi guttaṃ hauti, bhavaṅgampi, āvaj‍janādīni vīthicittānipi| tasmā javanakkhaṇai uppaj‍jamānaupi ‘‘cakkhudvārai saṃvarau’’ti vuc‍cati| saisadvāraisupi aisaiva nayau| aivaṃ indriyaisu aguttadvāratā, guttadvāratā ca vaiditabbā|

kathaṃ pana bhaujanai amattañ‍ñū, kathaṃ vā mattañ‍ñūti? yau hi puggalau mahicchau hutvā paṭiggahaṇai mattaṃ na jānāti| mahicchapuggalau hi yathā nāma kacchapuṭavāṇijau piḷandhanabhaṇḍakaṃ hatthaina gahaitvā ucchaṅgaipi pakkhipitabbayuttakaṃ pakkhipitvā mahājanassa passantassaiva ‘‘asukaṃ gaṇhatha, asukaṃ gaṇhathā’’ti mukhaina ugghausaiti, aivamaiva appamattakampi attanau sīlaṃ vā ganthaṃ vā dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ vā antamasau arañ‍ñavāsamattakampi mahājanassa jānantassaiva sambhāvaiti, sambhāvaitvā ca pana sakaṭaihipi upanītai pac‍cayai ‘‘ala’’nti avatvā paṭiggaṇhāti| tayau hi pūraituṃ na sak‍kā aggi upādānaina, samuddau udakaina, mahicchau pac‍cayaihīti –

‘‘aggikkhandhau samuddau ca, mahicchau cāpi puggalau|

bahukai pac‍cayai din‍nai, tayaupaitai na pūrayaiti’’||

mahicchapuggalau  hi vijātamātuyāpi manaṃ gaṇhituṃ na sak‍kauti| aivarūpau hi anuppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ na uppādaiti, uppan‍nalābhatau ca parihāyati| aivaṃ tāva paṭiggahaṇai amattañ‍ñū hauti| yau pana dhammaina samaina laddhampi āhāraṃ gadhitau mucchitau ajjhaupan‍nau anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau āharahatthakaalaṃsāṭakatatthavaṭṭakakākamāsakabhuttavamitakabrāhmaṇānaṃ añ‍ñatarau viya ayaunisau anupāyaina yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati| ayaṃ paribhaugai amattañ‍ñū nāma|

yau pana ‘‘yadipi daiyyadhammau bahu hauti, dāyakau appaṃ dātukāmau, dāyakassa vasaina appaṃ gaṇhāti| daiyyadhammau appau, dāyakau bahuṃ dātukāmau, daiyyadhammassa vasaina appaṃ gaṇhāti| daiyyadhammau bahu, dāyakaupi bahuṃ dātukāmau, attanau thāmaṃ ñatvā pamāṇayuttamaiva gaṇhātī’’ti aivaṃ vuttassa paṭiggahaṇai pamāṇajānanassa caiva, ‘‘paṭisaṅkhā  yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti, naiva davāya, na madāyā’’tiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 1355) ‘‘laddhañ‍ca piṇḍapātaṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhaupan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jatī’’ti ca ādinā nayaina vuttassa pac‍cavaikkhitvā paṭisaṅkhānapañ‍ñāya jānitvā āhāraparibhuñ‍janasaṅkhātassa paribhaugai pamāṇajānanassa ca vasaina bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti, ayaṃ bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū nāma| aivaṃ bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutā mattañ‍ñutā ca hautīti vaiditabbaṃ|

gāthāsu pana cakkhuntiādīsu cakkhatīti cakkhu, rūpaṃ assādaiti, samavisamaṃ ācikkhantaṃ viya hautīti vā atthau| suṇātīti sautaṃ| ghāyatīti ghānaṃ| jīvitanimittaṃ āhārarasau jīvitaṃ, taṃ avhāyatīti jivhā| kucchitānaṃ āyauti kāyau| manatai vijānātīti manau| paurāṇā panāhu munātīti manau, nāḷiyā minamānau viya mahātulāya dhārayamānau viya ca ārammaṇaṃ vijānātīti atthau| aivaṃ tāvaittha padatthau vaiditabbau|

bhāvatthatau pana duvidhaṃ cakkhu – maṃsacakkhu ca pañ‍ñācakkhu ca| taisu buddhacakkhu, samantacakkhu, ñāṇacakkhu, dibbacakkhu, dhammacakkhūti pañ‍cavidhaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhu| tattha ‘‘addasaṃ khau  ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.283) idaṃ buddhacakkhu nāma| ‘‘samantacakkhu vuc‍cati sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇa’’nti (cūḷava॰ dhautakamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 32) idaṃ samantacakkhu nāma| ‘‘cakkhuṃ udapādī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1081; mahāva॰ 15) idaṃ ñāṇacakkhu nāma| ‘‘addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhainā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.284) idaṃ dibbacakkhu nāma| ‘‘virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādī’’ti  (ma॰ ni॰ 2.395; mahāva॰ 16) idaṃ haiṭṭhimamaggattayasaṅkhātaṃ dhammacakkhu nāma|

maṃsacakkhupi duvidhaṃ – sasambhāracakkhu, pasādacakkhūti| tattha yvāyaṃ akkhikūpakai patiṭṭhitau haiṭṭhā akkhikūpakaṭṭhikaina, upari bhamukaṭṭhikaina, ubhatau akkhikūṭaihi, antau matthaluṅgaina, bahiddhā akkhilaumaihi paricchin‍nau maṃsapiṇḍau, saṅkhaipatau catassau dhātuyau – vaṇṇau, gandhau, rasau, aujāsambhavau saṇṭhānaṃ jīvitaṃ bhāvau kāyapasādau cakkhupasādauti cuddasa sambhārā| vitthāratau catassau dhātuyau taṃnissitā vaṇṇagandharasaaujāsaṇṭhānasambhavāti imāni dasa catusamuṭṭhānikattā cattālīsaṃ haunti, jīvitaṃ bhāvau kāyapasādau cakkhupasādauti cattāri aikantakammasamuṭṭhānaivāti imaisaṃ catucattālīsāya rūpānaṃ vasaina catucattālīsa sambhārā| yaṃ laukai ‘‘saitaṃ vaṭṭaṃ puthulaṃ visaṭaṃ vipulaṃ cakkhū’’ti sañ‍jānantau na cakkhuṃ sañ‍jānāti, vatthuṃ cakkhutau sañ‍jānāti, yau maṃsapiṇḍau akkhikūpakai patiṭṭhitau nhārusuttakaina matthaluṅgaina ābaddhau, yattha saitampi atthi kaṇhampi lauhitakampi pathavīpi āpaupi taijaupi vāyaupi| yaṃ saimhussadattā saitaṃ, pittussadattā kaṇhaṃ, ruhirussadattā lauhitakaṃ, pathavussadattā patthaddhaṃ, āpussadattā paggharati, taijussadattā pariḍayhati, vāyussadattā sambhamati, idaṃ sasambhāracakkhu nāma| yau pana aittha sitau aittha paṭibaddhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau, idaṃ pasādacakkhu nāma| idañhi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ yathārahaṃ vatthudvārabhāvaina pavattati|

sautādīsupi sautaṃ dibbasautaṃ, maṃsasautanti duvidhaṃ| aittha ‘‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇātī’’ti idaṃ dibbasautaṃ nāma| maṃsasautaṃ pana sasambhārasautaṃ pasādasautanti duvidhantiādi sabbaṃ cakkhumhi vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ, tathā ghānajivhā| kāyau pana caupanakāyau, karajakāyau, samūhakāyau, pasādakāyautiādinā bahuvidhau| tattha –

‘‘kāyaina  saṃvutā dhīrā, athau vācāya saṃvutā’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 234) –

ayaṃ caupanakāyau nāma| ‘‘imamhā kāyā añ‍ñaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimminātī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.236; paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.14) ayaṃ karajakāyau nāma| samūhakāyau pana viñ‍ñāṇādisamūhavasaina anaikavidhau āgatau| tathā hi ‘‘cha imai, āvusau, viñ‍ñāṇakāyā’’tiādīsu  (ma॰ ni॰ 1.101) viñ‍ñāṇasamūhau vuttau| ‘‘cha phassakāyā’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 3.323; ma॰ ni॰ 1.98) phassādisamūhau| tathā ‘‘kāyapassaddhi kāyalahutā’’tiādīsu (dha॰ sa॰ 114) vaidanākkhandhādayau| ‘‘idhaikac‍cau pathavikāyaṃ anic‍catau anupassati, āpaukāyaṃ taijaukāyaṃ vāyaukāyaṃ kaisakāyaṃ laumakāya’’ntiādīsu (paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.35) pathavādisamūhau| ‘‘kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 3.16) ayaṃ pasādakāyau| idhāpi pasādakāyau vaiditabbau| sau hi kāyaviñ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ yathārahaṃ vatthudvārabhāvaina pavattati| manauti pana kiñ‍cāpi sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vuc‍cati, tathāpi dvārabhāvassa idhādhippaitattā dvārabhūtaṃ sāvaj‍janaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

aitāni yassa dvārāni aguttāni ca bhikkhunauti yassa bhikkhunau aitāni manacchaṭṭhāni dvārāni sativaussaggaina pamādaṃ āpan‍nattā satikavāṭaina apihitāni| bhaujanamhi…pai॰… adhigacchatīti sau bhikkhu vuttanayaina bhaujanai amattañ‍ñū indriyaisu ca saṃvararahitau diṭṭhadhammikañ‍ca raugādivasaina, samparāyikañ‍ca duggatipariyāpan‍naṃ kāyadukkhaṃ rāgādikilaisasantāpavasaina, icchāvighātavasaina ca caitaudukkhanti sabbathāpi dukkhamaiva adhigacchati pāpuṇāti| yasmā caitadaivaṃ, tasmā duvidhainapi dukkhagginā idhalaukai ca paralaukai ca ḍayhamānaina kāyaina ḍayhamānaina caitasā divā vā yadi vā rattiṃ nic‍cakālamaiva tādisau puggalau dukkhamaiva viharati, na tassa sukhavihārassa sambhavau, vaṭṭadukkhānatik‍kamai pana vattabbamaiva natthīti|

paṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. sukhavihārasuttavaṇṇanā

29. dutiyai  vuttavipariyāyaina atthau vaiditabbau|

dutiyasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. tapanīyasuttavaṇṇanā

30. tatiyai tapanīyāti idha caiva samparāyai ca tapanti vibādhainti vihaiṭhaintīti tapanīyā| tapanaṃ vā dukkhaṃ diṭṭhai caiva dhammai abhisamparāyai ca tassa uppādanaina caiva anubalappadānaina  ca hitāti tapanīyā| atha vā tapanti  taināti tapanaṃ, pacchānutāpau, vippaṭisārauti atthau, tassa haitubhāvatau hitāti tapanīyā| akatakalyāṇauti akataṃ kalyāṇaṃ bhaddakaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ aitaināti akatakalyāṇau| saisapadadvayaṃ tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| puñ‍ñañhi pavattihitatāya āyatiṃsukhatāya ca bhaddakaṭṭhaina kalyāṇanti ca kucchitasalanādiatthaina kusalanti ca dukkhabhīrūnaṃ saṃsārabhīrūnañ‍ca rakkhanaṭṭhaina bhīruttāṇanti ca vuc‍cati| katapāpauti kataṃ upacitaṃ pāpaṃ aitaināti katapāpau| saisapadadvayaṃ tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| akusalakammañhi lāmakaṭṭhaina pāpanti ca attanau pavattikkhaṇai vipākakkhaṇai ca ghaurasabhāvatāya luddanti ca kilaisaihi dūsitabhāvaina kibbisanti ca vuc‍cati| iti bhagavā ‘‘dvai dhammā tapanīyā’’ti dhammādhiṭṭhānaina uddisitvā akataṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ katañ‍ca akusalaṃ dhammaṃ puggalādhiṭṭhānaina niddisi| idāni taisaṃ tapanīyabhāvaṃ dassaintau ‘‘sau akataṃ mai kalyāṇantipi tappati, kataṃ mai pāpantipi tappatī’’ti āha| cittasantāsaina tappati anutappati anusaucatīti atthau|

gāthāsu duṭṭhu caritaṃ, kilaisapūtikattā vā duṭṭhaṃ caritanti duc‍caritaṃ| kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ, kāyatau vā pavattaṃ duc‍caritaṃ kāyaduc‍caritaṃ| aivaṃ vacīmanauduc‍caritānipi daṭṭhabbāni| imāni ca kāyaduc‍caritādīni kammapathappattāni adhippaitānīti yaṃ na kammapathappattaṃ akusalajātaṃ, taṃ sandhāyāha ‘‘yañ‍cañ‍ñaṃ dausasañhita’’nti| tassatthau – yampi ca añ‍ñaṃ kammapathabhāvaṃ appattattā nippariyāyaina kāyakammādisaṅkhaṃ na labhati, rāgādikilaisasaṃsaṭṭhattā dausasahitaṃ akusalaṃ tampi katvāti atthau| nirayanti niratiatthaina nirassādaṭṭhaina vā nirayanti laddhanāmaṃ sabbampi duggatiṃ, ayasaṅkhātasukhappaṭikkhaipaina  vā sabbattha sugatiduggatīsu nirayadukkhaṃ| sau tādisau puggalau upagacchatīti aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau|

aittha ca kāyaduc‍caritassa tapanīyabhāvai nandau yakkhau nandau  māṇavakau nandau gaughātakau dvai bhātikāti aitaisaṃ vatthūni kathaitabbāni| tai kira gāviṃ vadhitvā maṃsaṃ dvai kauṭṭhāsai akaṃsu| tatau kaniṭṭhau jaiṭṭhaṃ āha – ‘‘mayhaṃ dārakā bahū, imāni mai antāni daihī’’ti| atha naṃ jaiṭṭhau – ‘‘sabbaṃ maṃsaṃ dvaidhā vibhattaṃ, puna kimaggahaisī’’ti paharitvā jīvitakkhayaṃ pāpaisi| nivattitvā ca naṃ aulaukaintau mataṃ disvā ‘‘bhāriyaṃ vata mayā kataṃ, svāhaṃ akāraṇainaiva naṃ māraisi’’nti cittaṃ uppādaisi| athassa balavavippaṭisārau uppaj‍ji| sau ṭhitaṭṭhānaipi nisin‍naṭṭhānaipi tadaiva kammaṃ āvaj‍jaiti, cittassādaṃ na labhati, asitapītakhāyitampissa sarīrai aujaṃ na pharati, aṭṭhicammamattamaiva ahausi| atha naṃ aikau thairau pucchi ‘‘upāsaka, tvaṃ ativiya kisau aṭṭhicammamattau jātau, kīdisau tai raugau, udāhu  atthi kiñ‍ci tapanīyaṃ kammaṃ kata’’nti? sau ‘‘āma, bhantai’’ti sabbaṃ āraucaisi| athassa sau ‘‘bhāriyaṃ tai, upāsaka, kammaṃ kataṃ, anaparādhaṭṭhānai aparaddha’’nti āha| sau tainaiva kammunā kālaṃ katvā nirayai nibbatti| vacīduc‍caritassa pana suppabuddhasak‍kakaukālikaciñ‍camāṇavikādīnaṃ vatthūni kathaitabbāni, manauduc‍caritassa uk‍kalajayabhañ‍ñādīnaṃ|

tatiyasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. atapanīyasuttavaṇṇanā

31. catutthai tatiyai vuttavipariyāyaina atthau vaiditabbau|

catutthasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. paṭhamasīlasuttavaṇṇanā

32. pañ‍camai pāpakaina ca sīlaināti pāpakaṃ nāma sīlaṃ sīlabhaidakarau asaṃvarauti vadanti| tattha yadi asaṃvarau asīlamaiva taṃdussīlyabhāvatau, kathaṃ sīlanti vuc‍cati? tatthāyaṃ adhippāyau siyā  – yathā nāma laukai adiṭṭhaṃ ‘‘diṭṭha’’nti vuc‍cati, asīlavā ‘‘sīlavā’’ti, aivamidhāpi asīlampi asaṃvaraupi  ‘‘sīla’’nti vauharīyati| atha vā ‘‘katamai ca, thapati, akusalā sīlā? akusalaṃ kāyakammaṃ, akusalaṃ vacīkammaṃ, pāpakau ājīvau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 2.264) vacanatau akusaladhammaisupi atthaiva sīlasamañ‍ñā, tasmā paricayavasaina sabhāvasiddhi viya pakatibhūtau sabbau samācārau ‘‘sīla’’nti vuc‍cati| tattha yaṃ akausal‍lasambhūtaṭṭhaina akusalaṃ lāmakaṃ, taṃ sandhāyāha ‘‘pāpakaina ca sīlainā’’ti| pāpikāya ca diṭṭhiyāti sabbāpi micchādiṭṭhiyau pāpikāva| visaisatau pana ahaitukadiṭṭhi, akiriyadiṭṭhi, natthikadiṭṭhīti imā tividhā diṭṭhiyau pāpikatarā| tattha pāpakaina sīlaina saman‍nāgatau puggalau payaugavipan‍nau hauti, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau āsayavipan‍nau hauti, aivaṃ payaugāsayavipan‍nau puggalau nirayūpagau hautiyaiva| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dvīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau, aivaṃ nirayai’’ti| aittha ca ‘‘dvīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau’’ti idaṃ lakkhaṇavacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, na tantiniddaisau| yathā taṃ laukai ‘‘yadimai byādhitā  siyuṃ, imaisaṃ idaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ dātabba’’nti| añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu aisaiva nayau| duppañ‍ñauti nippañ‍ñau|

pañ‍camasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. dutiyasīlasuttavaṇṇanā

33. chaṭṭhai bhaddakaina ca sīlaināti kāyasucaritādicatupārisuddhisīlaina| tañhi akhaṇḍādisīlabhāvaina sayañ‍ca kalyāṇaṃ, samathavipassanādikalyāṇaguṇāvahaṃ cāti ‘‘bhaddaka’’nti vuc‍cati| bhaddikāya ca diṭṭhiyāti kammassakatāñāṇaina caiva kammapathasammādiṭṭhiyā ca| tattha bhaddakaina sīlaina payaugasampan‍nau hauti, bhaddikāya diṭṭhiyā āsayasampan‍nau| iti payaugāsayasampan‍nau puggalau saggūpagau hauti| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, dvīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau, aivaṃ saggai’’ti| sappañ‍ñauti pañ‍ñavā| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

chaṭṭhasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. ātāpīsuttavaṇṇanā

34. sattamai  anātāpīti kilaisānaṃ ātāpanaṭṭhaina ātāpau, vīriyaṃ, sau aitassa atthīti ātāpī, na ātāpī anātāpī, sammappadhānavirahitau kusītauti vuttaṃ hauti| auttāpau vuc‍cati pāputrāsau, sau aitassa atthīti auttāpī, na auttāpī anauttāpī, auttāparahitau| atha vā ātāpappaṭipakkhau anātāpau, kausaj‍jaṃ sau assa atthīti anātāpī| yaṃ ‘‘na auttapati auttappitabbaina, na auttapati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā’’ti aivaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ anauttappaṃ anauttāpau| sau assa atthīti anauttāpīti aivamaittha atthau vaiditabbau|

abhabbauti anarahau| sambaudhāyāti ariyamaggatthāya| nibbānāyāti kilaisānaṃ ac‍cantavūpasamāya amatamahānibbānāya| anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassāti arahattaphalassa| tañhi uttaritarassa abhāvatau anuttaraṃ, catūhi yaugaihi anupaddutattā khaimaṃ nibbhayanti yaugakkhaimanti ca  vuc‍cati| adhigamāyāti pattiyā| ātāpīti vīriyavā| sau hi ‘‘āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisū’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.345) aivaṃ vuttaina vīriyārambhaina saman‍nāgatau kilaisānaṃ ac‍cantamaiva ātāpanasīlauti ātāpī| auttāpīti ‘‘yaṃ auttapati auttappitabbaina, auttapati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 31) aivaṃ vuttaina auttappaina saman‍nāgatattā auttapanasīlauti auttappī| ayañhi auttāpīti vuttau| tadavinābhāvatau hiriyā ca saman‍nāgatau aiva hautīti hirauttappasampan‍nau aṇumattaipi vaj‍jai bhayadassāvī sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti| ic‍cassa sīlasampadā dassitā| ātāpīti  iminā nayainassa kilaisaparitāpitādīpanaina samathavipassanābhāvanānuyuttatā dassitā| yathāvuttañ‍ca vīriyaṃ saddhāsatisamādhipañ‍ñāhi vinā na hautīti vimuttiparipācakāni saddhāpañ‍camāni indriyāni atthatau vuttānaiva haunti| taisu ca siddhaisu anic‍cai anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñāti cha nibbaidhabhāgiyā sañ‍ñā siddhā aivāti| aivaṃ imaihi dvīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa laukiyānaṃ  sīlasamādhipañ‍ñānaṃ sijjhanatau maggaphalanibbānādhigamassa bhabbataṃ dassaintau ‘‘ātāpī ca khau…pai॰… adhigamāyā’’ti āha|

gāthāsu kusītauti micchāvitak‍kabahulatāya kāmabyāpādavihiṃsāvitak‍kasaṅkhātaihi kucchitaihi pāpadhammaihi sitau sambandhau yuttauti kusītau| kucchitaṃ vā sīdati sammāpaṭipattitau avasīdatīti kusītau, da-kārassa ta-kāraṃ katvā| hīnavīriyauti nibbīriyau, catūsupi iriyāpathaisu vīriyakaraṇarahitau| anussāhasaṃhananasabhāvassa cittālasiyassa thinassa, asattivighātasabhāvassa kāyālasiyassa middhassa ca abhiṇhappavattiyā thinamiddhabahulau| pāpajigucchanalakkhaṇāya hiriyā abhāvaina tappaṭipakkhaina ahirikaina saman‍nāgatattā ca ahirikau| hirauttappavīriyānaṃ abhāvainaiva sammāpaṭipattiyaṃ natthi aitassa ādarauti anādarau| ubhayathāpi tathā dhammapuggalaina duvidhakiriyākaraṇaina anādarau| phuṭṭhunti phusituṃ| sambaudhimuttamanti sambaudhisaṅkhātaṃ uttamaṃ arahattaṃ adhigantuṃ abhabbauti atthau|

satimāti cirakatacirabhāsitānaṃ anussaraṇai samatthassa satinaipak‍kassa bhāvaina catusatipaṭṭhānayaugaina satimā| nipakauti sattaṭṭhāniyasampajañ‍ñasaṅkhātaina caiva kammaṭṭhānapariharaṇapañ‍ñāsaṅkhātaina ca naipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatattā nipakau| jhāyīti ārammaṇūpanijjhānaina lakkhaṇūpanijjhānaina cāti dvīhipi jhānaihi jhāyī| appamattauti ‘‘divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇiyaihi  dhammaihi  cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’tiādinā nayaina kammaṭṭhānabhāvanāya appamattau| saṃyaujanaṃ jātijarāya chaitvāti jātiyā caiva jarāya ca sattai saṃyaujaitīti saṃyaujananti laddhanāmaṃ kāmarāgādikaṃ dasavidhampi kilaisajātaṃ anusayasamugghātavasaina mūlatau chinditvā| atha vā saṃyaujanaṃ jātijarāya chaitvāti jātijarāya saṃyaujanaṃ chinditvā| yassa hi saṃyaujanāni acchin‍nāni, tassa jātijarāya acchaidau asamugghātauva| yassa pana tāni chin‍nāni, tassa jātijarāpi chin‍nāva kāraṇassa samugghātitattā| tasmā saṃyaujanaṃ chindantau aiva jātijarāpi chindati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘saṃyaujanaṃ jātijarāya  chaitvā’’ti| idhaiva sambaudhimanuttaraṃ phusaiti imasmiṃyaiva attabhāvai aggamaggaṃ arahattaṃ vā phusai pāpuṇaiyya|

sattamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. paṭhamanakuhanasuttavaṇṇanā

35. aṭṭhamai nayidanti aittha naiti paṭisaidhai nipātau, tassa ‘‘vussatī’’ti iminā sambandhau, yakārau padasandhikarau| idaṃ-saddau ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, samayaṃ uk‍kaṭṭhāyaṃ viharāmi subhagavanai sālarājamūlai’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.501) nipātamattaṃ| ‘‘idaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, appamattakaṃ auramattakaṃ sīlamattaka’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.27) yathāvuttai āsan‍napac‍cakkhai āgatau|

‘‘idañhi taṃ jaitavanaṃ, isisaṅghanisaivitaṃ|

āvutthaṃ dhammarājaina, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mamā’’ti|| –

ādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.48) vakkhamānai āsan‍napac‍cakkhai| idhāpi vakkhamānaiyaiva āsan‍napac‍cakkhai daṭṭhabbau|

brahmacariya-saddau –

‘‘kiṃ tai vataṃ kiṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ,

kissa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipākau|

iddhī  jutī balavīriyūpapatti,

idañ‍ca tai nāga mahāvimānaṃ||

‘‘ahañ‍ca  bhariyā ca manussalaukai,

saddhā ubhau dānapatī ahumhā|

aupānabhūtaṃ mai gharaṃ tadāsi,

santappitā samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca||

‘‘taṃ mai vataṃ taṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ,

tassa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipākau|

iddhī jutī balavīriyūpapatti,

idañ‍ca mai dhīra mahāvimāna’’nti|| (jā॰ 2.22.1592-1593, 1595) –

imasmiṃ  puṇṇakajātakai dānai āgatau|

‘‘kaina pāṇi kāmadadau, kaina pāṇi madhussavau|

kaina tai brahmacariyaina, puñ‍ñaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhati||

‘‘taina pāṇi kāmadadau, taina pāṇi madhussavau|

taina mai brahmacariyaina, puñ‍ñaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhatī’’ti|| (pai॰ va॰ 275, 277) –

imasmiṃ aṅkurapaitavatthusmiṃ vaiyyāvac‍cai| ‘‘idaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, tittiriyaṃ nāma brahmacariyaṃ ahausī’’ti (cūḷava॰ 311) imasmiṃ tittirajātakai pañ‍casikkhāpadasīlai| ‘‘taṃ khau pana, pañ‍casikha, brahmacariyaṃ naiva nibbidāya na virāgāya…pai॰… yāvadaiva brahmalaukūpapattiyā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.329) imasmiṃ mahāgauvindasuttai brahmavihārai| ‘‘parai abrahmacārī bhavissanti, mayamaittha brahmacārinau bhavissāmā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.83) sal‍laikhasuttai maithunaviratiyaṃ|

‘‘mayañ‍ca  bhariyā nātik‍kamāma,

amhai ca bhariyā nātik‍kamanti|

añ‍ñatra tāhi brahmacariyaṃ carāma,

tasmā hi amhaṃ daharā na mīyarai’’ti|| (jā॰ 1.10.97) –

mahādhammapālajātakai sadārasantausai| ‘‘abhijānāmi khau panāhaṃ, sāriputta, caturaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ brahmacariyaṃ  caritā – tapassī sudaṃ haumī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.155) laumahaṃsasuttai vīriyai|

‘‘hīnaina brahmacariyaina, khattiyai upapaj‍jati|

majjhimaina ca daivattaṃ, uttamaina visujjhatī’’ti|| (jā॰ 1.8.75) –

nimijātakai attadamanavasaina katai aṭṭhaṅgikaupausathai| ‘‘idaṃ khau pana, pañ‍casikha, brahmacariyaṃ aikantanibbidāya virāgāya…pai॰… ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.329) mahāgauvindasuttaiyaiva ariyamaggai| ‘‘tayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsita’’nti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.174) pāsādikasuttai sikkhattayasaṅgahai sakalasmiṃ sāsanai| idhāpi ariyamaggai sāsanai ca vattati|

vussatīti  vasīyati, carīyatīti atthau| janakuhanatthanti ‘‘ahau ayyau sīlavā vattasampan‍nau appicchau santuṭṭhau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau’’tiādinā janassa sattalaukassa vimhāpanatthaṃ| janalapanatthanti ‘‘aivarūpassa nāma ayyassa din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ bhavissatī’’ti pasan‍nacittaihi ‘‘kainatthau, kiṃ āharīyatū’’ti manussaihi vadāpanatthaṃ| lābhasak‍kārasilaukānisaṃsatthanti yvāyaṃ ‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘lābhī assaṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’nti, sīlai-svaivassa paripūrakārī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.65) sīlānisaṃsabhāvaina vuttau catupac‍cayalābhau, yau ca catun‍naṃ pac‍cayānaṃ sak‍kac‍cadānasaṅkhātau ādarabahumānagarukaraṇasaṅkhātau ca sak‍kārau, yau ca ‘‘sīlasampan‍nau bahussutau sutadharau āraddhavīriyau’’tiādinā nayaina uggatathutighausasaṅkhātau silaukau brahmacariyaṃ carantānaṃ diṭṭhadhammikau ānisaṃsau, tadatthaṃ| iti maṃ janau jānātūti ‘‘aivaṃ brahmacariyavāsai sati ‘ayaṃ sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau’tiādinā maṃ janau jānātu sambhāvaitū’’ti  attanau santaguṇavasaina sambhāvanatthampi na idaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vussatīti sambandhau|

kaici  pana ‘‘janakuhanatthanti pāpicchassa icchāpakatassa satau sāmantajappanairiyāpathanissitapac‍cayapaṭisaivanasaṅkhātaina tividhaina kuhanavatthunā kuhanabhāvaina janassa vimhāpanatthaṃ| janalapanatthanti pāpicchassaiva satau pac‍cayatthaṃ parikathaubhāsādivasaina lapanabhāvaina upalāpanabhāvaina vā janassa lapanatthaṃ| lābhasak‍kārasilaukānisaṃsatthanti pāpicchassaiva satau lābhādigarutāya lābhasak‍kārasilaukasaṅkhātassa ānisaṃsaudayassa nipphādanatthaṃ| iti maṃ janau jānātūti pāpicchassaiva satau asantaguṇasambhāvanādhippāyaina ‘iti aivaṃ maṃ janau jānātū’ti na idaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti aivamaittha atthaṃ vadanti| purimauyaiva pana atthau sāratarau|

atha khauti aittha athāti añ‍ñadatthai nipātau, khauti avadhāraṇai| taina kuhanāditau añ‍ñadatthāyaiva pana idaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ vussatīti dassaiti| idāni taṃ payaujanaṃ dassaintau ‘‘saṃvaratthañ‍caiva pahānatthañ‍cā’’ti  āha| tattha pañ‍cavidhau saṃvarau – pātimaukkhasaṃvarau, satisaṃvarau, ñāṇasaṃvarau, khantisaṃvarau, vīriyasaṃvarauti|

tattha ‘‘iminā pātimaukkhasaṃvaraina upaitau hauti samupaitau’’ti (vibha॰ 511) hi ādinā nayaina āgatau ayaṃ pātimaukkhasaṃvarau nāma, yau sīlasaṃvarauti ca pavuc‍cati| ‘‘rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.213; ma॰ ni॰ 1.295; saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.239; a॰ ni॰ 3.16) āgatau ayaṃ satisaṃvarau|

‘‘yāni sautāni laukasmiṃ (ajitāti bhagavā),

sati taisaṃ nivāraṇaṃ|

sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūmi,

pañ‍ñāyaitai pidhīyarai’’ti|| (su॰ ni॰ 1041) –

āgatau  ayaṃ ñāṇasaṃvarau| ‘‘khamau hauti sītassa uṇhassā’’tiādinā (ma॰ ni॰ 1.24; a॰ ni॰ 4.114; 6.58) nayaina āgatau ayaṃ khantisaṃvarau| ‘‘uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaitī’’tiādinā (ma॰ ni॰ 1.26; a॰ ni॰ 4.114; 6.58) nayaina āgatau ayaṃ vīriyasaṃvarau| atthatau pana pāṇātipātādīnaṃ pajahanavasaina, vattapaṭivattānaṃ karaṇavasaina ca pavattā caitanā viratiyau ca| saṅkhaipatau sabbau kāyavacīsaṃyamau, vitthāratau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ avītik‍kamau sīlasaṃvarau| sati aiva satisaṃvarau, satippadhānā vā kusalā khandhā| ñāṇamaiva ñāṇasaṃvarau| adhivāsanavasaina adausau, adausappadhānā vā tathā pavattā  kusalā khandhā khantisaṃvarau, pañ‍ñāti aikai| kāmavitak‍kādīnaṃ anadhivāsanavasaina pavattaṃ vīriyamaiva vīriyasaṃvarau| taisu paṭhamau kāyaduc‍caritādidussīlyassa saṃvaraṇatau saṃvarau, dutiyau muṭṭhassac‍cassa, tatiyau añ‍ñāṇassa, catutthau akkhantiyā, pañ‍camau kausaj‍jassa saṃvaraṇatau pidahanatau saṃvarauti vaiditabbau| aivamaitassa saṃvarassa atthāya saṃvaratthaṃ, saṃvaranipphādanatthanti atthau|

pahānampi pañ‍cavidhaṃ – tadaṅgappahānaṃ, vikkhambhanappahānaṃ, samucchaidappahānaṃ, paṭippassaddhippahānaṃ, nissaraṇappahānanti| tattha yaṃ vattabbaṃ, taṃ haiṭṭhā aikakanipātai paṭhamasuttavaṇṇanāyaṃ vuttamaiva| tassa pana pañ‍cavidhassapi tathā tathā rāgādikilaisānaṃ  paṭinissaj‍janaṭṭhaina samatik‍kamanaṭṭhaina vā pahānassa atthāya pahānatthaṃ, pahānasādhanatthanti atthau| tattha saṃvaraina kilaisānaṃ cittasantānai pavaisananivāraṇaṃ pahānaina pavaisananivāraṇañ‍caiva samugghātau cāti vadanti| ubhayaināpi pana yathārahaṃ ubhayaṃ sampaj‍jatīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| sīlādidhammā aiva hi saṃvaraṇatau saṃvarau, pajahanatau pahānanti|

gāthāsu anītihanti ītiyau vuc‍canti upaddavā – diṭṭhadhammikā ca samparāyikā ca| ītiyau hanati vināsaiti pajahatīti ītihaṃ, anu ītihanti anītihaṃ, sāsanabrahmacariyaṃ maggabrahmacariyañ‍ca| atha vā ītīhi anatthaihi  saddhiṃ hananti gacchanti pavattantīti ītihā, taṇhādiupak‍kilaisā| natthi aittha ītihāti anītihaṃ| ītihā vā yathāvuttainaṭṭhaina titthiyasamayā, tappaṭipakkhatau idaṃ anītihaṃ| ‘‘anitiha’’ntipi pāṭhau| tassatthau – ‘‘itihāya’’nti dhammaisu anaikaṃsaggāhabhāvatau vicikicchā itihaṃ nāma, sammāsambuddhappavaiditattā yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jantānaṃ nik‍kaṅkhabhāvasādhanatau natthi aittha itihanti anitihaṃ, aparappac‍cayanti atthau| vuttañhaitaṃ ‘‘pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti ‘‘atak‍kāvacarau’’ti ca| gāthāsukhatthaṃ pana ‘‘anītiha’’nti dīghaṃ katvā paṭhanti|

nibbānasaṅkhātaṃ augadhaṃ patiṭṭhaṃ pāraṃ gacchatīti nibbānaugadhagāmī, vimuttirasattā aikantainaiva nibbānasampāpakauti atthau| taṃ nibbānaugadhagāminaṃ brahmacariyaṃ| sauti yau sau samatiṃsa pāramiyau pūraitvā sabbakilaisai bhinditvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, sau bhagavā adaisayi daisaisi| nibbānaugadhauti vā ariyamaggau vuc‍cati| taina vinā nibbānaugāhanassa asambhavatau tassa ca nibbānaṃ anālambitvā appavattanatau, tañ‍ca taṃ aikantaṃ gacchatīti nibbānaugadhagāmī| atha vā nibbānaugadhagāminanti nibbānassa antaugāminaṃ maggabrahmacariyaṃ, nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ karitvā tassa antau aiva vattati pavattatīti| mahattaihīti mahāātumaihi uḷārajjhāsayaihi| mahantaṃ nibbānaṃ, mahantai vā sīlakkhandhādikai aisanti gavaisantīti mahaisinau buddhādayau ariyā| taihi anuyātau paṭipan‍nau| yathā buddhaina daisitanti yathā abhiñ‍ñaiyyādidhammai abhiñ‍ñaiyyādibhāvainaiva sammāsambuddhaina mayā daisitaṃ, aivaṃ yai aitaṃ  maggabrahmacariyaṃ tadatthaṃ sāsanabrahmacariyañ‍ca paṭipaj‍janti| tai diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikatthaihi  yathārahaṃ anusāsantassa satthu mayhaṃ sāsanakārinau auvādappaṭikarā sakalassa vaṭṭadukkhassa antaṃ pariyantaṃ appavattiṃ karissanti, dukkhassa vā antaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikarissantīti|

aṭṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9. dutiyanakuhanasuttavaṇṇanā

36. navamai abhiñ‍ñatthanti kusalādivibhāgaina khandhādivibhāgaina ca sabbadhammai abhivisiṭṭhaina ñāṇaina aviparītatau jānanatthaṃ| pariñ‍ñatthanti taibhūmakadhammai ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’ntiādinā parijānanatthaṃ samatik‍kamanatthañ‍ca| tattha abhiñ‍ñaiyyaabhijānanā catusac‍cavisayā| pariñ‍ñaiyyaparijānanā pana yadipi dukkhasac‍cavisayā, pahānasacchikiriyābhāvanābhisamayaihi pana vinā na pavattatīti pahānādayaupi idha gahitāti vaiditabbaṃ| saisaṃ anantarasuttai vuttatthamaiva|

navamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

10. saumanassasuttavaṇṇanā

37. dasamai sukhasaumanassabahulauti aittha sukhanti kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, saumanassanti caitasikaṃ| tasmā yassa kāyikaṃ caitasikañ‍ca sukhaṃ abhiṇhaṃ pavattati, sau sukhasaumanassabahulauti vuttau| yaunīti ‘‘catassau khau imā, sāriputta, yauniyau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.152) khandhakauṭṭhāsau yaunīti āgatau| ‘‘yauni haisā, bhūmija, phalassa adhigamāyā’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 3.226) kāraṇaṃ|

‘‘na  cāhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brūmi, yaunijaṃ mattisambhava’’nti ca|| (ma॰ ni॰ 2.457; dha॰ pa॰ 396; su॰ ni॰ 625)|

‘‘tamainaṃ kammajā vātā nibbattitvā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ samparivattaitvā mātu yaunimukhai sampaṭipādaintī’’ti ca ādīsu passāvamaggau| idha pana kāraṇaṃ  adhippaitaṃ| assāti anaina| āraddhāti paṭṭhapitā paggahitā paripuṇṇā sampāditā vā|

āsavānaṃ  khayāyāti aittha āsavantīti āsavā, cakkhutaupi…pai॰… manataupi savanti pavattantīti vuttaṃ hauti| dhammatau yāva gautrabhū, aukāsatau yāva bhavaggā savantīti vā āsavā| aitai dhammai aitañ‍ca aukāsaṃ antau karitvā pavattantīti atthau| antaukaraṇatthau hi ayaṃ ākārau| cirapārivāsiyaṭṭhaina madirādayau āsavā viyātipi āsavā| laukai hi cirapārivāsikā madirādayau āsavāti vuc‍canti| yadi ca cirapārivāsiyaṭṭhaina āsavā, aitai aiva bhavituṃ arahanti| vuttaṃ haitaṃ – ‘‘purimā, bhikkhavai, kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jāya, itau pubbai avij‍jā nāhausī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 10.61)| āyataṃ saṃsāradukkhaṃ savanti pasavantītipi āsavā| purimāni caittha nibbacanāni yattha kilaisā āsavāti āgatā, tattha yuj‍janti; pacchimaṃ kammaipi| na kaivalañ‍ca kammakilaisā aiva āsavā, apica khau nānappakārā upaddavāpi| abhidhammai hi ‘‘cattārau āsavā – kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, diṭṭhāsavau, avij‍jāsavau’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 1102) kāmarāgādayau kilaisā āsavāti āgatā| suttaipi ‘‘nāhaṃ, cunda, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃyaiva āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya dhammaṃ daisaimī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.182) aittha vivādamūlabhūtā kilaisā āsavāti āgatā|

‘‘yaina daivūpapatyassa, gandhabbau vā vihaṅgamau|

yakkhattaṃ yaina gacchaiyya, manussattañ‍ca abbajai|

tai mayhaṃ, āsavā khīṇā, viddhastā vinaḷīkatā’’ti|| (a॰ ni॰ 4.36) –

aittha taibhūmakaṃ kammaṃ avasaisā ca akusalā dhammā| ‘‘diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāyā’’ti (pārā॰ 39) aittha parūpaghātavippaṭisāravadhabandhādayau caiva apāyadukkhabhūtā nānappakārā upaddavā ca|

tai  panaitai āsavā vinayai ‘‘diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāyā’’ti dvaidhā āgatā| saḷāyatanai ‘‘tayaumai, āvusau, āsavā – kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, avij‍jāsavau’’ti  (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.321) tidhā āgatā| tathā añ‍ñaisu suttantaisu| abhidhammai taiyaiva diṭṭhāsavaina saddhiṃ catudhā āgatā| nibbaidhikapariyāyai pana ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, āsavā nirayagamanīyā, atthi āsavā tiracchānayaunigamanīyā, atthi āsavā paittivisayagamanīyā, atthi āsavā manussalaukagamanīyā, atthi āsavā daivalaukagamanīyā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.63) pañ‍cadhā āgatā| kammamaiva caittha āsavāti adhippaitaṃ| chak‍kanipātai ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, āsavā saṃvarā pahātabbā’’tiādinā (a॰ ni॰ 6.58) nayaina chadhā āgatā| sabbāsavapariyāyai taiyaiva dassanapahātabbaihi dhammaihi saddhiṃ sattadhā āgatā| idha pana abhidhammapariyāyaina cattārau āsavā adhippaitāti vaiditabbā|

khayāyāti aittha pana ‘‘yau āsavānaṃ khayau vayau bhaidau paribhaidau anic‍catā antaradhāna’’nti āsavānaṃ sarasabhaidau āsavānaṃ khayauti vuttau| ‘‘jānatau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, passatau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.15) aittha āsavānaṃ khīṇākārau natthibhāvau ac‍cantaṃ asamuppādau āsavakkhayauti vuttau|

‘‘saikhassa sikkhamānassa, ujumaggānusārinau|

khayasmiṃ paṭhamaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tatau añ‍ñā anantarā’’ti|| (itivu॰ 62) –

aittha ariyamaggau āsavakkhayauti vuttau| ‘‘āsavānaṃ khayā samaṇau hautī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.438) aittha phalaṃ|

‘‘paravaj‍jānupassissa, nic‍caṃ ujjhānasañ‍ñinau|

āsavā tassa vaḍḍhanti, ārā sau āsavakkhayā’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 253) –

aittha nibbānaṃ| idha pana phalaṃ sandhāya ‘‘āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti vuttaṃ, arahattaphalatthāyāti atthau|

saṃvaijanīyaisu ṭhānaisūti saṃvaigajanakaisu jātiādīsu saṃvaigavatthūsu| jāti, jarā, byādhi, maraṇaṃ, apāyadukkhaṃ, atītai vaṭṭamūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ, anāgatai vaṭṭamūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ, pac‍cuppan‍nai āhārapariyaiṭṭhimūlakaṃ  dukkhanti imāni hi saṃvaigavatthūni saṃvaijanīyaṭṭhānāni nāma| apica ‘‘ādittau laukasan‍nivāsau uyyuttau payātau kummaggappaṭipan‍nau, upanīyati laukau addhuvau, atāṇau laukau anabhissarau, assakau  laukau, sabbaṃ pahāya gamanīyaṃ, ūnau laukau atittau taṇhādāsau’’tiaivamādīni (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.117) caittha saṃvaijanīyaṭṭhānānīti vaiditabbāni| saṃvaijanaināti jātiādisaṃvaigavatthūni paṭic‍ca uppan‍nabhayasaṅkhātaina saṃvaijanaina| atthatau pana sahauttappañāṇaṃ saṃvaigau nāma|

saṃviggassāti gabbhauk‍kantikādivasaina anaikavidhaihi jātiādidukkhaihi saṃvaigajātassa| ‘‘saṃvaijitvā’’ti ca paṭhanti| yaunisau padhānaināti upāyapadhānaina, sammāvāyāmaināti atthau| sau hi yathā akusalā dhammā pahīyanti, kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, aivaṃ padahanatau uttamabhāvasādhanatau ca ‘‘padhāna’’nti vuc‍cati| tattha saṃvaigaina bhavādīsu kiñ‍ci tāṇaṃ laiṇaṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ apassantau tattha anaulīyantau alaggamānasau tappaṭipakkhaina ca vinivattitavisañ‍ñitau añ‍ñadatthu nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| sau kalyāṇamittasan‍nissayaina yaunisaumanasikārabahulau visuddhāsayappayaugau samathavipassanāsu yuttappayuttau sabbasmimpi saṅkhāragatai nibbindati viraj‍jati, vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaiti| tattha yadidaṃ yaunisaumanasikārabahulau visuddhāsayappayaugau samathavipassanāsu yuttappayuttau, tainassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhasaumanassabahulatā vaiditabbā| yaṃ panāyaṃ samathai patiṭṭhitau vipassanāya yuttappayuttau sabbasmimpi saṅkhāragatai nibbindati viraj‍jati, vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaiti, tainassa yauni āraddhā āsavānaṃ khayāyāti vaiditabbaṃ|

gāthāsu saṃvij‍jaithaivāti saṃvij‍jaiyya aiva saṃvaigaṃ karaiyya aiva| ‘‘saṃvij‍jitvānā’’ti ca paṭhanti| vuttanayaina saṃviggau hutvāti atthau| paṇḍitauti  sappañ‍ñau, tihaitukapaṭisandhīti vuttaṃ hauti| pañ‍ñāya samavaikkhiyāti saṃvaigavatthūni saṃvij‍janavasaina pañ‍ñāya sammā avaikkhiya| atha vā pañ‍ñāya sammā avaikkhitvāti| saisaṃ sabbattha uttānatthamaiva|

dasamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

iti paramatthadīpaniyā itivuttaka-aṭṭhakathāya

dukanipātai paṭhamavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. dutiyavaggau

1. vitak‍kasuttavaṇṇanā

38. dutiyavaggassa  paṭhamai tathāgataṃ, bhikkhavaiti aittha tathāgata-saddau tāva sattavauhārasammāsambuddhādīsu dissati| tathā haisa ‘‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’tiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.65) sattavauhārai|

‘‘tathāgataṃ daivamanussapūjitaṃ,

buddhaṃ namassāma suvatthi hautū’’ti|| (khu॰ pā॰ 6.16) –

ādīsu sammāsambuddhai|

‘‘tathāgataṃ daivamanussapūjitaṃ,

dhammaṃ namassāma suvatthi hautū’’ti|| (khu॰ pā॰ 6.17) –

ādīsu dhammai|

‘‘tathāgataṃ daivamanussapūjitaṃ,

saṅghaṃ namassāma suvatthi hautū’’ti|| (khu॰ pā॰ 6.18) –

ādīsu saṅghai| idha pana sammāsambuddhai| tasmā tathāgatanti aittha aṭṭhahi kāraṇaihi bhagavā tathāgatauti vuc‍cati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? tathā āgatauti tathāgatau, tathā gatauti tathāgatau, tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau, tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau, tathadassitāya tathāgatau, tathavāditāya tathāgatau, tathākāritāya tathāgatau, abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatauti|

kathaṃ bhagavā tathā āgatauti tathāgatau? yathā yaina abhinīhāraina dānapāramiṃ pūraitvā sīlanaikkhammapañ‍ñāvīriyakhantisac‍caadhiṭṭhānamaittāupaikkhāpāramiṃ pūraitvā imā dasa pāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyauti samatiṃsa pāramiyau pūraitvā aṅgaparic‍cāgaṃ, attaparic‍cāgaṃ, dhanaparic‍cāgaṃ, dāraparic‍cāgaṃ, raj‍japaric‍cāganti imāni pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgāni  paric‍cajitvā yathā  vipassiādayau sammāsambuddhā āgatā, tathā amhākaṃ bhagavāpi āgatauti tathāgatau| yathāha –

‘‘yathaiva laukamhi vipassiādayau,

sabbañ‍ñubhāvaṃ munayau idhāgatā|

tathā ayaṃ sakyamunīpi āgatau,

tathāgatau vuc‍cati taina cakkhumā’’ti|| –

aivaṃ tathā āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathā gatauti tathāgatau? yathā sampatijātāva vipassiādayau samaihi pādaihi pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya uttarābhimukhā sattapadavītihāraina gatā, tathā amhākaṃ bhagavāpi gatauti tathāgatau| yathāhu –

‘‘muhuttajātauva gavaṃpatī yathā,

samaihi pādaihi phusī vasundharaṃ|

sau vik‍kamī satta padāni gautamau,

saitañ‍ca chattaṃ anudhārayuṃ marū||

‘‘gantvāna sau satta padāni gautamau,

disā vilaukaisi samā samantatau|

aṭṭhaṅgupaitaṃ giramabbhudīrayi,

sīhau yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau’’ti|| –

aivaṃ tathā gatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau? sabbaisaṃ rūpārūpadhammānaṃ salakkhaṇaṃ, sāmañ‍ñalakkhaṇaṃ, tathaṃ, avitathaṃ, ñāṇagatiyā āgatau, avirajjhitvā pattau, anubuddhauti tathāgatau| yathāha –

‘‘sabbaisaṃ  pana dhammānaṃ, sakasāmañ‍ñalakkhaṇaṃ|

tathamaivāgatau yasmā, tasmā nāthau tathāgatau’’ti|| –

aivaṃ tathalakkhaṇaṃ āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau? tathadhammā nāma cattāri ariyasac‍cāni| yathāha ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāni  avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| katamāni cattāri? idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍canti, bhikkhavai, tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaita’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1090) vitthārau| tāni ca bhagavā abhisambuddhau, tasmāpi tathānaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgatau| abhisambuddhatthau hi aittha gata-saddau| aivaṃ tathadhammai yāthāvatau abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathadassitāya tathāgatau? yaṃ sadaivakai laukai…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya pajāya aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ cakkhudvārai āpāthamāgacchantaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ nāma atthi, taṃ bhagavā sabbākāratau jānāti passati| aivaṃ jānatā passatā cānaina taṃ iṭṭhādivasaina vā diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātaisu labbhamānapadavasaina vā ‘‘katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītaka’’ntiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 616) nayaina anaikaihi nāmaihi tairasahi vāraihi dvaipañ‍ñāsāya nayaihi vibhaj‍jamānaṃ tathamaiva hauti, vitathaṃ natthi| aisa nayau sautadvārādīsu āpāthamāgacchantaisu saddādīsu| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tamahaṃ jānāmi…pai॰… tamahaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, taṃ tathāgatassa viditaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau na upaṭṭhāsī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.24)|

aivaṃ tathadassitāya tathāgatau| aittha tathadassiatthai tathāgatauti padassa sambhavau vaiditabbau|

kathaṃ tathavāditāya tathāgatau? yaṃ rattiṃ bhagavā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, yañ‍ca  rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyi, aitthantarai pañ‍cacattālīsavassaparimāṇakālai yaṃ bhagavatā bhāsitaṃ suttagaiyyādi, sabbaṃ taṃ parisuddhaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ rāgamadādinimmadanaṃ aikasadisaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ| taināha –

‘‘yañ‍ca, cunda, rattiṃ tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, yaṃ  aitasmiṃ antarai bhāsati lapati niddisati, sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.188; a॰ ni॰ 4.23)|

gadaatthau hi aittha  gatasaddau| aivaṃ tathavāditāya tathāgatau| apica āgadanaṃ āgadau, vacananti atthau| tathau aviparītau āgadau assāti dakārassa takāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti, aivampaittha padasiddhi vaiditabbā|

kathaṃ tathākāritāya tathāgatau? bhagavatau hi vācāya kāyau anulaumaiti, kāyassapi vācā| tasmā yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī ca hauti| aivaṃbhūtassa cassa yathā vācā, kāyaupi tathā gatau pavattau| yathā ca kāyau, vācāpi tathā gatāti tathāgatau| taināha ‘‘yathāvādī, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| iti yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| aivaṃ tathākāritāya tathāgatau|

kathaṃ abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau? yasmā bhagavā upari bhavaggaṃ haiṭṭhā avīciṃ pariyantaṃ karitvā tiriyaṃ aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu sabbasattai abhibhavati sīlainapi samādhināpi pañ‍ñāyapi vimuttiyāpi vimuttiñāṇadassanainapi, na tassa tulā vā pamāṇaṃ vā atthi, atha khau atulau appamaiyyau anuttarau daivānaṃ atidaivau sak‍kānaṃ atisak‍kau brahmānaṃ atibrahmā sabbasattuttamau, tasmā tathāgatau| taināha –

‘‘sadaivakai, bhikkhavai, laukai…pai॰… manussāya tathāgatau abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthu dasau vasavattī, tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.188; a॰ ni॰ 4.23)|

tatrāyaṃ  padasiddhi – agadau viya agadau, daisanāvilāsau caiva puñ‍ñussayau ca| taina haisa mahānubhāvau bhisak‍kau viya dibbāgadaina sappai, sabbaparappavādinau sadaivakañ‍ca laukaṃ abhibhavati| iti sabbalaukābhibhavanai tathau aviparītau yathāvuttau agadau aitassāti dakārassa takāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti vaiditabbau| aivaṃ abhibhavanaṭṭhaina tathāgatau|

apica  tathāya gatauti tathāgatau, tathaṃ gatauti tathāgatau| tattha sakalalaukaṃ tīraṇapariñ‍ñāya tathāya gatau avagatauti tathāgatau, laukasamudayaṃ pahānapariñ‍ñāya tathāya gatau atītauti tathāgatau, laukaniraudhaṃ sacchikiriyāya tathāya gatau adhigatauti tathāgatau| laukaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ tathaṃ  gatau paṭipan‍nauti tathāgatau| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘laukau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau| laukasmā tathāgatau visaṃyuttau| laukasamudayau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau, laukasamudayau tathāgatassa pahīnau| laukaniraudhau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau, laukaniraudhau tathāgatassa sacchikatau| laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhā, laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā tathāgatassa bhāvitā| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa…pai॰… sabbaṃ taṃ tathāgataina abhisambuddhaṃ| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.23)|

aparaihipi aṭṭhahi kāraṇaihi bhagavā tathāgatau| tathāya āgatauti tathāgatau, tathāya gatauti tathāgatau, tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau, tathā gatauti tathāgatau, tathāvidhauti tathāgatau, tathāpavattikauti tathāgatau, tathaihi āgatauti tathāgatau, tathā gatabhāvaina tathāgatauti|

kathaṃ tathāya āgatauti tathāgatau? yā sā bhagavatā sumaidhabhūtaina dīpaṅkaradasabalassa pādamūlai –

‘‘manussattaṃ liṅgasampatti, haitu satthāradassanaṃ|

pabbaj‍jā guṇasampatti, adhikārau ca chandatā|

aṭṭhadhammasamaudhānā, abhinīhārau samijjhatī’’ti|| (bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.59) –

aivaṃ vuttaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ abhinīhāraṃ sampādaintaina ‘‘ahaṃ sadaivakaṃ laukaṃ tiṇṇau tāraissāmi, muttau maucaissāmi, dantau damaissāmi, assatthau assāsaissāmi, parinibbutau parinibbāpaissāmi, suddhau saudhaissāmi, buddhau baudhaissāmī’’ti mahāpaṭiñ‍ñā pavattitā| vuttaṃ haitaṃ –

‘‘kiṃ mai aikaina tiṇṇaina, purisaina thāmadassinā|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇitvā, santāraissaṃ sadaivakaṃ||

‘‘iminā mai adhikāraina, kataina purisuttamai|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇitvā, tāraimi janataṃ bahuṃ||

‘‘saṃsārasautaṃ  chinditvā, viddhaṃsaitvā tayau bhavai|

dhammanāvaṃ samāruyha, santāraissaṃ sadaivakaṃ||

‘‘kiṃ mai añ‍ñātavaisaina, dhammaṃ sacchikatainidha|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇitvā, buddhau haissaṃ sadaivakai’’ti|| (bu॰ vaṃ॰ 55-58)|

taṃ panaitaṃ mahāpaṭiñ‍ñaṃ sakalassapi buddhakaradhammasamudāyassa pavicayapac‍cavaikkhaṇasamādānānaṃ kāraṇabhūtaṃ avisaṃvādaintau laukanāthau yasmā mahākappānaṃ satasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni sak‍kac‍caṃ nirantaraṃ niravasaisatau dānapāramiādayau samatiṃsapāramiyau pūraitvā, aṅgaparic‍cāgādayau pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgai paric‍cajitvā, sac‍cādhiṭṭhānādīni cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni paribrūhaitvā, puñ‍ñañāṇasambhārai sambharitvā pubbayaugapubbacariyadhammakkhānañātatthacariyādayau uk‍kaṃsāpaitvā, buddhicariyaṃ paramakauṭiṃ pāpaitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhi; tasmā tassaiva sā mahāpaṭiñ‍ñā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā, na tassa vālaggamattampi vitathaṃ atthi| tathā hi dīpaṅkarau dasabalau kauṇḍañ‍ñau, maṅgalau…pai॰… kassapau bhagavāti imai catuvīsati sammāsambuddhā paṭipāṭiyā uppan‍nā ‘‘buddhau bhavissatī’’ti naṃ byākariṃsu| aivaṃ catuvīsatiyā buddhānaṃ santikai laddhabyākaraṇau yai tai katābhinīhāraihi baudhisattaihi laddhabbā ānisaṃsā, tai labhitvāva āgatauti tāya yathāvuttāya mahāpaṭiñ‍ñāya tathāya abhisambuddhabhāvaṃ āgatau adhigatauti tathāgatau| aivaṃ tathāya āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathāya gatauti tathāgatau? yāyaṃ mahākaruṇā laukanāthassa, yāya mahādukkhasambādhappaṭipan‍naṃ sattanikāyaṃ disvā ‘‘tassa  natthañ‍ñau kauci paṭisaraṇaṃ, ahamaiva naṃ itau  saṃsāradukkhatau muttau maucaissāmī’’ti samussāhitamānasau mahābhinīhāraṃ akāsi| katvā ca yathāpaṇidhānaṃ sakalalaukahitasampādanāya ussuk‍kamāpan‍nau attanau kāyajīvitanirapaikkhau paraisaṃ sautapathagamanamattainapi  cittutrāsasamuppādikā atiduk‍karā duk‍karacariyā samācarantau yathā mahābaudhisattānaṃ paṭipatti hānabhāgiyā saṃkilaisabhāgiyā ṭhitibhāgiyā vā na hauti, atha khau uttari visaisabhāgiyāva hauti, tathā paṭipaj‍jamānau anupubbaina niravasaisai baudhisambhārai samānaitvā abhisambaudhiṃ pāpuṇi| tatau parañ‍ca tāyaiva mahākaruṇāya sañ‍cauditamānasau pavivaikaratiṃ paramañ‍ca santaṃ vimaukkhasukhaṃ pahāya bālajanabahulai laukai taihi samuppāditaṃ sammānāvamānavippakāraṃ agaṇaitvā vainaiyyajanavinayanaina niravasaisaṃ buddhakic‍caṃ niṭṭhapaisi| tatra yau bhagavatau sattaisu mahākaruṇāya samauk‍kamanākārau, sau paratau āvi bhavissati| yathā buddhabhūtassa laukanāthassa sattaisu mahākaruṇā, aivaṃ baudhisattabhūtassapi mahābhinīhārakālādīsūti sabbattha sabbadā ca aikasadisatāya tathāva sā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| tasmā tīsupi avatthāsu sabbasattaisu samānarasāya tathāya mahākaruṇāya sakalalaukahitāya gatau paṭipan‍nauti tathāgatau| aivaṃ tathāya gatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau? tathāni nāma cattāri ariyamaggañāṇāni| tāni hi ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti aivaṃ sabbañaiyyasaṅgāhakānaṃ pavattinivattitadubhayahaitubhūtānaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ, dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau saṅkhataṭṭhau santāpaṭṭhau vipariṇāmaṭṭhau, samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau nidānaṭṭhau saṃyaugaṭṭhau palibaudhaṭṭhau, niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau vivaikaṭṭhau asaṅkhataṭṭhau amataṭṭhau, maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau haitvaṭṭhau dassanaṭṭhau adhipataiyyaṭṭhautiādīnaṃ tabbibhāgānañ‍ca yathābhūtasabhāvāvabaudhavibandhakassa saṃkilaisapakkhassa samucchindanaina paṭiladdhāya tattha asammauhābhisamayasaṅkhātāya aviparītākārappavattiyā dhammānaṃ sabhāvasarasalakkhaṇassa avisaṃvādanatau tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni, tāni  bhagavā  anañ‍ñanaiyyau sayamaiva āgatau adhigatau, tasmā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

yathā ca maggañāṇāni, aivaṃ bhagavatau tīsu kālaisu appaṭihatañāṇāni catupaṭisambhidāñāṇāni catuvaisāraj‍jañāṇāni pañ‍cagatiparicchaidañāṇāni chaasādhāraṇañāṇāni sattabaujjhaṅgavibhāvanañāṇāni aṭṭhamaggaṅgavibhāvanañāṇāni navānupubbavihārasamāpattiñāṇāni dasabalañāṇāni ca vibhāvaitabbāni|

tatrāyaṃ  vibhāvanā – yañhi kiñ‍ci aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ hīnādibhaidabhin‍nānaṃ hīnādibhaidabhin‍nāsu atītāsu khandhāyatanadhātūsu sabhāvakic‍cādi avatthāvisaisādi khandhapaṭibaddhanāmagauttādi ca jānitabbaṃ| anindriyabaddhaisu ca atisukhumatirauhitavidūradaisaisu rūpadhammaisu yau taṃtaṃpac‍cayavisaisaihi saddhiṃ pac‍cayuppan‍nānaṃ vaṇṇasaṇṭhānagandharasaphassādivisaisau, tattha sabbatthaiva hatthatalai ṭhapitaāmalakau viya pac‍cakkhatau asaṅgamappaṭihataṃ bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ pavattati, tathā anāgatāsu pac‍cuppan‍nāsu cāti imāni tīsu kālaisu appaṭihatañāṇāni nāma| yathāha –

‘‘atītaṃsai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, anāgataṃsai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, pac‍cuppan‍naṃsai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.5)|

tāni panaitāni tattha tattha dhammānaṃ sabhāvasarasalakkhaṇassa avisaṃvādanatau tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni, tāni bhagavā sayambhuñāṇaina adhigañchi| aivaṃ tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāti catassau paṭisambhidā| tattha atthapabhaidassa sal‍lakkhaṇavibhāvanavavatthānakaraṇasamatthaṃ atthai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| dhammapabhaidassa sal‍lakkhaṇavibhāvanavavatthānakaraṇasamatthaṃ dhammai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| niruttipabhaidassa sal‍lakkhaṇavibhāvanavavatthānakaraṇasamatthaṃ niruttābhilāpai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| paṭibhānapabhaidassa  sal‍lakkhaṇavibhāvanavavatthānakaraṇasamatthaṃ  paṭibhānai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘atthai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā’’ti (vibha॰ 718)|

aittha ca haituanusāraina araṇīyatau adhigantabbatau ca saṅkhaipatau haituphalaṃ atthau nāma| pabhaidatau pana yaṃkiñ‍ci pac‍cayuppan‍naṃ, nibbānaṃ, bhāsitatthau, vipākau, kiriyāti imai pañ‍ca dhammā atthau| taṃ atthaṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa tasmiṃ atthai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| dhammauti  saṅkhaipatau pac‍cayau| sau hi yasmā taṃ taṃ atthaṃ vidahati pavattaiti caiva pāpaiti ca, tasmā dhammauti vuc‍cati| pabhaidatau pana yau kauci phalanibbattakau haitu, ariyamaggau, bhāsitaṃ, kusalaṃ, akusalanti imai pañ‍ca dhammā dhammau, taṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa tasmiṃ dhammai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘dukkhai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā’’ti (vibha॰ 719)|

atha vā haitumhi ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, haituphalai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yai dhammā jātā bhūtā sañ‍jātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā, imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yamhā dhammā tai dhammā jātā bhūtā sañ‍jātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā, taisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| jarāmaraṇai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, jarāmaraṇasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| jarāmaraṇaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| jātiyā, bhavai, upādānai, taṇhāya, vaidanāya, phassai, saḷāyatanai, nāmarūpai, viñ‍ñāṇai, saṅkhāraisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, saṅkhārasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| saṅkhāraniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā|

‘‘idha  bhikkhu dhammaṃ jānāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ…pai॰… vaidal‍laṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati dhammapaṭisambhidā| sau tassa tassaiva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti – ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau, ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau’ti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati atthapaṭisambhidā (vibha॰ 724)|

‘‘katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā’’tiādi vitthārau (vibha॰ 725)|

tasmiṃ  atthai ca dhammai ca sabhāvanirutti abyabhicāravauhārau abhilāpau, tasmiṃ sabhāvaniruttābhilāpai māgadhikāya sabbasattānaṃ mūlabhāsāya ‘‘ayaṃ sabhāvanirutti, ayaṃ na sabhāvaniruttī’’ti pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yathāvuttaisu taisu ñāṇaisu gaucarakic‍cādivasaina vitthāratau pavattaṃ sabbampi ñāṇamārammaṇaṃ katvā pac‍cavaikkhantassa tasmiṃ ñāṇai pabhaidagataṃ ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| iti imāni cattāri paṭisambhidāñāṇāni sayamaiva bhagavatā adhigatāni atthadhammādikai tasmiṃ tasmiṃ attanau visayai avisaṃvādanavasaina aviparītākārappavattiyā tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā yaṃ kiñ‍ci ñaiyyaṃ nāma, sabbaṃ taṃ bhagavatā sabbākāraina ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ adhigataṃ abhisambuddhaṃ| tathā hissa abhiñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyatau buddhā, pariñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyatau buddhā, pahātabbā dhammā pahātabbatau buddhā, sacchikātabbā dhammā sacchikātabbatau buddhā, bhāvaitabbā dhammā bhāvaitabbatau buddhā, yatau naṃ kauci samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā ‘‘imai nāma tai dhammā anabhisambuddhā’’ti saha dhammaina anuyuñ‍jituṃ samatthau natthi|

yaṃ  kiñ‍ci pahātabbaṃ nāma, sabbaṃ taṃ bhagavatā anavasaisatau baudhimūlaiyaiva pahīnaṃ anuppattidhammaṃ, na tassa pahānāya uttari karaṇīyaṃ atthi| tathā hissa laubhadausamauhaviparītamanasikāraahirikānauttappathinamiddha- kaudhūpanāhamakkhapalāsaissāmacchariya- māyāsāṭhaiyyathambhasārambhamānātimānamadapamādatividhākusalamūladuc‍carita- visamasañ‍ñāmalavitak‍kapapañ‍caaisanātaṇhācatubbidhavipariyaisaāsava- ganthaaughayaugāgatitaṇhupādānapañ‍cābhinandananīvaraṇa- caitaukhilacaitasauvinibandhachavivādamūlasattānusaya- aṭṭhamicchattanavaāghātavatthutaṇhāmūlakadasaakusala- kammapathaaikavīsatianaisanadvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigataaṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritādippabhaidaṃ diyaḍḍhakilaisasahassaṃ saha vāsanāya pahīnaṃ samucchin‍naṃ samūhataṃ, yatau naṃ kauci samaṇau vā…pai॰… brahmā vā ‘‘imai nāma tai kilaisā appahīnā’’ti saha dhammaina anuyuñ‍jituṃ samatthau natthi|

yai cimai bhagavatā kammavipākakilaisūpavādaāṇāvītik‍kamappabhaidā antarāyikā dhammā vuttā, alamaiva tai aikantaina antarāyāya, yatau naṃ kauci samaṇau vā…pai॰… brahmā vā ‘‘nālaṃ tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāyā’’ti saha dhammaina anuyuñ‍jituṃ samatthau natthi|

yau  ca bhagavatā niravasaisavaṭṭadukkhanissaraṇāya sīlasamādhipañ‍ñāsaṅgahau sattakauṭṭhāsikau sattatiṃsappabhaidau ariyamaggapubbaṅgamau anuttarau niyyānadhammau daisitau, sau aikantainaiva niyyāti paṭipan‍nassa vaṭṭadukkhatau, yatau naṃ kauci samaṇau vā…pai॰… brahmā vā ‘‘niyyānadhammau tayā daisitau na niyyātī’’ti saha dhammaina anuyuñ‍jituṃ samatthau natthi| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘sammāsambuddhassa tai paṭijānatau imai dhammā anabhisambuddhā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.150) vitthārau| aivamaitāni attanau ñāṇappahānadaisanāvisaisānaṃ avitathabhāvāvabaudhanatau aviparītākārappavattāni bhagavatau catuvaisāraj‍jañāṇāni tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā nirayagati, tiracchānagati, paitagati, manussagati, daivagatīti pañ‍ca gatiyau| tāsu sañ‍jīvādayau aṭṭha mahānirayā, kuk‍kuḷādayau sauḷasa ussadanirayā, laukantarikanirayau cāti sabbaipimai aikantadukkhatāya nirassādaṭṭhaina nirayā ca, sakakammunā gantabbatau gati cāti nirayagati| tibbandhakārasītanarakāpi aitaisvaiva antaugadhā kimikīṭapaṭaṅgasarīsapapakkhisauṇasiṅgālādayau tiriyaṃ añchitabhāvaina tiracchānā nāma| tai aiva gatīti tiracchānagati| khuppipāsitaparadattūpajīvinijjhāmataṇhikādayau dukkhabahulatāya pakaṭṭhasukhatau itā vigatāti paitā, tai aiva gatīti paitagati| kālakañ‍cikādiasurāpi aitaisvaiva antaugadhā| parittadīpavāsīhi saddhiṃ jambudīpādicatumahādīpavāsinau manasau ussan‍natāya manussā, tai aiva gatīti manussagati| cātumahārājikatau paṭṭhāya yāva naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagāti imai chabbīsati daivanikāyā dibbanti attanau iddhānubhāvaina kīḷanti jautainti cāti daivā, tai aiva gatīti daivagati|

tā panaitā gatiyau yasmā taṃtaṃkammanibbattau upapattibhavavisaisau, tasmā atthatau vipākakkhandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| tattha ‘‘ayaṃ nāma gati nāma iminā kammunā jāyati, tassa kammassa pac‍cayavisaisaihi aivaṃ vibhāgabhin‍nattā visuṃ aitai sattanikāyā aivaṃ vibhāgabhin‍nā’’ti yathāsakaṃhaituphalavibhāgaparicchindanavasaina ṭhānasau haitusau bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ pavattati| taināha bhagavā –

‘‘pañ‍ca khau imā, sāriputta, gatiyau| katamā pañ‍ca? nirayau, tiracchānayauni, paittivisayau, manussā, daivā| nirayañ‍cāhaṃ, sāriputta, pajānāmi, nirayagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ, nirayagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ; yathā paṭipan‍nau ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā  apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tañ‍ca pajānāmī’’tiādi (ma॰ ni॰ 1.153)|

tāni panaitāni bhagavatau ñāṇāni tasmiṃ tasmiṃ visayai aviparītākārappavattiyā avisaṃvādanatau tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā yaṃ sattānaṃ saddhādiyaugavikalabhāvāvabaudhaina apparajakkhamahārajakkhatādivisaisavibhāvanaṃ pañ‍ñāsāya ākāraihi pavattaṃ bhagavatau indriyaparaupariyattañāṇaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ  – ‘‘saddhau puggalau apparajakkhau, assaddhau puggalau mahārajakkhau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.111) vitthārau|

yañ‍ca  ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau apparajakkhau, ayaṃ sassatadiṭṭhikau, ayaṃ ucchaidadiṭṭhikau, ayaṃ anulaumikāyaṃ khantiyaṃ ṭhitau, ayaṃ yathābhūtañāṇai ṭhitau, ayaṃ kāmāsayau, na naikkhammādiāsayau, ayaṃ naikkhammāsayau, na kāmādiāsayau’’tiādinā ‘‘imassa kāmarāgau ativiya thāmagatau, na paṭighādikau, imassa paṭighau ativiya thāmagatau, na kāmarāgādikau’’tiādinā ‘‘imassa puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau adhikau, na apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau na ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau, imassa apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau adhikau, na puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau na ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau, imassa ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau adhikau, na puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau na apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau| imassa kāyasucaritaṃ adhikaṃ, imassa vacīsucaritaṃ, imassa manausucaritaṃ| ayaṃ hīnādhimuttikau, ayaṃ paṇītādhimuttikau, ayaṃ kammāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, ayaṃ kilaisāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, ayaṃ vipākāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, ayaṃ na kammāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau, na kilaisāvaraṇaina, na vipākāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatau’’tiādinā ca sattānaṃ āsayādīnaṃ yathābhūtaṃ vibhāvanākārappavattaṃ bhagavatau āsayānusayañāṇaṃ| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘idha tathāgatau sattānaṃ āsayaṃ jānāti, anusayaṃ jānāti, caritaṃ jānāti, adhimuttiṃ jānāti, bhabbābhabbai sattai jānātī’’tiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.113)|

yañ‍ca uparimahaiṭṭhimapuratthimapacchimakāyaihi dakkhiṇavāmaakkhikaṇṇasautanāsikāsautaaṃsakūṭapassahatthapādaihi aṅgulaṅgulantaraihi laumalaumakūpaihi ca aggikkhandhūdakadhārāpavattanaṃ anañ‍ñasādhāraṇaṃ  vividhavikubbaniddhinimmāpanakaṃ bhagavatau yamakapāṭihāriyañāṇaṃ| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ –

‘‘idha tathāgatau yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ karauti asādhāraṇaṃ sāvakaihi| uparimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, haiṭṭhimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattati| haiṭṭhimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, uparimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattatī’’tiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.116)|

yañ‍ca  rāgādīhi jātiādīhi ca anaikaihi dukkhadhammaihi  upaddutaṃ sattanikāyaṃ tatau nīharitukāmatāvasaina nānānayaihi pavattassa bhagavatau mahākaruṇauk‍kamanassa pac‍cayabhūtaṃ mahākaruṇāsamāpattiñāṇaṃ| yathāha –

‘‘katamaṃ tathāgatassa mahākaruṇāsamāpattiñāṇaṃ? bahukaihi ākāraihi passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamati, ādittau laukasan‍nivāsauti passantānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ sattaisu mahākaruṇā auk‍kamatī’’ti| –

ādinā (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.117) aikūnanavutiyā ākāraihi vibhajanaṃ kataṃ|

yaṃ pana yāvatā dhammadhātu, yattakaṃ ñātabbaṃ saṅkhatāsaṅkhatādi, tassa sabbassa paraupadaisaina vinā sabbākāratau paṭijānanasamatthaṃ ākaṅkhāmattappaṭibaddhavutti anañ‍ñasādhāraṇaṃ bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ sabbathā anavasaisasaṅkhatāsaṅkhatasammutisac‍cāvabaudhatau sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ, tatthāvaraṇābhāvatauva nissaṅgappavattiṃ upādāya anāvaraṇañāṇanti ca vuc‍cati| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau, vitthārau pana paratau āvi bhavissati|

aivamaitāni bhagavatau cha asādhāraṇañāṇāni aviparītākārappavattiyā yathāsakaṃvisayassa avisaṃvādanatau tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.17; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.185) aivaṃ sarūpatau yāyaṃ laukuttaramaggakkhaṇai  uppaj‍jamānā līnuddhac‍capatiṭṭhānāyūhanakāmasukhattakilamathānuyaugaucchaidasassatābhinivaisādīnaṃ anaikaisaṃ upaddavānaṃ paṭipakkhabhūtā satiādibhaidā dhammasāmaggī, yāya ariyasāvakau bujjhati, kilaisaniddāya uṭṭhahati, cattāri vā sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati, nibbānamaiva vā sacchikarauti, sā dhammasāmaggī ‘‘baudhī’’ti vuc‍cati, tassā baudhiyā aṅgāti baujjhaṅgā| ariyasāvakau vā yathāvuttāya dhammasāmaggiyā bujjhatīti katvā ‘‘baudhī’’ti vuc‍cati| tassa baudhissa aṅgāti baujjhaṅgāti aivaṃ  sāmañ‍ñalakkhaṇatau, upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇau satisambaujjhaṅgau, pavicayalakkhaṇau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, paggahalakkhaṇau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pharaṇalakkhaṇau pītisambaujjhaṅgau, upasamalakkhaṇau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, avikkhaipalakkhaṇau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau paṭisaṅkhānalakkhaṇau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgauti aivaṃ visaisalakkhaṇatau|

‘‘tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? idha bhikkhu satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā hauti anussaritā’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 467) sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñūpakāravasaina aikakkhaṇai pavattidassanatau| ‘‘tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? atthi ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu sati, atthi bahiddhā dhammaisu satī’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 469) taisaṃ visayavibhāvanāpavattidassanatau| ‘‘tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ, virāganissitaṃ, niraudhanissitaṃ, vausaggapariṇāmi’’ntiādinā (vibha॰ 471) bhāvanāvidhidassanatau| ‘‘tattha katamai satta baujjhaṅgā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… tasmiṃ samayai satta baujjhaṅgā haunti, satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? yā sati anussatī’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 478) chanavutiyā nayasahassavibhāgaihīti aivaṃ nānākāratau pavattāni bhagavatau baujjhaṅgavibhāvanañāṇāni tassa tassa atthassa avisaṃvādanatau tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā ‘‘tattha katamaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhī’’ti (vibha॰ 205) aivaṃ sarūpatau| sabbakilaisaihi ārakattā ariyabhāvakarattā ariyaphalapaṭilābhakarattā ca ariyau| ariyānaṃ aṭṭhavidhattā nibbānādhigamāya aikantakāraṇattā ca aṭṭhaṅgikau| kilaisai māraintau gacchati, atthikaihi maggīyati, sayaṃ vā nibbānaṃ maggayatīti maggauti aivaṃ sāmañ‍ñalakkhaṇatau| ‘‘sammādassanalakkhaṇā sammādiṭṭhi, sammāabhiniraupanalakkhaṇau sammāsaṅkappau, sammāpariggahaṇalakkhaṇā sammāvācā, sammāsamuṭṭhāpanalakkhaṇau sammākammantau, sammāvaudānalakkhaṇau sammāājīvau, sammāpaggahalakkhaṇau sammāvāyāmau, sammāupaṭṭhānalakkhaṇā sammāsati, sammāavikkhaipalakkhaṇau sammāsamādhī’’ti aivaṃ visaisalakkhaṇatau| sammādiṭṭhi tāva añ‍ñaihipi attanau pac‍canīkakilaisaihi saddhiṃ micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati, nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ karauti, tappaṭicchādakamauhavidhamanaina asammauhatau sampayuttadhammai ca passati, tathā sammāsaṅkappādayaupi micchāsaṅkappādīni pajahanti, niraudhañ‍ca ārammaṇaṃ karaunti, sahajātadhammānaṃ sammāabhiniraupanapariggahaṇasamuṭṭhāpanavaudānapaggahaupaṭṭhānasamādahanāni ca karauntīti aivaṃ kic‍cavibhāgatau| sammādiṭṭhi pubbabhāgai nānakkhaṇā visuṃ dukkhādiārammaṇā hutvā maggakālai aikakkhaṇā nibbānamaiva ārammaṇaṃ katvā kic‍catau ‘‘dukkhai ñāṇa’’ntiādīni cattāri nāmāni labhati| sammāsaṅkappādayaupi pubbabhāgai nānakkhaṇā nānārammaṇā, maggakālai aikakkhaṇā aikārammaṇā, taisu sammāsaṅkappau kic‍catau ‘‘naikkhammasaṅkappau’’tiādīni tīṇi nāmāni labhati| sammāvācādayau tayau pubbabhāgai ‘‘musāvādā vairamaṇī’’tiādivibhāgā viratiyaupi caitanāyaupi hutvā maggakkhaṇai viratiyauva, sammāvāyāmasatiyau kic‍catau sammappadhānasatipaṭṭhānavasaina cattāri nāmāni labhanti| sammāsamādhi pana maggakkhaṇaipi paṭhamajjhānādivasaina nānā aivāti aivaṃ pubbabhāgāparabhāgaisu pavattivibhāgatau| ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissita’’ntiādinā (vibha॰ 489) bhāvanāvidhitau| ‘‘tattha katamau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhu, yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau hauti – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 499) caturāsītiyā nayasahassavibhāgaihīti aivaṃ anaikākāratau pavattāni bhagavatau ariyamaggavibhāvanañāṇāni atthassa avisaṃvādanatau sabbānipi tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

tathā paṭhamajjhānasamāpattiyā ca niraudhasamāpattīti aitāsu anupaṭipāṭiyā viharitabbaṭṭhaina samāpaj‍jitabbaṭṭhaina ca anupubbavihārasamāpattīsu sampādanapac‍cavaikkhaṇādivasaina yathārahaṃ sampayaugavasaina ca pavattāni bhagavatau ñāṇāni tadatthasiddhiyā tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau| tathā ‘‘idaṃ imassa ṭhānaṃ, idaṃ aṭṭhāna’’nti aviparītaṃ tassa tassa phalassa kāraṇākāraṇajānanaṃ, taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ atītādibhaidabhin‍nassa kammasamādānassa anavasaisatau yathābhūtaṃ vipākantarajānanaṃ, āyūhanakkhaṇaiyaiva tassa tassa sattassa ‘‘ayaṃ nirayagāminī paṭipadā…pai॰… ayaṃ nibbānagāminī  paṭipadā’’ti yāthāvatau sāsavānāsavakammavibhāgajānanaṃ, khandhāyatanānaṃ upādin‍nānupādin‍nādianaikasabhāvaṃ nānāsabhāvañ‍ca tassa laukassa ‘‘imāya nāma dhātuyā ussan‍nattā imasmiṃ dhammappabandhai ayaṃ visaisau jāyatī’’tiādinā nayaina yathābhūtaṃ dhātunānattajānanaṃ, saddhādiindriyānaṃ tikkhamudutājānanaṃ saṃkilaisādīhi saddhiṃ jhānavimaukkhādijānanaṃ, sattānaṃ aparimāṇāsu jātīsu tappaṭibandhaina saddhiṃ anavasaisatau pubbainivutthakkhandhasantatijānanaṃ hīnādivibhāgaihi saddhiṃ cutipaṭisandhijānanaṃ, ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’ntiādinā haiṭṭhā vuttanayainaiva catusac‍cajānananti imāni bhagavatau dasabalañāṇāni avirajjhitvā yathāsakaṃvisayāvagāhanatau yathādhippaitatthasādhanatau ca yathābhūtavuttiyā tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘idha tathāgatau ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānātī’’tiādi (vibha॰ 809; a॰ ni॰ 10.21)|

aivampi bhagavā tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

yathā caitaisampi ñāṇānaṃ vasaina, aivaṃ yathāvuttānaṃ satipaṭṭhānasammappadhānādivibhāvanañāṇādianantāparimaiyyabhaidānaṃ anañ‍ñasādhāraṇānaṃ pañ‍ñāvisaisānaṃ vasaina bhagavā tathāni ñāṇāni āgatau adhigatauti tathāgatau, aivampi tathāni āgatauti tathāgatau|

kathaṃ tathā gatauti tathāgatau? yā tā bhagavatau abhijātiabhisambaudhidhammavinayapañ‍ñāpanaanupādisaisanibbānadhātuyau, tā tathā| kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti? yadatthaṃ tā laukanāthaina abhipatthitā pavattitā ca, tadatthassa aikantasiddhiyā avisaṃvādanatau aviparītatthavuttiyā tathā avitathā  anañ‍ñathā| tathā hi ayaṃ bhagavā baudhisattabhūtau samatiṃsapāramiparipūraṇādikaṃ vuttappakāraṃ sabbabuddhattahaituṃ sampādaitvā  tusitapurai ṭhitau buddhakaulāhalaṃ sutvā dasasahassacak‍kavāḷadaivatāhi aikatau san‍nipatitāhi upasaṅkamitvā –

‘‘kālau khau tai mahāvīra, uppaj‍ja mātukucchiyaṃ|

sadaivakaṃ tārayantau, bujjhassu amataṃ pada’’nti|| (bu॰ vaṃ॰ 1.67) –

āyācitau uppan‍napubbanimittau pañ‍ca mahāvilaukanāni vilaukaitvā ‘‘idāni ahaṃ manussayauniyaṃ  uppaj‍jitvā abhisambujjhissāmī’’ti āsāḷhipuṇṇamāyaṃ sakyarājakulai mahāmāyāya daiviyā kucchiyaṃ paṭisandhiṃ gahaitvā dasa māsai daivamanussaihi mahatā parihāraina parihariyamānau visākhapuṇṇamāyaṃ pac‍cūsasamayai abhijātiṃ pāpuṇi|

abhijātikkhaṇai panassa paṭisandhiggahaṇakkhaṇai viya dvattiṃsa pubbanimittāni pāturahaisuṃ, ayaṃ dasasahassilaukadhātu saṃkampi sampakampi sampavaidhi, dasasu cak‍kavāḷasahassaisu aparimāṇau aubhāsau phari, tassa, taṃ siriṃ daṭṭhukāmā viya andhā cakkhūni paṭilabhiṃsu, badhirā saddaṃ suṇiṃsu, mūgā samālapiṃsu, khuj‍jā ujugattā ahaisuṃ, paṅgulā padasā gamanaṃ paṭilabhiṃsu, bandhanagatā sabbasattā andubandhanādīhi muc‍ciṃsu, sabbanarakaisu aggi nibbāyi, paittivisayai khuppipāsā vūpasami, tiracchānānaṃ bhayaṃ nāhausi, sabbasattānaṃ raugau vūpasami, sabbasattā piyaṃvadā ahaisuṃ, madhurainākāraina assā hasiṃsu, vāraṇā gaj‍jiṃsu, sabbatūriyāni sakasakanin‍nādaṃ muñ‍ciṃsu, aghaṭṭitāni aiva manussānaṃ hatthūpagādīni ābharaṇāni madhurainākāraina saddaṃ muñ‍ciṃsu, sabbadisā vippasan‍nā ahaisuṃ, sattānaṃ sukhaṃ uppādayamānau mudusītalavātau vāyi, akālamaighau vassi, pathavitaupi udakaṃ ubbhij‍jitvā vissandi, pakkhinau ākāsagamanaṃ vijahiṃsu, nadiyau asandamānā aṭṭhaṃsu, mahāsamuddai madhuraṃ udakaṃ ahausi, upak‍kilaisavinimuttai sūriyai dippamānai aiva ākāsagatā sabbā jautiyau jautiṃsu, ṭhapaitvā arūpāvacarai daivai avasaisā sabbai daivā sabbai ca nairayikā dissamānarūpā ahaisuṃ, tarukuṭṭakavāṭasailādayau  anāvaraṇabhūtā ahaisuṃ, sattānaṃ cutūpapātā nāhaisuṃ, sabbaṃ aniṭṭhagandhaṃ abhibhavitvā dibbagandhau pavāyi, sabbai phalūpagā rukkhā phaladharā sampaj‍jiṃsu, mahāsamuddau sabbatthakamaiva pañ‍cavaṇṇaihi padumaihi sañchan‍natalau ahausi, thalajajalajādīni  sabbapupphāni pupphiṃsu, rukkhānaṃ khandhaisu khandhapadumāni, sākhāsu sākhāpadumāni, latāsu latāpadumāni,
