gāthāsu yataṃ caraiti yatamānau caraiyya, caṅkamanādivasaina gamanaṃ kappaintaupi ‘‘anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamatī’’tiādinā  (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.651-662; vibha॰ 390) nayaina vuttasammappadhānavīriyavasaina yatantau ghaṭaintau vāyamantau yathā akusalā dhammā pahīyanti, kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, aivaṃ gamanaṃ  kappaiyyāti atthau| aisa nayau saisaisupi| kaici pana ‘‘yata’’nti aitassa saṃyatauti atthaṃ vadanti| tiṭṭhaiti tiṭṭhaiyya ṭhānaṃ kappaiyya| acchaiti nisīdaiyya| sayaiti nipaj‍jaiyya| yatamainaṃ pasārayaiti aitaṃ pasāraitabbaṃ hatthapādādiṃ yataṃ yatamānau yathāvuttavīriyasamaṅgīyaiva hutvā pasāraiyya, sabbattha pamādaṃ vijahaiyyāti adhippāyau|

idāni yathā paṭipaj‍jantau yataṃ yatamānau nāma hauti, taṃ paṭipadaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘uddha’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha uddhanti upari| tiriyanti tiriyatau, puratthimadisādivasaina samantatau disābhāgaisūti atthau| apācīnanti haiṭṭhā| yāvatā jagatau gatīti yattakā sattasaṅkhārabhaidassa laukassa pavatti, tattha sabbatthāti atthau| aittāvatā anavasaisatau sammasanañāṇassa visayaṃ saṅgahaitvā dassaiti| samavaikkhitāti sammā haitunā ñāyaina avaikkhitā, anic‍cādivasaina vipassakauti vuttaṃ hauti| dhammānanti sattasuñ‍ñānaṃ| khandhānanti rūpādīnaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ| udayabbayanti udayañ‍ca vayañ‍ca| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – upari tiriyaṃ adhauti tisaṅgahai sabbasmiṃ laukai atītādibhaidabhin‍nānaṃ pañ‍cupādānakkhandhasaṅkhātānaṃ sabbaisaṃ rūpārūpadhammānaṃ anic‍catādisammasanādhigataina udayabbayañāṇaina pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi udayaṃ, pañ‍cavīsatiyā ākāraihi vayañ‍ca samavaikkhitā samanupassitā bhavaiyyāti|

caitausamathasāmīcinti  cittasaṃkilaisānaṃ ac‍cantavūpasamanatau  caitausamathasaṅkhātassa ariyamaggassa anucchavikapaṭipadaṃ ñāṇadassanavisuddhiṃ| sikkhamānanti paṭipaj‍jamānaṃ bhāvaintaṃ ñāṇaparamparaṃ nibbattaintaṃ| sadāti sabbakālaṃ, rattiñ‍caiva divā ca| satanti catusampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatāya satiyā sataukāriṃ| satataṃ pahitattauti sabbakālaṃ pahitattau nibbānaṃ paṭipaisitattauti tathāvidhaṃ bhikkhuṃ buddhādayau ariyā āhu ācikkhanti kathainti| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

dvādasamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

13. laukasuttavaṇṇanā

112. tairasamai laukauti luj‍janapaluj‍janaṭṭhaina laukau, atthatau purimaṃ ariyasac‍cadvayaṃ idha pana dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ vaiditabbaṃ| svāyaṃ sattalaukau, saṅkhāralaukau, aukāsalaukauti vibhāgatau  sarūpatau ca haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| apica khandhalaukādivasaina ca anaikavidhau laukau| yathāha –

‘‘laukauti khandhalaukau, dhātulaukau, āyatanalaukau, vipattibhavalaukau, vipattisambhavalaukau, sampattibhavalaukau, sampattisambhavalaukau, aikau laukau sabbai sattā ahāraṭṭhitikā, dvai laukā nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, tayau laukā tissau vaidanā, cattārau laukā cattārau āhārā, pañ‍ca laukā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, cha laukā cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni, satta laukā satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau, aṭṭha laukā aṭṭha laukadhammā, nava laukā nava sattāvāsā, dasa laukā dasāyatanāni, dvādasa laukā dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa laukā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau’’ti (mahāni॰ 3; cūḷani॰ ajitamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 2)|

aivamanaikadhā vibhattaupi laukau pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu aiva saṅgahaṃ samausaraṇaṃ gacchati, upādānakkhandhā ca dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ jātipi dukkhā  …pai॰… saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhāpi dukkhāti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘atthatau purimaṃ ariyasac‍cadvayaṃ, idha pana dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ vaiditabba’’nti| nanu ca luj‍janapaluj‍janaṭṭhau avisaisaina pañ‍casu khandhaisu sambhavatīti? sac‍caṃ sambhavati| yaṃ pana na luj‍jatīti gahitaṃ, taṃ tathā na hauti, aikaṃsainaiva luj‍jati paluj‍jatīti  sau laukauti upādānakkhandhaisvaiva laukasaddau nirūḷhauti vaiditabbau| tasmā laukauti dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ aiva|

yadipi tathāgata-saddassa haiṭṭhā tathāgatasuttai nānānayaihi vitthāratau atthau vibhattau, tathāpi pāḷiyā atthasaṃvaṇṇanāmukhaina ayamatthavibhāvanā – abhisambuddhauti ‘‘abhiñ‍ñaiyyatau pariñ‍ñaiyyatau’’ti pubbai vuttavibhāgaina vā avisaisatau tāva āsayānusayacariyādhimuttiādibhaidatau kusalākusalādivibhāgatau vaṭṭappamāṇasaṇṭhānādibhaidatau, visaisatau vā pana ‘‘ayaṃ sassatāsayau, ayaṃ ucchaidāsayau’’tiādinā ‘‘kakkhaḷalakkhaṇā pathavīdhātu, paggharaṇalakkhaṇā āpaudhātū’’tiādinā ca abhivisiṭṭhaina sayambhuñāṇaina sammā aviparītaṃ yau yau atthau yathā yathā bujjhitabbau, tathā tathā buddhau ñātau attapac‍cakkhau katauti abhisambuddhau|

laukasmāti yathāvuttalaukatau| visaṃyuttauti visaṃsaṭṭhau, tappaṭibaddhānaṃ sabbaisaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ sammadaiva samucchin‍nattā tatau vippamuttauti atthau| laukasamudayauti suttantanayaina taṇhā, abhidhammanayaina  pana abhisaṅkhāraihi saddhiṃ diyaḍḍhakilaisasahassaṃ| pahīnauti baudhimaṇḍai arahattamaggañāṇaina samucchaidappahānavasaina savāsanaṃ pahīnau| laukaniraudhauti nibbānaṃ| sacchikatauti attapac‍cakkhau katau| laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti sīlādikkhandhattayasaṅgahau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| sau hi laukaniraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ gacchati adhigacchati, tadatthaṃ ariyaihi paṭipaj‍jīyati cāti laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti vuc‍cati|

aittāvatā tathāni abhisambuddhau yāthāvatau gatauti tathāgatauti ayamatthau dassitau hauti| cattāri hi ariyasac‍cāni tathāni nāma| yathāha –

‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| katamāni cattāri? idaṃ dukkhanti, bhikkhavai, tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ, anañ‍ñathamaita’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1090) vitthārau|

apica tathāya gatauti tathāgatau, tathaṃ gatauti tathāgatau, gatauti ca avagatau atītau pattau paṭipan‍nauti atthau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yasmā bhagavā sakalalaukaṃ tīraṇapariñ‍ñāya tathāya aviparītāya gatau avagatau, tasmā laukau tathāgataina abhisambuddhauti tathāgatau| laukasamudayaṃ pahānapariñ‍ñāya tathāya gatau atītauti tathāgatau| laukaniraudhaṃ sacchikiriyāya tathāya gatau pattauti tathāgatau| laukaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ tathaṃ aviparītaṃ gatau paṭipan‍nauti tathāgatauti| aivaṃ imissā pāḷiyā bhagavatau tathāgatabhāvadīpanavasaina atthau vaiditabbau|

iti bhagavā catusac‍cābhisambaudhanavasaina attanau tathāgatabhāvaṃ pakāsaitvā idāni tattha diṭṭhādiabhisambaudhivasainapi taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai’’tiādimāha| aṅguttaraṭṭhakathāyaṃ (a॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.4.23) pana ‘‘catūhi sac‍caihi attanau buddhabhāvaṃ kathaitvā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taṃ tathāgatasadda-buddhasaddānaṃ atthatau nin‍nānākaraṇataṃ dassaituṃ vuttaṃ| tathā caiva hi pāḷi pavattāti| tattha diṭṭhanti rūpāyatanaṃ| sutanti saddāyatanaṃ| mutanti patvā gahaitabbatau gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanañ‍ca| viñ‍ñātanti sukhadukkhādidhammārammaṇaṃ| pattanti pariyaisitvā vā apariyaisitvā vā pattaṃ| pariyaisitanti pattaṃ vā appattaṃ vā pariyaisitaṃ| anuvicaritaṃ manasāti cittaina anusañ‍caritaṃ| kassa pana anuvicaritaṃ manasāti? sadaivakassa…pai॰… sadaivamanussāyāti sambandhanīyaṃ| tattha saha daivaihīti sadaivakau, tassa sadaivakassa| saisapadaisupi aisaiva nayau|

sadaivakavacanaina  caittha pañ‍cakāmāvacaradaivaggahaṇaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, samārakavacanaina chaṭṭhakāmāvacaradaivaggahaṇaṃ, sabrahmakavacanaina brahmakāyikādibrahmaggahaṇaṃ, sassamaṇabrāhmaṇivacanaina sāsanassa pac‍catthikasamaṇabrāhmaṇaggahaṇañ‍caiva samitapāpabāhitapāpasamaṇabrāhmaṇaggahaṇañ‍ca, pajāvacanaina sattalaukaggahaṇaṃ, sadaivamanussavacanaina sammutidaivaavasaisamanussaggahaṇaṃ| aivamaittha tīhi padaihi daivamārabrahmaihi saddhiṃ sattalaukau, dvīhi pajāvasaina sattalaukau gahitauti vaiditabbau|

aparau  nayau – sadaivakaggahaṇaina arūpāvacaradaivalaukau gahitau, samārakavacanaina chakāmāvacaradaivalaukau, sabrahmakavacanaina rūpībrahmalaukau, sassamaṇabrāhmaṇādivacanaina sammutidaivaihi saha avasaisasattalaukau gahitau| apicaittha sadaivakavacanaina uk‍kaṭṭhaparicchaidatau sabbalaukavisayassa bhagavatau abhisambuddhabhāvai pakāsitai yaisamaivaṃ siyā ‘‘mārau nāma mahānubhāvau chakāmāvacarissarau vasavattī, brahmā pana tataupi mahānubhāvatarau dasahi aṅgulīhi dasasu cak‍kavāḷasahassaisu ālaukaṃ pharati, uttamajjhānasamāpattisukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| puthū ca samaṇabrāhmaṇā iddhimantau dibbacakkhukā paracittavidunau mahānubhāvā saṃvij‍janti| ayañ‍ca sattakāyau anantau aparimāṇau, kimaitaisaṃ sabbaisaṃyaiva visayau anavasaisatau bhagavatā abhisambuddhau’’ti? taisaṃ vimatiṃ vidhamaintau bhagavā ‘‘sadaivakassa laukassā’’tiādimāha|

paurāṇā panāhu – ‘‘sadaivakassā’’ti daivatāhi saddhiṃ avasaisalaukaṃ pariyādiyati, ‘‘samārakassā’’ti māraina saddhiṃ avasaisalaukaṃ, ‘‘sabrahmakassā’’ti brahmaihi saddhiṃ avasaisalaukaṃ| aivaṃ sabbaipi tibhavūpagai sattai tīsu padaisu pakkhipitvā puna dvīhi padaihi pariyādiyantau ‘‘sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāyā’’ti āha| aivaṃ pañ‍cahipi padaihi khandhattayaparicchin‍nai sabbasattai pariyādiyati|

yasmā taṃ tathāgataina abhisambuddhanti iminā idaṃ dassaiti – yaṃ aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu imassa sadaivakassa laukassa ‘‘nīlaṃ pītaka’’ntiādi rūpārammaṇaṃ cakkhudvārai āpāthaṃ āgacchati, taṃ sabbaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ sattau imasmiṃ khaṇai imaṃ nāma rūpārammaṇaṃ disvā sumanau vā dummanau vā majjhattau vā jātau’’ti tathāgatassa aivaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ| tathā yaṃ aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu imassa sadaivakassa laukassa ‘‘bhairisaddau mudiṅgasaddau’’tiādi saddārammaṇaṃ sautadvārai āpāthaṃ āgacchati, ‘‘mūlagandhau tacagandhau’’tiādi gandhārammaṇaṃ ghānadvārai āpāthamāgacchati, ‘‘mūlarasau khandharasau’’tiādi rasārammaṇaṃ jivhādvārai āpāthamāgacchati, ‘‘kakkhaḷaṃ muduka’’ntiādi pathavīdhātutaijaudhātuvāyaudhātubhaidaṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ kāyadvārai āpāthamāgacchati, ‘‘ayaṃ sattau imasmiṃ khaṇai imaṃ nāma phauṭṭhabbaṃ  phusitvā sumanau vā dummanau vā majjhattau vā jātau’’ti sabbaṃ taṃ tathāgatassa aivaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ|

tathā yaṃ aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu imassa sadaivakassa laukassa sukhādibhaidaṃ dhammārammaṇaṃ manaudvārai āpāthamāgacchati, ‘‘ayaṃ sattau imasmiṃ khaṇai imaṃ nāma dhammārammaṇaṃ jānitvā sumanau vā dummanau vā majjhattau vā jātau’’ti sabbaṃ taṃ tathāgatassa aivaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ| aivaṃ yaṃ imassa sadaivakassa laukassa diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ, taṃ tathāgataina adiṭṭhaṃ vā asutaṃ vā amutaṃ vā aviñ‍ñātaṃ vā natthi| imassa pana mahājanassa pariyaisitvā appattampi atthi, apariyaisitvā appattampi atthi, pariyaisitvā pattampi atthi, apariyaisitvā pattampi atthi| sabbampi tathāgatassa appattaṃ nāma natthi ñāṇaina asacchikataṃ| tatau aiva yaṃ aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ cakkhudvārai āpāthamāgacchantaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ nāma atthi, taṃ bhagavā sabbaṃ sabbākāraina jānāti passati| aivaṃ jānatā passatā cānaina taṃ iṭṭhāniṭṭhādivasaina vā diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātaisu labbhamānapadavasaina vā ‘‘katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītaka’’ntiādinā (dha॰ sa॰ 617) nayaina anaikaihi nāmaihi tairasahi vāraihi dvaipañ‍ñāsāya nayaihi vibhaj‍jamānaṃ tathaiva hauti, vitathaṃ natthi| aisa nayau sautadvārādīsupi āpāthamāgacchantaisu saddādīsu|

tasmā tathāgatauti vuc‍catīti yaṃ yathā laukaina gataṃ, tassa tathaiva gatattā tathāgatauti vuc‍cati| pāḷiyaṃ pana ‘‘abhisambuddha’’nti vuttaṃ, taṃ tathāgatasaddaina samānatthaṃ| iminā tathādassibhāvatau tathāgatauti ayamatthau dassitau hauti| vuttañhaitaṃ dhammasaināpatinā –

‘‘na tassa addiṭṭhamidhatthi kiñ‍ci,

athau aviñ‍ñātamajānitabbaṃ|

sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñāsi yadatthi naiyyaṃ,

tathāgatau taina samantacakkhū’’ti|| (mahāni॰ 156; cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 85; paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.121)|

suttantaipi  vuttaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya pajāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ  viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tamahaṃ jānāmi, tamahaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, taṃ tathāgatassa viditaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau na upaṭṭhāsī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.24)|

yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhatīti yassañ‍ca visākhapuṇṇamarattiyaṃ tathā āgatādiatthaina tathāgatau bhagavā baudhimaṇḍai aparājitapal‍laṅkai nisin‍nau tiṇṇaṃ mārānaṃ matthakaṃ madditvā uttaritarābhāvatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ āsavakkhayañāṇaina saddhiṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ adhigacchati| yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyatīti yassañ‍ca visākhapuṇṇamarattiyaṃyaiva kusinārāyaṃ upavattanai mal‍lānaṃ sālavanai yamakasālānamantarai anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati| yaṃ aitasmiṃ antaraiti imāsaṃ dvin‍naṃ saupādisaisaanupādisaisanibbānadhātūnaṃ vaimajjhai pañ‍cacattālīsavassaparimāṇai kālai paṭhamabaudhiyampi, majjhimabaudhiyampi, pacchimabaudhiyampi yaṃ suttagaiyyādippabhaidaṃ dhammaṃ bhāsati niddisanavasaina, lapati uddhisanavasaina, niddisati paṭiniddisanavasaina| sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hautīti taṃ aitthantarai daisitaṃ sabbaṃ suttagaiyyādinavaṅgaṃ buddhavacanaṃ atthatau byañ‍janatau ca anupavaj‍jaṃ anūnaṃ anadhikaṃ sabbākāraparipuṇṇaṃ rāgamadanimmadanaṃ…pai॰… mauhamadanimmadanaṃ, natthi tattha vālaggamattampi avakkhalitaṃ, aikamuddikāya lañchitaṃ viya aikanāḷiyā mitaṃ viya aikatulāya tulitaṃ viya ca taṃ tathaiva hauti yassatthāya bhāsitaṃ, aikantainaiva tassa sādhanatau, nau añ‍ñathā| tasmā tathaṃ, avitathaṃ, anañ‍ñathaṃ| aitaina tathāvāditāya tathāgatauti dassaiti| gadaatthau ayaṃ gatasaddau dakārassa takāraṃ katvā, tasmā tathaṃ gadatīti tathāgatauti atthau| atha vā āgadanaṃ āgadau, vacananti atthau| tathau aviparītau āgadau yassāti dakārassa takāraṃ katvā tathāgatauti aivamaittha padasiddhi vaiditabbā|

yathāvādī tathākārīti yai dhammai bhagavā ‘‘imai dhammā akusalā sāvaj‍jā viñ‍ñugarahitā samattā samādin‍nā  ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattantī’’ti  paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintau vadati, tai dhammai aikantainaiva sayaṃ pahāsi| yai pana dhammai bhagavā ‘‘imai dhammā kusalā anavaj‍jā viñ‍ñuppasatthā samattā samādin‍nā hitāya sukhāya saṃvattantī’’ti vadati, tai dhammai aikantainaiva sayaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| tasmā yathāvādī bhagavā, tathākārīti vaiditabbau| yathākārī tathāvādīti sammadaiva sīlādiparipūraṇavasaina sammā paṭipan‍nau sayaṃ yathākārī bhagavā, tathaiva dhammadaisanāya paraisaṃ tattha patiṭṭhāpanavasaina  tathāvādī| bhagavatau hi vācāya kāyau anulaumaiti, kāyassapi vācā| tasmā yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī ca hauti| aivaṃbhūtassa cassa yathā vācā, kāyaupi tathā gatau pavattau| yathā ca kāyau, vācāpi tathā gatā pavattāti atthau|

abhibhū anabhibhūtauti upari bhavaggaṃ haiṭṭhā avīcinirayaṃ pariyantaṃ katvā tiriyaṃ aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu bhagavā sabbasattai abhibhavati sīlainapi samādhināpi pañ‍ñāyapi vimuttiyāpi vimuttiñāṇadassanainapi, na tassa tulā vā pamāṇaṃ vā atthi, asamau asamasamau appaṭimau appaṭibhāgau appaṭipuggalau atulau appamaiyyau anuttarau dhammarājā daivānaṃ atidaivau sak‍kānaṃ atisak‍kau brahmānaṃ atibrahmā| tatau aiva sayaṃ na kainaci abhibhūtauti anabhibhūtau| añ‍ñadatthūti aikaṃsatthai nipātau| yañhi kiñ‍ci naiyyaṃ nāma, sabbaṃ taṃ hatthatalai āmalakaṃ viya passatīti dasau| aviparītaṃ āsayādiavabaudhaina hitūpasaṃhārādinā ca sattai, bhāvañ‍ñathattūpanayavasaina saṅkhārai sabbākāraina suciṇṇavasitāya samāpattiyau cittañ‍ca vasai vattaitīti vasavattī| aittāvatā abhibhavanaṭṭhaina bhagavā attanau tathāgatabhāvaṃ dassaiti|

tatraivaṃ padasiddhi vaiditabbā – agadau viya agadau| kau panaisa? daisanāvilāsau caiva puñ‍ñussayau ca| tainaiva haisa mahānubhāvau bhisak‍kau viya dibbāgadaina sappai, sabbai parappavādinau sadaivakañ‍ca laukaṃ abhibhavati| iti sabbalaukābhibhavanai tathau aviparītau daisanāvilāsau caiva puñ‍ñussayau ca agadau assāti dakārassa takāraṃ katvā  tathāgatauti vaiditabbau| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sadaivakai, bhikkhavai, laukai…pai॰… vasavattī, tasmā tathāgatauti vuc‍catī’’ti|

gāthāsu  sabbalaukaṃ abhiñ‍ñāyāti taidhātukalaukasan‍nivāsaṃ jānitvā| sabbalaukai yathātathanti tasmiṃ taidhātukalaukasan‍nivāsai yaṃkiñ‍ci naiyyaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ yathātathaṃ aviparītaṃ jānitvā| sabbalaukavisaṃyuttauti catun‍naṃ yaugānaṃ anavasaisappahānaina sabbainapi laukaina visaṃyuttau vippamuttau| anūpayauti sabbasmimpi laukai taṇhādiṭṭhiupayaihi anūpayau taihi upayaihi virahitau|

sabbābhibhūti rūpādīni sabbārammaṇāni, sabbaṃ saṅkhāragataṃ, sabbaipi mārai abhibhavitvā ṭhitau| dhīrauti dhitisampan‍nau| sabbaganthappamaucanauti sabbai abhijjhākāyaganthādikai maucaitvā ṭhitau  vainaiyyasantānaipi attanau daisanāvilāsaina taisaṃ pamaucanatau sabbaganthappamaucanau| phuṭṭhāssāti phuṭṭhā assa| karaṇatthai idaṃ sāmivacanaṃ, phuṭṭhā anaināti atthau| paramā santīti nibbānaṃ| tañhi taina ñāṇaphusanaina phuṭṭhaṃ| tainaivāha ‘‘nibbānaṃ akutaubhaya’’nti| atha vā paramā santīti uttamā santi| katarā sāti? nibbānaṃ| yasmā pana nibbānai kutauci bhayaṃ natthi, tasmā taṃ akutaubhayanti vuc‍cati|

anīghauti niddukkhau| sabbakammakkhayaṃ pattauti sabbaisaṃ kammānaṃ khayaṃ pariyausānaṃ ac‍cantābhāvaṃ pattau| vimuttau upadhisaṅkhayaiti upadhisaṅkhayasaṅkhātai nibbānai tadārammaṇāya phalavimuttiyā vimuttau| aisa sauti aisau sau| sīhau anuttarauti parissayānaṃ sahanaṭṭhaina, kilaisānaṃ hananaṭṭhaina ca, tathāgatau anuttarau sīhau nāma| brahmanti saiṭṭhaṃ| cak‍kanti dhammacak‍kaṃ| pavattayīti tiparivaṭṭaṃ dvādasākāraṃ pavattaisi|

itīti  aivaṃ tathāgatassa guṇai jānitvā| saṅgammāti  samāgantvā| taṃ namassantīti taṃ tathāgataṃ tai saraṇaṃ gatā daivamanussā namassanti| mahantaihi sīlādiguṇaihi saman‍nāgatattā mahantaṃ, catuvaisāraj‍jayaugaina vītasāradaṃ| idāni yaṃ vadantā tai namassanti, taṃ dassaituṃ dantautiādi vuttaṃ| taṃ uttānatthamaiva|

iti imasmiṃ catuk‍kanipātai chaṭṭhai sattamai ca suttai vaṭṭaṃ kathitaṃ, paṭhamadutiyatatiyadvādasamatairasamaisu vivaṭṭaṃ kathitaṃ, saisaisu vaṭṭavivaṭṭaṃ kathitanti vaiditabbaṃ|

tairasamasuttavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

iti paramatthadīpaniyā

khuddakanikāya-aṭṭhakathāya

itivuttakassa catuk‍kanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

nigamanakathā

aittāvatā  ca –

dhammissaraina jagatau, dhammālaukavidhāyinā|

dhammānaṃ baudhanaiyyānaṃ, jānatā daisanāvidhiṃ||

taṃ taṃ nidānamāgamma, sabbalaukahitaisinā|

aikakādippabhaidaina, daisitāni mahaisinā||

dasuttarasataṃ dvai ca, suttāni itivuttakaṃ|

itivuttappabhaidaina, saṅgāyiṃsu mahaisayau||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā vasippattā, pabhin‍napaṭisambhidā|

yaṃ taṃ sāsanadhaurayhā, dhammasaṅgāhakā purai||

tassa atthaṃ pakāsaituṃ, paurāṇaṭṭhakathānayaṃ|

nissāya yā samāraddhā, atthasaṃvaṇṇanā mayā||

sā tattha paramatthānaṃ, suttantaisu yathārahaṃ|

pakāsanā paramattha-dīpanī nāma nāmatau||

sampattā pariniṭṭhānaṃ, anākulavinicchayā|

aṭṭhattiṃsappamāṇāya, pāḷiyā bhāṇavāratau||

iti taṃ saṅkharauntaina, yaṃ taṃ adhigataṃ mayā|

puñ‍ñaṃ tassānubhāvaina, laukanāthassa sāsanaṃ||

augāhaitvā visuddhāya, sīlādipaṭipattiyā|

sabbaipi pāṇinau hauntu, vimuttirasabhāginau||

ciraṃ  tiṭṭhatu laukasmiṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanaṃ|

tasmiṃ sagāravā nic‍caṃ, hauntu sabbaipi pāṇinau||

sammā vassatu kālaina, daivaupi jagatippati|

saddhammaniratau laukaṃ, dhammainaiva pasāsatūti||

iti badaratitthavihāravāsinā ācariyadhammapālaina katā

itivuttakassa aṭṭhakathā niṭṭhitā|
